📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 麻疹 传播 风险在 2025-2026 年 麻疹 之前 2-3 年在学校层面突破了流行病阈值,而县级估计值始终低于该阈值。 [10.1038/s41591-026-04612-2]。
  • 麻疹病毒 传播 的潜力在学校中已经变得非常关键,但在当前 监测 系统监控的规模上仍然不可见。它还强调,大流行加剧了 疫苗 的犹豫,使平均敏感性增加了一倍,从大约 5% 增加到 10%。
  • 纯政策组和对照组之间的 MCV1 覆盖率相似(调整后的风险差异 [ARD] = -1%、95%CI:-17%、15%),但在 5 剂转换组中,9 个月时的及时 MCV1(ARD = 18%、95%CI:7%、28%)和 MCV2 覆盖率(ARD = 17%、95%CI:1%、34%)显着上升,并且浪费下降(ARD = -7%,95%CI:-14%,-1%)。
  • 该研究报告称,2024 年全国 疫苗接种 覆盖率达到 75.3%,比 2022 年的 65.3% 提高了 10 个百分点。显着的地理差异仍然存在 (p = 0.0233),大城市 (63.2%) 的覆盖率低于内陆地区 (78.4%) 和中等地区 (76.2%)。居住在大城市显着降低覆盖率(OR 调整 = 0.25;p = 0.010),而男孩性别(OR 调整 = 1.33;p = 0.040)、在医疗机构出生(OR 调整 = 1.38;p = 0.046)和拥有出生证明(OR 调整 = 2.30;p < 0.001)与较高的覆盖率相关。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Measles transmission risk crossed the epidemic threshold at the school level 2–3 years before the 2025–2026 outbreaks, while county-level estimates consistently remained below that threshold. [10.1038/s41591-026-04612-2].
  • The review concludes that measles virus transmission potential has become supercritical in schools but remains invisible at the scale monitored by current surveillance systems.
  • MCV1 coverage was similar between policy-only and control (adjusted risk difference [ARD] = -1%, 95%CI: -17%, 15%), but in the 5-dose switch group, timely MCV1 at 9 months (ARD = 18%, 95%CI: 7%, 28%) and MCV2 coverage (ARD = 17%, 95%CI: 1%, 34%) rose significantly, and wastage fell (ARD = -7%, 95%CI: -14%, -1%).
  • The study reported a national post-campaign vaccination coverage of 75.3% in 2024, a 10-point increase from 65.3% in 2022.

  • The Iowa Department of Health and Human Services (Iowa HHS) confirmed a measles outbreak in Iowa with 12 cases and evidence of person-to-person spread. This is a confirmed event reported by the Iowa HHS.
  • The Virginia Department of Health (VDH) is promoting immunization awareness, including measles vaccine recommendations, and ensuring access to childhood vaccinations.
  • WHO, in collaboration with the Kyrgyzstan Ministry of Health and the International Federation of Red Cross and Red Crescent Societies, conducted an assessment of the measles outbreak response in Kyrgyzstan.
  • Penn State Health notified the public of a potential measles exposure at Golisano Children’s Hospital. Reported by Penn State Health News. [news-299346d73d6c8ff2].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Frontiers in Immunology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-18  |  DOI: 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1919103  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 17
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

疫苗 诱导的 麻疹 抗体的减弱和后消除时代的群体免疫缺口:中国多中心血清流行病学研究

Waning of vaccine-induced measles antibodies and population immunity gaps in the post-elimination era: a multicenter seroepidemiological study in China
作者： Wen Wang, Zhisheng Chen, Hanwen Zhang, Jinting Xie, Yuanyuan Zhu, Guzel Nurpulat, Xiang Sun
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管含有 麻疹 (MCV) 的 麻疹 覆盖率持续较高,但消除后维持人群免疫力仍然具有挑战性,因此需要表征 麻疹 抗体的持久性并确定中国的免疫差距。
设计与对象
2024年至2025年,对来自江苏省和新疆维吾尔自治区的2,083名参与者进行了多中心横断面血清学调查。
核心方法
使用 ELISA 测量 麻疹 特异性 IgG 抗体,血清阳性定义为 IgG ≥200 mIU/mL。限制三次样条模型在排除没有记录 疫苗接种 的个体、疫苗接种 后一个月内采样的个体以及潜在的主要 疫苗 失败的个体后评估抗体动力学。多变量逻辑回归和倾向评分匹配评估了与血清阴性相关的因素。
主要结果
Overall measles IgG seropositivity was 84.59% (95% CI: 82.98–86.08%), with a GMT of 482.48 mIU/mL. Seropositivity varied significantly by region, ethnicity, age, and vaccination history. Antibody levels showed a non-linear decline with increasing time since vaccination, and each additional year since the last vaccination increased seronegativity risk (AOR = 1.11, 95% CI: 1.07–1.15). Children aged 8–17 months had the lowest seropositivity (28.13%).
解释与创新
作者解释说,高 MCV 覆盖率并不能确保后消除时代持续的群体免疫力,将残余易感性归因于体液免疫减弱、疫苗接种 延迟和异质 疫苗接种 历史。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持基于免疫力的 麻疹 和有针对性的干预措施,以解决免疫力减弱和 疫苗接种 延迟的问题,特别是在幼儿中,以防止 麻疹 在消除环境中卷土重来。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且来自未测量因素(例如先前感染或 疫苗 批次变异性)的残余混杂可能会影响血清阴性估计,需要谨慎解释。

Waning of vaccine-induced measles antibodies and population immunity gaps in the post-elimination era: a multicenter seroepidemiological study in China

Authors: Wen Wang, Zhisheng Chen, Hanwen Zhang, Jinting Xie, Yuanyuan Zhu, Guzel Nurpulat, Xiang Sun
Original abstract (English)
Background Despite sustained high measles-containing vaccine (MCV) coverage, maintaining population immunity after measles elimination remains challenging. This study aimed to characterize measles antibody persistence, identify immunity gaps, and evaluate factors associated with seronegativity in China. Methods A multicenter cross-sectional serological survey was conducted among 2,083 participants from Jiangsu Province and Xinjiang Uygur Autonomous Region during 2024–2025. Measles-specific IgG antibodies were measured using ELISA, with seropositivity defined as IgG ≥200 mIU/mL. Restricted cubic spline models were used to assess antibody kinetics after excluding individuals without documented vaccination, those sampled within one month after vaccination, and potential primary vaccine failures. Multivariable logistic regression and propensity score matching were performed to evaluate factors associated with seronegativity. Results Overall measles IgG seropositivity was 84.59% (95% CI: 82.98–86.08%), with a GMT of 482.48 mIU/mL. Seropositivity varied significantly by region, ethnicity, age, and vaccination history. Antibody levels showed a non-linear decline with increasing time since vaccination, and each additional year since the last vaccination increased seronegativity risk (AOR = 1.11, 95% CI: 1.07–1.15). Children aged 8–17 months had the lowest seropositivity (28.13%), indicating a potential susceptibility window. Although migrants had higher seropositivity than permanent residents (93.04% vs. 83.90%), migration status was not independently associated with seronegativity after adjustment. Conclusions High MCV coverage does not necessarily ensure sustained population immunity in the post-elimination era. Waning humoral immunity, delayed vaccination, and heterogeneous vaccination histories contribute to residual susceptibility, highlighting the need for immunity-based surveillance and targeted interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite sustained high measles-containing vaccine (MCV) coverage, maintaining population immunity after elimination remains challenging, creating a need to characterize measles antibody persistence and identify immunity gaps in China.
Design and population
A multicenter cross-sectional serological survey was conducted among 2,083 participants from Jiangsu Province and Xinjiang Uygur Autonomous Region during 2024–2025.
Core methods
Measles-specific IgG antibodies were measured using ELISA with seropositivity defined as IgG ≥200 mIU/mL. Restricted cubic spline models assessed antibody kinetics after excluding individuals without documented vaccination, those sampled within one month after vaccination, and potential primary vaccine failures. Multivariable logistic regression and propensity score matching evaluated factors associated with seronegativity.
Main results
Overall measles IgG seropositivity was 84.59% (95% CI: 82.98–86.08%), with a GMT of 482.48 mIU/mL. Seropositivity varied significantly by region, ethnicity, age, and vaccination history. Antibody levels showed a non-linear decline with increasing time since vaccination, and each additional year since the last vaccination increased seronegativity risk (AOR = 1.11, 95% CI: 1.07–1.15). Children aged 8–17 months had the lowest seropositivity (28.13%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that high MCV coverage does not ensure sustained population immunity in the post-elimination era, attributing residual susceptibility to waning humoral immunity, delayed vaccination, and heterogeneous vaccination histories.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support immunity-based surveillance and targeted interventions to address waning immunity and delayed vaccination, particularly in young children, to prevent measles resurgence in elimination settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and residual confounding from unmeasured factors such as prior infection or vaccine batch variability may influence seronegativity estimates, warranting cautious interpretation.

Journal: Australian and New Zealand journal of public health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-30  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.anzjph.2026.100577  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 100577

在持续消除 麻疹 后,估计澳大利亚目前对 麻疹 的敏感性。

Estimating current susceptibility to measles in Australia following sustained measles elimination.
作者： Zoë Croker, Angus McLure, Stephen Lambert, Helen Quinn, Alexis Pillsbury, Kristine Macartney, Peter McIntyre, Frank Beard
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了澳大利亚人口对 麻疹 免疫力减弱的公共卫生问题,尽管 麻疹 的覆盖率持续较高,但在消除数十年后,该研究发现了有关特定年龄易感性的知识差距。
设计与对象
这项国家生态研究将 1920-1999 年出生的 队列研究 的 血清流行率 调查与 2000-2018 年出生的 队列研究 的澳大利亚免疫登记覆盖率数据结合起来,以估计 2019 年特定年龄的 麻疹 易感性。
核心方法
1920-1960年的易感性是根据血清调查中的感染获得性免疫推断的,1961-1999年的易感性是根据感染和疫苗获得性免疫的组合推断的,2000-2018年的易感性是根据疫苗保护每年0·04%的衰减调整后的一剂或两剂覆盖率推断的。
主要结果
Susceptibility rose from <1% in those born 1920–1960 to 11% in the 1993–1995 cohort, ranged from 7–14% for those born 2000–2015, and an estimated 1.7 million Australians (6·7%) were susceptible in 2019 (95%UI: 1·3–3·2 million).
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为显示澳大利亚的整体免疫力处于世界卫生组织群体保护阈值的下限(93-95%),他们强调了将所有年龄段的血清调查和免疫登记数据结合起来的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些估计提供了基线数据,以指导 疫苗接种 针对免疫力差距的活动,特别是针对老年群体和可能出国旅行的群体。
局限与证据强度
该研究的生态设计和对基于登记的覆盖范围的依赖以及假设的减弱率限制了因果推断,总敏感性周围的不确定性区间表明证据强度适中。

Estimating current susceptibility to measles in Australia following sustained measles elimination.

Authors: Zoë Croker, Angus McLure, Stephen Lambert, Helen Quinn, Alexis Pillsbury, Kristine Macartney, Peter McIntyre, Frank Beard
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: This study estimates Australia's measles susceptibility across all age groups. To our knowledge, this study is the first internationally to combine multiple consecutive serosurvey and population immunisation register data. METHODS: We estimated age-specific measles susceptibility in 2019 using data from nationally representative seroprevalence surveys for people born between 1920 and 1960 (infection-acquired immunity) and those born 1961-1999 (infection/vaccine-acquired immunity). For people born 2000-2018 (vaccine-acquired immunity only), we estimated measles susceptibility from coverage of one or two doses of measles-containing vaccine from the Australian Immunisation Register and presumed vaccine effectiveness, adjusting for 0·04% waning of protection per year. RESULTS: Susceptibility increased from less than 1% across those born 1920-1960 to 11% for the 1993-1995 cohort, and ranged from 7-14% for those born 2000-2015. We estimated that 1.7 million Australians (6·7%) were susceptible to measles in 2019 (95%UI: 1·3-3·2 million). Among notifications of measles infection between 2008 and 2022, people aged 15-35 years at time of notification accounted for 50%. CONCLUSIONS: Despite sustained high two-dose measles vaccine coverage, Australia's overall population-level measles immunity is at the lower end of the World Health Organization estimated herd protection threshold range (93-95%). IMPLICATIONS FOR PUBLIC HEALTH: Our estimates provide baseline data to inform vaccination activities to address immunity gaps in older age groups, particularly those likely to travel internationally.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem of waning population immunity to measles in Australia despite sustained high vaccine coverage, identifying a knowledge gap about age-specific susceptibility after decades of elimination.
Design and population
This national ecological study combined seroprevalence surveys for cohorts born 1920–1999 with Australian Immunisation Register coverage data for cohorts born 2000–2018 to estimate age-specific measles susceptibility in 2019.
Core methods
Susceptibility for 1920–1960 was inferred from infection-acquired immunity in serosurveys, for 1961–1999 from combined infection- and vaccine-acquired immunity, and for 2000–2018 from one- or two-dose coverage adjusted for 0·04% annual waning of vaccine protection.
Main results
Susceptibility rose from <1% in those born 1920–1960 to 11% in the 1993–1995 cohort, ranged from 7–14% for those born 2000–2015, and an estimated 1.7 million Australians (6·7%) were susceptible in 2019 (95%UI: 1·3–3·2 million).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as showing Australia’s overall immunity at the lower end of the WHO herd-protection threshold (93–95%), and they highlight the novelty of combining serosurvey and immunisation register data across all age groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The estimates provide baseline data to guide vaccination activities targeting immunity gaps, particularly in older age groups and those likely to travel internationally.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s ecological design and reliance on register-based coverage with assumed waning rates limit causal inference, and the uncertainty interval around total susceptibility indicates moderate evidence strength.

Journal: Vaccine: X  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-01  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.jvacx.2026.100892  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 32, 100892

麻疹 疫苗接种 在埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚地区选定地区的及时性 – 2021

Timeliness of measles vaccination in selected districts of Oromia Region, Ethiopia – 2021
作者： Monica P. Shah, Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Almea M. Matanock, Binyam Moges Azmeraye, Leuel Lisanwork, Getnet Yimer, Shu-Hua Wang, Xueliang Pan, Paulos Samuel, Bekana Tolera
摘要中文翻译背景 延迟 麻疹 与含 麻疹 的 疫苗(MCV1、MCV2)的首剂/次剂量会增加 麻疹 传播 的风险。MCV2 于 2019 年被添加到埃塞俄比亚的常规免疫计划中,在 15 个月大时接种。我们描述了 麻疹 疫苗接种 的年龄以及与 MCV2 引入后及时 疫苗接种 相关的特征。方法 对 2021 年埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚州 12-35 个月龄儿童进行的横断面家庭调查进行了二次分析。MCV 疫苗接种 状态和 疫苗接种 年龄是根据孩子的 疫苗接种 卡确定的。估计了按 疫苗接种 年龄划分的 MCV1 和 MCV2(24-35 个月儿童)的累积卡确认 疫苗接种 覆盖率。在接受 MCV 的儿童中,疫苗接种 时间分为早期(MCV1:<9 个月;MCV2:<15 个月)、及时(MCV1:9 至 <10 个月;MCV2:15 至 <16 个月)或延迟(MCV1:≥10 个月;MCV2:≥16 个月)。使用多元逻辑回归和城市/农村分层分析与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征。结果 总体而言,对 651 名 12-35 个月的儿童 (MCV1) 和 270 名 24-35 个月的儿童 (MCV2) 进行了分析。9 个月时的累积 MCV1 覆盖率为 59.9%,12 个月时为 76.3%,15 个月时的 MCV2 累积覆盖率为 33.0%,24 个月时为 44.8%。与农村地区相比,城市地区的 MCV 覆盖率普遍较高。接种儿童中,MCV1 早期接种、及时接种和延迟接种比例分别为 30.1%、44.5% 和 25.3%,MCV2 接种比例分别为 28.6%、41.2% 和 30.3%。与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征在城市和农村地区有所不同,但共同主题包括护理人员免疫意识和服务提供方面的提高。结论 虽然大多数接受 MCV1 的儿童在 12 个月大时就接种了疫苗,但许多人接受了延迟剂量。无论 疫苗接种 时间如何,MCV2 覆盖率都很低。研究结果强调了常规追赶疫苗接种政策和指导的重要性,以及在城乡环境中制定量身定制的策略以提高MCV覆盖率和及时疫苗接种的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
延迟麻疹首次剂量含有麻疹的疫苗(MCV1,MCV2)会增加麻疹 传播的风险。我们描述了 麻疹 疫苗接种 的年龄以及与 MCV2 引入后及时 疫苗接种 相关的特征。
设计与对象
对 2021 年埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚州 12-35 个月大儿童进行的横断面家庭调查进行了二次分析。总体而言,对 651 名 12-35 个月的儿童 (MCV1) 和 270 名 24-35 个月的儿童 (MCV2) 进行了分析。
核心方法
MCV 疫苗接种 状态和 疫苗接种 年龄是根据孩子的 疫苗接种 卡确定的。使用多元逻辑回归和城市/农村分层分析与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征。
主要结果
9 个月时的累积 MCV1 覆盖率为 59.9%,12 个月时为 76.3%,15 个月时的 MCV2 累积覆盖率为 33.0%,24 个月时为 44.8%。与农村地区相比,城市地区的 MCV 覆盖率普遍较高。
解释与创新
与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征在城市和农村地区有所不同,但共同主题包括护理人员免疫意识和服务提供方面的提高。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了常规追赶疫苗接种政策和指导的重要性,以及在城乡环境中制定量身定制的策略以提高MCV覆盖率和及时疫苗接种的必要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Timeliness of measles vaccination in selected districts of Oromia Region, Ethiopia – 2021

Authors: Monica P. Shah, Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Almea M. Matanock, Binyam Moges Azmeraye, Leuel Lisanwork, Getnet Yimer, Shu-Hua Wang, Xueliang Pan, Paulos Samuel, Bekana Tolera
Original abstract (English)
Background Delays in vaccination with the first/s dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1, MCV2) increases risk of measles transmission. MCV2 was added to Ethiopia's routine immunization schedule in 2019, administered at age 15 months. We describe age at measles vaccination and characteristics associated with timely vaccination following MCV2 introduction. Methods A secondary analysis from a 2021 cross-sectional household survey of children aged 12–35 months in Oromia, Ethiopia was conducted. MCV vaccination status and age at vaccination were determined from the child's vaccination card. The cumulative card-confirmed vaccination coverage of MCV1 and MCV2 (among children 24–35 months) by age at vaccination was estimated. Among children who received MCV, vaccination timing was classified as early (MCV1: <9 months; MCV2: <15 months), timely (MCV1: 9 to <10 months; MCV2: 15 to <16 months), or delayed (MCV1: ≥10 months; MCV2: ≥16 months). Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination were analyzed using multivariate logistic regression, with urban/rural stratification. Results Overall, 651 children aged 12–35 months (MCV1) and a subset of 270 children aged 24–35 months (MCV2) were analyzed. Cumulative MCV1 coverage was 59.9% by age 9 months and 76.3% by age 12 months, and MCV2 was 33.0% by age 15 months and 44.8% by age 24 months. MCV coverage was generally higher in urban compared to rural areas. Among vaccinated children, the proportion receiving early, timely, and delayed doses was 30.1%, 44.5%, and 25.3% for MCV1, and 28.6%, 41.2%, and 30.3% for MCV2, respectively. Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination differed in urban and rural areas, but common themes included increased caregiver immunization awareness and service delivery aspects. Conclusions Although most children who received MCV1 were vaccinated by 12 months, many received delayed doses. MCV2 coverage was low, irrespective of vaccination timing. Findings highlight the importance of routine catch-up vaccination policies and guidance, and the need for tailored strategies in urban and rural settings to improve MCV coverage and timely vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Delays in vaccination with the first dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1, MCV2) increases risk of measles transmission. We describe age at measles vaccination and characteristics associated with timely vaccination following MCV2 introduction.
Design and population
A secondary analysis from a 2021 cross-sectional household survey of children aged 12–35 months in Oromia, Ethiopia was conducted. Overall, 651 children aged 12–35 months (MCV1) and a subset of 270 children aged 24–35 months (MCV2) were analyzed.
Core methods
MCV vaccination status and age at vaccination were determined from the child's vaccination card. Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination were analyzed using multivariate logistic regression, with urban/rural stratification.
Main results
Cumulative MCV1 coverage was 59.9% by age 9 months and 76.3% by age 12 months, and MCV2 was 33.0% by age 15 months and 44.8% by age 24 months. MCV coverage was generally higher in urban compared to rural areas.
Interpretation and novelty
Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination differed in urban and rural areas, but common themes included increased caregiver immunization awareness and service delivery aspects.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings highlight the importance of routine catch-up vaccination policies and guidance, and the need for tailored strategies in urban and rural settings to improve MCV coverage and timely vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

埃塞俄比亚错过了 疫苗接种 研究的机会:2021-2022 年 5 剂 麻疹 疫苗 小瓶的整群随机评估和灵活的开瓶政策

Ethiopia missed opportunities for vaccination study: Cluster-randomized evaluation of 5-dose measles vaccine vials and a flexible open-vial policy, 2021–2022
作者： Aaron S. Wallace, Ciara Sugerman, Bezawit Getachew, Almea Matanock, Mekonnen Admassu, Tesfaye Kassahun, Habtamu Teklie Wubie, Mulat Nigus Alemu, Yohannes Lakew Tefera, Melkamu Ayalew Kokebie
摘要中文翻译简介:2024 年,全球估计有 95,000 人死于 麻疹,主要是由于含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV) 的覆盖范围不理想。卫生工作者可能会推迟为符合条件的儿童接种疫苗,以避免浪费 10 剂 MCV 小瓶中的剂量,这些小瓶必须在打开后六小时内丢弃。疫苗接种 (MOV) 错失的这些机会降低了覆盖范围和及时性。埃塞俄比亚在 9 个月和 15 个月时提供 MCV,考虑改用 5 剂小瓶。方法:我们在埃塞俄比亚进行了一项为期 15 个月的嵌套横断面设计随机对照试验。60 名被随机分配到:(1) 仅限政策,指示卫生工作者为任意数量的符合条件的儿童打开 10 剂小瓶,并增加库存以防止浪费;(2)5剂转换,将本政策与10剂瓶更换为5剂瓶相结合;(3) 对照(常规 10 剂练习)。基线和终点的家庭和卫生机构调查评估了对第一剂 (MCV1) 和第二剂 (MCV2) 覆盖率、MCV1 及时性和浪费的影响。广义估计方程模型估计了净干预效应与控制的关系。我们还估算了全国范围内更换 5 剂疫苗的政府成本。结果:纯政策和对照之间的 MCV1 覆盖率相似(调整后风险差 [ARD] = -1%,95%CI:-17%,15%),MCV1 及时性或 MCV2 覆盖率没有显着差异。在 5 剂转换组中,MCV1 覆盖率变化不大(ARD = 1%、95%CI:-17%、18%),但 9 个月时及时 MCV1(ARD = 18%、95%CI:7%、28%)和 MCV2 覆盖率(ARD = 17%、95%CI:1%、34%)显着上升,且损耗下降(ARD = -7%、 95%CI:-14%,-1%)。截至终点,29% 的 5 剂卫生工作者每月打开药瓶 ≥10 次(无对照);两个干预组的护理人员报告与 麻疹 相关的 MOV 减少了 11%。据估计,全国范围内更换 5 剂疫苗可为每个完全接种疫苗(两剂)的儿童节省 17% 的采购成本。结论:联合 5 剂干预措施提高了 MCV1 的及时性和 MCV2 的覆盖率,并减少了浪费,解决了关键的操作障碍,并可能支持埃塞俄比亚消除 麻疹。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于含有 麻疹 的 麻疹 (MCV) 的覆盖效果不佳,麻疹 仍然是 死亡率 的一个主要原因,卫生工作者可能会推迟为符合条件的儿童接种疫苗,以避免浪费 10 剂 MCV 小瓶在打开后 6 小时丢弃的剂量。
设计与对象
在埃塞俄比亚进行了一项为期 15 个月、采用巢式横断面设计的整群随机对照试验,将 60 名威尔达随机分为三个组:仅政策组、5 剂转换组和对照组。
核心方法
基线和终点的家庭和卫生机构调查评估了第一剂 (MCV1) 和第二剂 (MCV2) 覆盖率、MCV1 及时性和浪费,并使用广义估计方程模型估计净干预效果与对照。
主要结果
纯政策组和对照组之间的 MCV1 覆盖率相似(调整后的风险差异 [ARD] = -1%、95%CI:-17%、15%),但在 5 剂转换组中,9 个月时的及时 MCV1(ARD = 18%、95%CI:7%、28%)和 MCV2 覆盖率(ARD = 17%、95%CI:1%、34%)显着上升,并且浪费下降(ARD = -7%,95%CI:-14%,-1%)。
解释与创新
联合 5 剂干预措施提高了 MCV1 的及时性和 MCV2 的覆盖率,并减少了浪费,解决了埃塞俄比亚消除 麻疹 的关键操作障碍。
科研与公卫意义
据估计,全国范围内更换 5 剂疫苗可节省每个完全疫苗接种(两剂)儿童 17% 的采购成本,支持 麻疹 消除工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有报告超过 15 个月的长期随访,也没有评估间接的人群水平影响,从而限制了更广泛的消除时间表的普遍性。

Ethiopia missed opportunities for vaccination study: Cluster-randomized evaluation of 5-dose measles vaccine vials and a flexible open-vial policy, 2021–2022

Authors: Aaron S. Wallace, Ciara Sugerman, Bezawit Getachew, Almea Matanock, Mekonnen Admassu, Tesfaye Kassahun, Habtamu Teklie Wubie, Mulat Nigus Alemu, Yohannes Lakew Tefera, Melkamu Ayalew Kokebie
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: In 2024, an estimated 95,000 people died from measles globally, largely from suboptimal coverage with the measles-containing vaccine (MCV). Health workers may defer vaccinating eligible children to avoid wasting doses from 10-dose MCV vials, which must be discarded six hours after opening. These missed opportunities for vaccination (MOV) reduce coverage and timeliness. Ethiopia, which provides MCV at 9 and 15 months, considered switching to 5-dose vials. METHODS: We conducted a 15-month, randomized controlled trial with a nested cross-sectional design in Ethiopia. Sixty woredas were randomized to: (1) policy-only, instructing health workers to open 10-dose vials for any number of eligible children, with additional stock for increased wastage; (2) 5-dose switch, combining this policy with replacement of 10-dose by 5-dose vials; or (3) control (routine 10-dose practice). Household and health-facility surveys at baseline and endline assessed effects on first-dose (MCV1) and second-dose (MCV2) coverage, MCV1 timeliness, and wastage. Generalized estimating equation models estimated net intervention effects versus control. We also estimated the government cost of a nationwide 5-dose switch. RESULTS: MCV1 coverage was similar between policy-only and control (adjusted risk difference [ARD] = -1%, 95%CI: -17%, 15%), with no significant differences in MCV1 timeliness or MCV2 coverage. In the 5-dose switch group, MCV1 coverage changed little (ARD = 1%, 95%CI: -17%, 18%), but timely MCV1 at 9 months (ARD = 18%, 95%CI: 7%, 28%) and MCV2 coverage (ARD = 17%, 95%CI: 1%, 34%) rose significantly, and wastage fell (ARD = -7%, 95%CI: -14%, -1%). By endline, 29% of 5-dose health workers opened vials ≥10 times monthly (none in control); caregivers in both intervention groups reported 11% fewer measles-related MOVs. A nationwide 5-dose switch was estimated to save 17% in procurement cost per fully vaccinated (two-dose) child. CONCLUSIONS: The combined 5-dose intervention improved MCV1 timeliness and MCV2 coverage and reduced wastage, addressing a key operational barrier and potentially supporting measles elimination in Ethiopia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a major global cause of mortality due to suboptimal coverage with the measles-containing vaccine (MCV), and health workers may defer vaccinating eligible children to avoid wasting doses from 10-dose MCV vials discarded six hours after opening.
Design and population
A 15-month, cluster-randomized controlled trial with a nested cross-sectional design was conducted in Ethiopia, randomizing 60 woredas to three arms: policy-only, 5-dose switch, and control.
Core methods
Household and health-facility surveys at baseline and endline assessed first-dose (MCV1) and second-dose (MCV2) coverage, MCV1 timeliness, and wastage, with generalized estimating equation models estimating net intervention effects versus control.
Main results
MCV1 coverage was similar between policy-only and control (adjusted risk difference [ARD] = -1%, 95%CI: -17%, 15%), but in the 5-dose switch group, timely MCV1 at 9 months (ARD = 18%, 95%CI: 7%, 28%) and MCV2 coverage (ARD = 17%, 95%CI: 1%, 34%) rose significantly, and wastage fell (ARD = -7%, 95%CI: -14%, -1%).
Interpretation and novelty
The combined 5-dose intervention improved MCV1 timeliness and MCV2 coverage and reduced wastage, addressing a key operational barrier to measles elimination in Ethiopia.
Scientific and public-health significance
A nationwide 5-dose switch was estimated to save 17% in procurement cost per fully vaccinated (two-dose) child, supporting measles elimination efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not report long-term follow-up beyond 15 months or assess indirect population-level effects, limiting generalisability to broader elimination timelines.

2019-2023 年印度综合疾病 监测 计划 (IDSP) 报告的 麻疹 暴发 分析

Analysis of measles outbreaks reported to Integrated Disease Surveillance Programme (IDSP), India, 2019–2023
作者： Gopi Kumbha, Sushma Choudhary, Mohan Kumar Raju, Meera Dhuria, Archana Ramalingam
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是全球儿童 死亡率 的主要原因,预计 2022 年将有 136,000 人死亡,其中主要是五岁以下儿童。尽管在印度,含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV) 初始覆盖率很高,但只有少数儿童接受第二剂,从而使人群容易感染 暴发。
设计与对象
使用综合疾病 监测 计划 (IDSP) 进行了一项横断面研究,对 2019 年至 2023 年印度报告的 麻疹 暴发 进行描述性 流行病学。数据从公开的 IDSP 每周 暴发 报告中提取,并使用 MS Excel 进行分析。
核心方法
对 2019-2023 年 麻疹 暴发 数据进行分析,以评估时间、地点和人员分布。暴发 分为实验室确诊或临床怀疑,并根据发生周评估报告及时性。
主要结果
在报告的 259 例 暴发 中,89% 得到实验室确诊,56.3% 及时报告。病例的中位年龄为 6.5 岁(IQR 5-9.5)。百分之五十二点八的 暴发 发生在四个邦:比哈尔邦 (18.5%)、贾坎德邦 (13.5%)、北方邦 (11.1%) 和古吉拉特邦 (9.6%)。2023 年记录了 暴发 的最大数量(116 例)。在 56% 的 暴发 期间提供了维生素 A 补充剂,据报道有 20 人死亡。
解释与创新
该研究强调,近一半的 暴发 集中在五个州,表明地理集群。大多数报告中的延迟报告和人口统计数据缺失凸显了 监测 质量和完整性方面的严重差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要及时进行 暴发 报告、在 死亡率 报告中纳入年龄和性别数据,并确保在 暴发 期间广泛补充维生素 A,以降低发病率和 死亡率。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于,暴发 报告中分别有 56.4% 和 96% 的报告缺乏年龄和性别数据,这限制了详细的个人层面分析。由于依赖常规 监测 数据,可能存在漏报和不完整的变量,因此证据是中等的。

Analysis of measles outbreaks reported to Integrated Disease Surveillance Programme (IDSP), India, 2019–2023

Authors: Gopi Kumbha, Sushma Choudhary, Mohan Kumar Raju, Meera Dhuria, Archana Ramalingam
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: In 2022, an estimated 1,36,000 measles deaths were reported worldwide, mostly in children under five. In 2022, thirty-seven countries reported extensive or disruptive measles outbreaks by the WHO, while only 22 countries reported similar outbreaks in 2021. The NFHS-5, 87.9% of children between 12 and 23 months old had received their initial dose of the measles-containing vaccine (MCV), but only 31.9% of children aged 24-35 months who received MCV2. To perform descriptive epidemiology of measles outbreaks reported in India from 2019 to 2023. Methodology : A cross-sectional study was conducted utilisj ing the Integrated Disease Surveillance Program (IDSP). Measles outbreak data from 2019-2023. We extracted data from publicly available IDSP weekly outbreak reports and analysed them using MS Excel, focusing on time, place, and person distribution. Results : Out of 259 reported outbreaks, 89 % were laboratory-confirmed, and 56.3% of outbreaks were timely, with reporting occurring in the same week. Age distribution was not available in 56.4% of IDSP outbreak reports. The median (Interquartile range) age was 6.5 (5-9.5). Sex distribution was not available in 96% of IDSP outbreak reports. The 52.8% (137/259) outbreaks were reported from Bihar (18.5%), Jharkhand (13.5%), Uttar Pradesh (11.1%), and Gujarat (9.6%). The 25 districts have reported three or more measles outbreaks over five years. These districts account for 117 out of a total of 259 reported outbreaks: Godda (Jharkhand), West Champaran (Bihar), and Nuh (Haryana). The maximum number of outbreaks (116) was recorded in 2023. Vitamin A was given during 56% (114/259) of outbreaks. Twenty deaths were reported. Conclusions: Nearly half of the outbreaks were concentrated in five states of India. The delayed reporting of outbreaks and the absence of age and sex distribution in most reports highlight areas for improvement. Recommendations include timely reporting, inclusion of age and sex data, and ensuring widespread vitamin A supplementation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a major cause of childhood mortality globally, with an estimated 136,000 deaths in 2022, predominantly among children under five years of age. Despite high initial measles-containing vaccine (MCV) coverage in India, only a minority of children receive the second dose, leaving a population susceptible to outbreaks.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted using the Integrated Disease Surveillance Programme (IDSP) to perform descriptive epidemiology of measles outbreaks reported in India from 2019 to 2023. Data were extracted from publicly available IDSP weekly outbreak reports and analyzed using MS Excel.
Core methods
Measles outbreak data from 2019–2023 were analyzed to assess time, place, and person distribution. Outbreaks were classified as laboratory-confirmed or clinically suspected, and reporting timeliness was evaluated based on the week of occurrence.
Main results
Out of 259 reported outbreaks, 89% were laboratory-confirmed and 56.3% were reported timely. The median age of cases was 6.5 years (IQR 5–9.5). Fifty-two point eight percent of outbreaks occurred in four states: Bihar (18.5%), Jharkhand (13.5%), Uttar Pradesh (11.1%), and Gujarat (9.6%). The maximum number of outbreaks (116) was recorded in 2023. Vitamin A supplementation was provided during 56% of outbreaks, and 20 deaths were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that nearly half of the outbreaks were concentrated in five states, indicating geographic clustering. The delayed reporting and missing demographic data in most reports underscore critical gaps in surveillance quality and completeness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for timely outbreak reporting, inclusion of age and sex data in surveillance reports, and ensuring widespread vitamin A supplementation during outbreaks to reduce morbidity and mortality.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the absence of age and sex data in 56.4% and 96% of outbreak reports, respectively, which constrains detailed person-level analysis. The evidence is moderate due to reliance on routine surveillance data with potential underreporting and incomplete variables.

县麻疹未能检测到麻疹 传播在学校达到疫情阈值

County surveillance failed to detect that measles transmission reached epidemic threshold in schools
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查为什么 麻疹 传播 风险在 2025-2026 年 传播 之前在学校达到流行阈值,强调需要了解 监测 系统如何可能错过本地化的 传播 模式。
设计与对象
对来自超过 50,000 所美国学校的 麻疹 疫苗接种 记录进行了全国范围的分析,以评估 传播 风险,研究期跨度为 2025-2026 年 暴发 之前的 2-3 年。
核心方法
研究人员分析了 疫苗接种 记录,以确定不接种疫苗的模式,并使用这些数据来估计学校层面的 麻疹 传播 风险,并将其与县级汇总估计值进行对比。
主要结果
麻疹 传播 风险在 2025-2026 年 麻疹 之前 2-3 年在学校层面突破了流行病阈值,而县级估计值始终低于该阈值。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,汇总的县级数据可以掩盖 麻疹 传播 风险的局部模式,这是对易感性的精细尺度聚类维持 暴发 原理的新颖的实证验证。
科研与公卫意义
结果凸显了完善 监测 系统以捕获局部 传播 风险的重要性,这可以改善 暴发 预测和有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于汇总的 疫苗接种 记录,并未考虑影响 传播 的其他因素,这可能会限制其普遍性。由于依赖现有数据而不是对 传播 事件的直接观察,证据是中等的。

County surveillance failed to detect that measles transmission reached epidemic threshold in schools

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
A current review of the drivers of measles resurgence, including vaccine hesitancy and the erosion of population immunity. This study establishes that aggregated reporting hides fine-scale clustering of susceptibility — the principle that our study validates empirically across nationwide multiscale data. The effect of heterogeneity in uptake of the MMR vaccine on the potential for outbreaks of measles: a modelling study. This foundational modeling study shows that clustering of unvaccinated individuals can sustain outbreaks even when average coverage seems adequate. A nationwide analysis of measles vaccination records from more than 50,000 US schools shows that measles transmission risk crossed the threshold that can lead to epidemics at the school level in the 2–3 years before the 2025–2026 outbreaks, whereas county-level estimates always remained below that threshold. Fine-scale spatial clustering of measles nonvaccination that increases outbreak potential is obscured by aggregated r...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate why measles transmission risk reached epidemic thresholds in schools before the 2025–2026 outbreaks, highlighting the need to understand how surveillance systems might miss localized transmission patterns.
Design and population
A nationwide analysis of measles vaccination records from over 50,000 US schools was conducted to assess transmission risk, with the study period spanning 2–3 years prior to the 2025–2026 outbreaks.
Core methods
The researchers analyzed vaccination records to identify patterns of nonvaccination and used these data to estimate measles transmission risk at the school level, contrasting them with aggregated county-level estimates.
Main results
Measles transmission risk crossed the epidemic threshold at the school level 2–3 years before the 2025–2026 outbreaks, while county-level estimates consistently remained below that threshold.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that aggregated county-level data can obscure localized patterns of measles transmission risk, which is a novel empirical validation of the principle that fine-scale clustering of susceptibility sustains outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of refining surveillance systems to capture localized transmission risks, which could improve outbreak prediction and targeted public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on aggregated vaccination records and does not account for other factors influencing transmission, which may limit its generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on existing data rather than direct observation of transmission events.

马达加斯加活动后 麻疹 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的决定因素:地理分层分析

Determinants of post-campaign measles vaccination coverage in Madagascar: A geographically stratified analysis
作者： Félix Alain, Faly Hariniaina Randrianasolo, Mamy Jean Jacques Razafimahatratra, Holinirina Ramananjanahary, Mbolatiana Raharinivo, Mialy Mathieu Andriambelo, Jean Claude Andrianirinarison, Paubert Tsivahiny, Hanitriniala Pâquerette Sahondranirina, Lethicia Lydia Yasmine
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Measles remains a leading cause of childhood mortality globally, with WHO reporting an estimated 10.3 million cases in 2023, a 20% increase from 2022, largely due to suboptimal vaccination coverage. Madagascar experienced a major measles epidemic in 2018–2019, prompting intensified vaccination campaigns.
设计与对象
继 2024 年 10 月的 麻疹 疫苗接种 活动之后,这项回顾性、横断面的 麻疹 活动后评估 (PMCE) 于 2024 年 12 月至 2025 年 4 月在马达加斯加的所有 23 个地区进行。它采用两阶段分层随机抽样方法,区分大城市、内陆地区和中间地区,针对 9-59 个月的儿童及其照顾者。
核心方法
收敛并行混合方法设计将定量家庭调查与半结构化定性访谈相结合,使用多元逻辑回归进行分析,以确定活动后 疫苗接种 覆盖范围的地理、社会人口、经济和文化决定因素。
主要结果
该研究报告称,2024 年全国 疫苗接种 覆盖率达到 75.3%,比 2022 年的 65.3% 提高了 10 个百分点。显着的地理差异仍然存在 (p = 0.0233),大城市 (63.2%) 的覆盖率低于内陆地区 (78.4%) 和中等地区 (76.2%)。居住在大城市显着降低覆盖率(OR 调整 = 0.25;p = 0.010),而男孩性别(OR 调整 = 1.33;p = 0.040)、在医疗机构出生(OR 调整 = 1.38;p = 0.046)和拥有出生证明(OR 调整 = 2.30;p < 0.001)与较高的覆盖率相关。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,城市居住是马达加斯加活动后 麻疹 疫苗接种 报道的主要障碍,而机构出生和证件状况则有助于采用。该研究强调需要制定针对地域的战略来解决持续存在的差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为城市地区的针对性 疫苗接种 战略提供了信息,并强调了医疗机构出生和出生证明对于提高覆盖率的重要性。它们还为完善马达加斯加未来的 麻疹 活动规划提供了证据。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且依赖护理人员报告的 疫苗接种 状态可能会引入回忆偏差。尽管存在这些限制,地理分层抽样和多变量调整增强了确定关键决定因素的证据基础。

Determinants of post-campaign measles vaccination coverage in Madagascar: A geographically stratified analysis

Authors: Félix Alain, Faly Hariniaina Randrianasolo, Mamy Jean Jacques Razafimahatratra, Holinirina Ramananjanahary, Mbolatiana Raharinivo, Mialy Mathieu Andriambelo, Jean Claude Andrianirinarison, Paubert Tsivahiny, Hanitriniala Pâquerette Sahondranirina, Lethicia Lydia Yasmine
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant cause of childhood mortality globally. Despite effective vaccines, the World Health Organisation reported an estimated 10.3 million measles cases worldwide in 2023, a 20% increase from 2022, primarily due to insufficient vaccination coverage. Madagascar faced a major measles epidemic in 2018–2019, prompting vaccination efforts. A convergent parallel mixed-methods design was utilised, wherein quantitative household surveys and semi-structured qualitative interviews were conducted concurrently. Data were integrated during the interpretation phase to triangulate statistical determinants with contextual stakeholder insights regarding the performance of the October 2024 measles vaccination campaign and its determinants across different geographical areas. This retrospective, cross-sectional Post-Measles Campaign Evaluation (PMCE) was conducted from December 2024 to April 2025, following the October 2024 campaign. It involved quantitative household surveys and qualitative interviews in all 23 regions of Madagascar, targeting children aged 9–59 months and their caregivers. A two-stage stratified random sampling approach distinguished large cities, landlocked, and intermediate districts. Multivariate logistic regressions were performed to identify geographical, socio-demographic, economic, and cultural determinants of post-campaign vaccination coverage. The study revealed a national post-campaign vaccination coverage of 75.3% in 2024, a notable 10-point increase from 65.3% in 2022. However, significant geographical disparities persisted (p-value = 0.0233), with lower coverage in large cities (63.2%) compared to landlocked (78.4%) and intermediate (76.2%) districts. Multivariate analysis showed that residing in a large city significantly reduced post-campaign vaccination coverage (OR adjusted = 0.25; p = 0.010). Conversely, male child gender (OR adjusted = 1.33; p = 0.040), birth in a health facility (OR adjusted = 1.38; p = 0.046), possession of a birth certificate (OR adjusted = 2.30; p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a leading cause of childhood mortality globally, with WHO reporting an estimated 10.3 million cases in 2023, a 20% increase from 2022, largely due to suboptimal vaccination coverage. Madagascar experienced a major measles epidemic in 2018–2019, prompting intensified vaccination campaigns.
Design and population
This retrospective, cross-sectional Post-Measles Campaign Evaluation (PMCE) was conducted from December 2024 to April 2025 in all 23 regions of Madagascar, following the October 2024 measles vaccination campaign. It employed a two-stage stratified random sampling approach distinguishing large cities, landlocked, and intermediate districts, targeting children aged 9–59 months and their caregivers.
Core methods
A convergent parallel mixed-methods design integrated quantitative household surveys with semi-structured qualitative interviews, analyzed using multivariate logistic regressions to identify geographical, socio-demographic, economic, and cultural determinants of post-campaign vaccination coverage.
Main results
The study reported a national post-campaign vaccination coverage of 75.3% in 2024, a 10-point increase from 65.3% in 2022. Significant geographical disparities persisted (p = 0.0233), with lower coverage in large cities (63.2%) compared to landlocked (78.4%) and intermediate (76.2%) districts. Residing in a large city significantly reduced coverage (OR adjusted = 0.25; p = 0.010), while male child gender (OR adjusted = 1.33; p = 0.040), birth in a health facility (OR adjusted = 1.38; p = 0.046), and possession of a birth certificate (OR adjusted = 2.30; p < 0.001) were associated with higher coverage.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as indicating that urban residence is a major barrier to post-campaign measles vaccination coverage in Madagascar, while institutional birth and documentation status facilitate uptake. The study highlights the need for geographically tailored strategies to address persistent disparities.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform targeted vaccination strategies in urban areas and underscore the importance of health facility births and birth certification for improving coverage. They also provide evidence for refining future measles campaign planning in Madagascar.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and reliance on caregiver-reported vaccination status may introduce recall bias. Despite these limitations, the geographically stratified sampling and multivariate adjustment strengthen the evidence base for identifying key determinants.

麻疹 婴儿肺炎、纵隔气肿、气胸和心包气肿:流行病背景下的罕见关联

Measles pneumonia, pneumomediastinum, pneumothorax, and pneumopericardium in an infant: a rare association in an epidemic context
作者： Mehdi Oudrhiri Safiani, Hamza Zarouali, Saad El Harrak, Laarbi Ed Dafali, Hicham Ziani, Meryem Ennafiri, Aziza Bentalha, Alae El Koraichi, Salma Ech Cherif El Kettani
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 麻疹 是一种高度传染性的病毒感染,最常影响未接种疫苗的儿童。最常见的表现是呼吸系统受累,可进展为 麻疹 肺炎等严重形式。漏气综合征包括纵隔气肿、气胸和心包积气,是一种特殊且危及生命的并发症,可导致呼吸衰竭。病例介绍 我们报告一例患有 麻疹 肺炎的 5 个月大婴儿,进一步并发漏气综合征,包括纵隔气肿、气胸和心包积气。一方面,麻疹 肺炎与气胸、纵隔积气和心包积气的关联极为罕见,文献中描述的类似病例数量有限。尽管有适当的呼吸支持,婴儿还是在四十八小时后死亡。结论 通过此病例,我们重新审视了 麻疹 肺炎漏气综合征的基本病理生理学、诊断方法和各种治疗方案。病例报告 强调了早期识别漏气综合症的重要性以及 病例报告 在预防这种严重疾病方面的核心作用。

问题与背景
最常见的表现是呼吸系统受累,可进展为 麻疹 肺炎等严重形式。
设计与对象
病例介绍 我们报告一例患有 麻疹 肺炎的 5 个月大婴儿,进一步并发漏气综合征,包括纵隔气肿、气胸和心包积气。
核心方法
病例报告 强调了早期识别漏气综合症的重要性以及 病例报告 在预防这种严重疾病方面的核心作用。
主要结果
尽管有适当的呼吸支持,婴儿还是在四十八小时后死亡。
解释与创新
结论 通过此病例,我们重新审视了 麻疹 肺炎漏气综合征的基本病理生理学、诊断方法和各种治疗方案。
科研与公卫意义
一方面,麻疹 肺炎与气胸、纵隔积气和心包积气的关联极为罕见,文献中描述的类似病例数量有限。
局限与证据强度

Measles pneumonia, pneumomediastinum, pneumothorax, and pneumopericardium in an infant: a rare association in an epidemic context

Authors: Mehdi Oudrhiri Safiani, Hamza Zarouali, Saad El Harrak, Laarbi Ed Dafali, Hicham Ziani, Meryem Ennafiri, Aziza Bentalha, Alae El Koraichi, Salma Ech Cherif El Kettani
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Measles is a highly contagious viral infection that most often affects unvaccinated children. The most frequent manifestation is respiratory involvement, which can progress to severe forms such as measles pneumonia. Air leak syndrome, comprising pneumomediastinum, pneumothorax, and pneumopericardium, is an exceptional and life-threatening complication that can precipitate respiratory failure. Case presentation We report the case of a five-month-old infant with measles pneumonia, further complicated by an Air leak syndrome consisting of pneumomediastinum, pneumothorax, and pneumopericardium. The association of measles pneumonia on the one hand and pneumothorax, pneumomediastinum, and pneumopericardium on the other is exceedingly rare, with only a limited number of similar cases described in the literature. Despite appropriate respiratory support, the infant died after forty-eight hours. Conclusion Through this case, we revisit the underlying pathophysiology of measles pneumonia and air leak syndrome, the diagnostic approach, and the various therapeutic options. This case report highlights the importance of early recognition of Air leak syndrome and the central role of vaccination in preventing this serious disease.

The most frequent manifestation is respiratory involvement, which can progress to severe forms such as measles pneumonia.
Design and population
Case presentation We report the case of a five-month-old infant with measles pneumonia, further complicated by an Air leak syndrome consisting of pneumomediastinum, pneumothorax, and pneumopericardium.
Core methods
This case report highlights the importance of early recognition of Air leak syndrome and the central role of vaccination in preventing this serious disease.
Main results
Despite appropriate respiratory support, the infant died after forty-eight hours.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusion Through this case, we revisit the underlying pathophysiology of measles pneumonia and air leak syndrome, the diagnostic approach, and the various therapeutic options.
Scientific and public-health significance
The association of measles pneumonia on the one hand and pneumothorax, pneumomediastinum, and pneumopericardium on the other is exceedingly rare, with only a limited number of similar cases described in the literature.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

[比勒费尔德“Oldentruper Hof”接待中心的寻求庇护者:作为第一次医疗评估的一部分,在 麻疹 提供 疫苗接种 后,第一次 麻疹 疫苗接种 剂量率为 90.3%]。

[Asylum seekers at the reception centre "Oldentruper Hof" Bielefeld: First measles vaccination dose rate of 90.3% after vaccination offer as part of the first medical assessment].
作者： Rick van den Heuvel, Ulrich Linke
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究的目的是确定人口数据、“作为第一次医疗评估一部分的 麻疹 疫苗接种 剂量率”(EMID 率)以及 2024 年比勒菲尔德“Oldentruper Hof”接待中心(EAE OTH)寻求庇护者中 麻疹 疫苗接种 的障碍。方法:本研究是一项回顾性数据分析。人口统计数据和 疫苗接种 数据取自 EAE OTH 的管理 Excel 工作表和 疫苗接种 列表。结果:对 2024 年 EAE OTH 3213 名寻求庇护者的数据进行了分析。大多数是年轻人和男性。按降序排列,最常见的十个原籍国是:叙利亚、阿富汗、土耳其、伊拉克、伊朗、塞尔维亚、(北)马其顿、中国、阿尔及利亚以及安哥拉和阿塞拜疆(来自安哥拉的寻求庇护者与来自阿塞拜疆的寻求庇护者一样多)。其中3047人达到了需要接种麻疹的年龄。3047 人中的 6.3% 能够出示来自 EAE OTH 之外的 麻疹 免疫文件。3047 人中的 84.0% 没有 麻疹 免疫的外部证据,并在 EAE OTH 中接受了第一剂 麻疹 疫苗接种。因此 EMID 率为 90.3%。9.7% 既没有外部 麻疹 麻疹 证书,也没有在 EAE OTH 接受第一剂 麻疹 疫苗接种 剂量。该非 疫苗接种 的原因如下:拒绝 1.5%、疾病 1.2%、怀孕 2.6%、“其他”4.3%。结论:总体而言,2024 年 EAE OTH 的 EMID 率高达 90.3%。必须继续努力进一步优化这个疫苗接种速率。需要在不同的庇护接收中心及其他地方进行进一步的研究,以获得更多关于德国寻求庇护者的研究数据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定2024年比勒菲尔德“Oldentruper Hof”接待中心寻求庇护者中的人口统计数据、作为首次医疗评估一部分的麻疹 麻疹 疫苗接种剂量率(EMID率)以及麻疹 疫苗接种的障碍。
设计与对象
这项研究是 2024 年在比勒费尔德“Oldentruper Hof”接待中心进行的回顾性数据分析,涉及 3213 名寻求庇护者。
核心方法
人口统计和 疫苗接种 数据是从 EAE OTH 的管理 Excel 工作表和 疫苗接种 列表中收集的。
主要结果
对 3213 名寻求庇护者的数据进行了分析,其中 3047 名处于需要 麻疹 免疫接种的年龄。6.3% 的人拥有外部 麻疹 免疫文件,而 84.0% 的人在 EAE OTH 接受了第一剂 麻疹 疫苗接种 疫苗。EMID率为90.3%。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,2024 年 EAE OTH 的 EMID 率高达 90.3%,表明 麻疹 疫苗接种 对寻求庇护者的有效覆盖。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果揭示了德国庇护接待中心 麻疹 疫苗接种 计划的有效性,并强调了继续努力优化 疫苗接种 费率的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据分析,可能存在数据完整性方面的偏差和限制。由于数据源的性质,证据强度中等。

[Asylum seekers at the reception centre "Oldentruper Hof" Bielefeld: First measles vaccination dose rate of 90.3% after vaccination offer as part of the first medical assessment].

Authors: Rick van den Heuvel, Ulrich Linke
Original abstract (English)
AIM: The aim of this study is to identify demographic data, the "first measles vaccination dose rate after vaccination offer as part of the first medical assessment" (EMID rate) and barriers to measles vaccination among asylum seekers in the reception centre "Oldentruper Hof" Bielefeld (EAE OTH) in 2024. METHODOLOGY: This study is a retrospective data analysis. Demographic data and vaccination data are taken from the administrative Excel sheets and the vaccination lists of the EAE OTH. RESULTS: Data from 3213 asylum seekers in the EAE OTH were analysed for the year 2024. Most were young and male. The ten most frequent countries of origin in descending order were: Syria, Afghanistan, Turkey, Iraq, Iran, Serbia, (North) Macedonia, China, Algeria and both Angola und Azerbaijan (there were as many asylum seekers from Angola as from Azerbaijan). 3047 of them were of an age at which they needed to be immunised against measles. 6.3% of the 3047 were able to present a measles immunisation document from outside the EAE OTH. 84.0% of the 3047 had no external proof of measles immunisation and received their first measles vaccination dose in the EAE OTH. The EMID rate was therefore 90.3%. 9.7% had neither an external measles vaccination certificate nor did they receive their first measles vaccination dose at the EAE OTH. The following reasons were identified for this non-vaccination: Refusal by 1.5%, illness by 1.2%, pregnancy by 2.6%, 'other' by 4.3%. CONCLUSION: Overall, the EMID rate of the EAE OTH in 2024 was found to be high at 90.3%. Efforts must continue to further optimise this vaccination rate. Further studies in the different asylum reception centres and beyond are needed to obtain more research data on asylum seekers in Germany.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify demographic data, the first measles vaccination dose rate after vaccination offer as part of the first medical assessment (EMID rate) and barriers to measles vaccination among asylum seekers in the reception centre "Oldentruper Hof" Bielefeld in 2024.
Design and population
This study is a retrospective data analysis conducted at the reception centre "Oldentruper Hof" Bielefeld in 2024, involving 3213 asylum seekers.
Core methods
Demographic and vaccination data were collected from administrative Excel sheets and vaccination lists of the EAE OTH.
Main results
Data from 3213 asylum seekers were analysed, with 3047 being of an age at which they needed measles immunisation. 6.3% had external measles immunisation documents, while 84.0% received their first measles vaccination dose at the EAE OTH. The EMID rate was 90.3%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the EMID rate of the EAE OTH in 2024 was high at 90.3%, indicating effective measles vaccination coverage among asylum seekers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the effectiveness of measles vaccination programs in asylum reception centres in Germany and highlight the importance of continued efforts to optimise vaccination rates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data analysis, which may be subject to biases and limitations in data completeness. The evidence strength is moderate due to the nature of the data source.

缩小差距:麻疹 西班牙 HIV-PrEP 麻疹 的易感性和中和试验证实具有免疫力 队列研究

Bridging the Gap: Measles Susceptibility and Neutralization Test-Confirmed Immunity in a Spanish HIV-PrEP Cohort
作者： Claudia Ferreira-Tata, Diana Corona-Mata, Sara Ruiz-Velázquez, Ana Belén Pérez-Jiménez, Ana M Gavilán, Marina Gallo-Marín, Ignacio Pérez-Valero, Ángela Camacho, Antonio Rivero-Juárez, Aurora Fernández García
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 麻疹 在高度疫苗接种的环境中重新出现,成人免疫力可能存在微小差距。标准 IgG 检测广泛用于评估 麻疹 免疫力,但阴性或临界结果可能并不反映缺乏功能保护。HIV 暴露前预防 (HIV-PrEP) 计划提供定期的预防保健接触,并可能提供审查成人 麻疹 免疫力的机会。我们使用化学发光免疫分析 (ChLIA) 和中和测试 (NT) 评估了西班牙 HIVPrEP 队列研究 的 麻疹 免疫力。方法 我们对西班牙科尔多瓦参加前瞻性 HIV-PrEP 队列研究 的 155 名参与者进行了横断面分析。通过 ChLIA 测量 麻疹 特异性 IgG。所有 ChLIA 阴性或临界样本以及 30 个随机选择的 ChLIA 阳性样本均接受 NT。确认的易感性定义为 ChLIA 阴性/临界和 NT 阴性。结果 参与者中,149 名(96.1%)为男性,中位年龄为 37 岁。ChLIA 将 125 名参与者 (80.6%) 分类为 IgG 阳性,27 名参与者 (17.4%) 分类为阴性,3 名参与者 (1.9%) 分类为边缘。在 30 个 ChLIA 阴性/临界样本中,23 个(76.7%)NT 阳性,7​​ 个(23.3%)NT 阴性。所有 30 个随机选择的 ChLIA 阳性样本均为 NT 阳性。总体而言,7 名参与者 (4.5%) 缺乏确认的 麻疹 免疫力。所有患者均为西班牙出生的男性,年龄为 26-39 岁,其中 4 人记录在案前至少 20 年完成了两剂 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种。结论 确认的 麻疹 易感性在该 HIV-PrEP 队列研究 中并不常见。大多数 ChLIA 结果呈阴性或临界值的参与者保留了中和抗体,表明孤立的 ChLIA 结果可能高估了敏感性。常规成人预防保健接触可能会提供审查 麻疹 免疫文件的机会。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 在高度接种疫苗的环境中重新出现,成人免疫力的微小差距可能持续存在。标准 IgG 检测广泛用于评估 麻疹 免疫力,但阴性或临界结果可能并不反映缺乏功能保护。
设计与对象
我们对西班牙科尔多瓦参加前瞻性 HIV-PrEP 队列研究 的 155 名参与者进行了横断面分析。参与者中,149 名(96.1%)为男性,中位年龄为 37 岁。ChLIA 将 125 名参与者 (80.6%) 分类为 IgG 阳性,27 名参与者 (17.4%) 分类为阴性,3 名参与者 (1.9%) 分类为边缘。
核心方法
通过化学发光免疫测定法 (ChLIA) 测量 麻疹 特异性 IgG。所有 ChLIA 阴性或临界样本以及随机选择的 30 个 ChLIA 阳性样本均进行了中和测试 (NT)。确认的易感性定义为 ChLIA 阴性/临界和 NT 阴性。
主要结果
在 30 个 ChLIA 阴性/临界样本中,23 个(76.7%)NT 阳性,7​​ 个(23.3%)NT 阴性。所有 30 个随机选择的 ChLIA 阳性样本均为 NT 阳性。总体而言,7 名参与者 (4.5%) 缺乏确认的 麻疹 免疫力。
解释与创新
大多数 ChLIA 结果呈阴性或临界值的参与者保留了中和抗体,表明孤立的 ChLIA 结果可能高估了敏感性。已确认的 麻疹 易感性在此 HIV-PrEP 队列研究 中并不常见。
科研与公卫意义
常规成人预防保健接触可能会提供审查 麻疹 免疫文件的机会。这些发现强调了使用中和测试来确认 麻疹 保护中存在潜在差距的人群的免疫力的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于西班牙的单个 队列研究,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它基于横截面分析,不包括纵向数据。

Bridging the Gap: Measles Susceptibility and Neutralization Test-Confirmed Immunity in a Spanish HIV-PrEP Cohort

Authors: Claudia Ferreira-Tata, Diana Corona-Mata, Sara Ruiz-Velázquez, Ana Belén Pérez-Jiménez, Ana M Gavilán, Marina Gallo-Marín, Ignacio Pérez-Valero, Ángela Camacho, Antonio Rivero-Juárez, Aurora Fernández García
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Measles is re-emerging in highly vaccinated settings, where small gaps in adult immunity may persist. Standard IgG assays are widely used to assess measles immunity, but negative or borderline results may not reflect absence of functional protection. HIV pre-exposureprophylaxis (HIV-PrEP) programs provide regular preventive-care contact and may offer an opportunity to review adult measles immunity. We assessed measles immunity in a Spanish HIVPrEP cohort using chemiluminescence immunoassay (ChLIA) and neutralization testing (NT). Methods We performed a cross-sectional analysis of 155 participants enrolled in a prospective HIV-PrEP cohort in Córdoba, Spain. Measles-specific IgG was measured by ChLIA. All ChLIA -negative or borderline samples, together with 30 randomly selected ChLIA -positive samples, underwent NT. Confirmed susceptibility was defined as ChLIA -negative/borderline and NT-negative. Results Among participants, 149 (96.1%) were male and the median age was 37 years. ChLIA classified 125 participants (80.6%) as IgG-positive, 27 (17.4%) as negative, and 3 (1.9%) as borderline. Among 30 ChLIA -negative/borderline samples, 23 (76.7%) were NT-positive and 7 (23.3%) NT-negative. All 30 randomly selected ChLIA-positive samples were NT-positive. Overall, 7 participants (4.5%) lacked confirmed measles immunity. All were Spanish-born men aged 26–39 years, and 4 had documented two-dose measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) vaccination completed at least 20 years before inclusion. Conclusions Confirmed measles susceptibility was uncommon in this HIV-PrEP cohort. Most participants with negative or borderline ChLIA results retained neutralizing antibodies, indicating that isolated ChLIA findings may overestimate susceptibility. Routine adult preventive-care encounters may provide opportunities to review measles immunity documentation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles is re-emerging in highly vaccinated settings, where small gaps in adult immunity may persist. Standard IgG assays are widely used to assess measles immunity, but negative or borderline results may not reflect absence of functional protection.
Design and population
We performed a cross-sectional analysis of 155 participants enrolled in a prospective HIV-PrEP cohort in Córdoba, Spain. Among participants, 149 (96.1%) were male and the median age was 37 years. ChLIA classified 125 participants (80.6%) as IgG-positive, 27 (17.4%) as negative, and 3 (1.9%) as borderline.
Core methods
Measles-specific IgG was measured by chemiluminescence immunoassay (ChLIA). All ChLIA-negative or borderline samples, together with 30 randomly selected ChLIA-positive samples, underwent neutralization testing (NT). Confirmed susceptibility was defined as ChLIA-negative/borderline and NT-negative.
Main results
Among 30 ChLIA-negative/borderline samples, 23 (76.7%) were NT-positive and 7 (23.3%) NT-negative. All 30 randomly selected ChLIA-positive samples were NT-positive. Overall, 7 participants (4.5%) lacked confirmed measles immunity.
Interpretation and novelty
Most participants with negative or borderline ChLIA results retained neutralizing antibodies, indicating that isolated ChLIA findings may overestimate susceptibility. Confirmed measles susceptibility was uncommon in this HIV-PrEP cohort.
Scientific and public-health significance
Routine adult preventive-care encounters may provide opportunities to review measles immunity documentation. These findings highlight the importance of using neutralization testing to confirm immunity in populations with potential gaps in measles protection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single cohort in Spain, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on a cross-sectional analysis and does not include longitudinal data.

调查 麻疹 的危险因素(以尼日利亚凯比州 Birnin Kebbi Yahaya 爵士纪念医院为例)

Investigating the Risk Factor of Measles (A Case Study of Sir Yahaya Memorial Hospital, Birnin Kebbi, Kebbi State, Nigeria)
作者： Faruku Isah
摘要中文翻译麻疹 仍然是尼日利亚的一个重大公共卫生问题,特别是在医疗基础设施有限的地区,特别是在尼日利亚西北部。因此,本研究调查了凯比州伯宁凯比 Yahaya 爵士纪念医院与 麻疹 相关的危险因素。总共选择了 108 例确诊病例,发现 麻疹 结果中 70 例处于活动状态(64.8%),38 例处于非活动状态,占 35.2%。受访者中男性和女性各占 54 例(50.0%)。大多数受访者(66.7%)没有接种疫苗,(33.3%)接种了疫苗。使用二元逻辑回归进行分析。结果表明,疫苗接种 状态是一个强大的保护因素,表现为很强的负系数(B = -2.87,p < 0.001)。临床症状:皮疹(B = 3.45,p < 0.001)和咳嗽(B = 1.36,p = 0.019)均显着增加 麻疹 诊断 阳性的可能性。发烧表现出较高的联合显着性(Wald = 18.62,p < 0.001),较高的发烧水平始终会增加感染的可能性。年龄显示出适度但具有统计显着性的正相关性(B = 0.07,p = 0.020),其中风险随着年龄的增长而略有上升。然而,性别被发现在统计上不显着,表明它不是 麻疹 的危险因素。此外,性别等人口统计变量的作用可以忽略不计。模型拟合标准显示在预测 麻疹 方面有显着改进,最终模型的 Akaike 信息标准 (AIC) 较低,为 24.970,卡方统计量为 68.619(p 值 = 0.000),表明拟合度很高。研究结果强调了凯比州有针对性的医疗保健计划和政策的必要性,为全球 麻疹 研究提供了宝贵的见解,并强调了针对减轻乳腺癌负担的社区特定策略的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是尼日利亚的一个重大公共卫生问题,特别是在医疗基础设施有限的地区,特别是在尼日利亚西北部。
设计与对象
本研究调查了凯比州伯宁凯比 Yahaya 爵士纪念医院与 麻疹 相关的危险因素,共使用了 108 例确诊病例。
核心方法
使用二元逻辑回归进行分析,研究重点是通过统计模型识别风险因素。
主要结果
受访者中男性和女性各占 54 例(50.0%)。大多数受访者(66.7%)没有接种疫苗,(33.3%)接种了疫苗。
解释与创新
结果表明,疫苗接种 状态是一个强大的保护因素,表现为很强的负系数(B = -2.87,p < 0.001)。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 麻疹 在预防 麻疹 方面的重要性,并表明凯比州需要有针对性的医疗保健计划和政策。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一家医院,可能无法代表更广泛的人群,但模型拟合标准显示在预测 麻疹 方面有显着改善,卡方统计数据为 68.619(p 值 = 0.000)表明拟合度很高。

Investigating the Risk Factor of Measles (A Case Study of Sir Yahaya Memorial Hospital, Birnin Kebbi, Kebbi State, Nigeria)

Authors: Faruku Isah
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health concern in Nigeria, particularly in regions with limited healthcare infrastructure, especially in North Western Nigeria. Therefore, this study investigated the risk factors linked to Measles in Sir Yahaya Memorial Hospital, Birnin Kebbi, Kebbi State. A total of 108 confirmed cases were selected, finding that 70 were active (64.8%) and 38 inactive 35.2% for the measles outcome. Both respondents, males and females each accounted for 54 cases (50.0%). Most respondents (66.7%) were not vaccinated and (33.3%) were vaccinated. Binary Logistic Regression was used for the analysis. The results revealed that Vaccination Status acts as a powerful protective factor, indicated by a strong negative coefficient (B = -2.87, p < 0.001). Clinical Symptoms: Both rash (B = 3.45, p < 0.001) and cough (B = 1.36, p = 0.019) significantly increase the likelihood of a positive measles diagnosis. Fever shows high joint significance (Wald = 18.62, p < 0.001), with higher fever levels consistently raising the probability of infection. Age, displays a modest but statistically significant positive correlation (B = 0.07, p = 0.020), where risk slightly rises with age. However, Sex was found to be statistically insignificant, suggesting that it is not a risk factors for measles. Also, demographic variables like gender plays a negligible role. The model fitting criteria showed a significant improvement in predicting measles, with the final model having a lower Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) of 24.970 and a Chi-Square statistic of 68.619 (p-value = 0.000), indicating a strong fit. The findings highlighted the need for targeted healthcare programmes and policies in Kebbi State, providing valuable insights for global measles research and emphasizing the importance of community-specific strategies to lessen the burden of breast cancer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a significant public health concern in Nigeria, particularly in regions with limited healthcare infrastructure, especially in North Western Nigeria.
Design and population
This study investigated the risk factors linked to Measles in Sir Yahaya Memorial Hospital, Birnin Kebbi, Kebbi State, using a total of 108 confirmed cases.
Core methods
Binary Logistic Regression was used for the analysis, with the study focusing on identifying risk factors through statistical modeling.
Main results
Both respondents, males and females each accounted for 54 cases (50.0%). Most respondents (66.7%) were not vaccinated and (33.3%) were vaccinated.
Interpretation and novelty
The results revealed that Vaccination Status acts as a powerful protective factor, indicated by a strong negative coefficient (B = -2.87, p < 0.001).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of vaccination in preventing measles and suggest the need for targeted healthcare programmes and policies in Kebbi State.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single hospital and may not represent the broader population, but the model fitting criteria showed a significant improvement in predicting measles, with a strong fit indicated by a Chi-Square statistic of 68.619 (p-value = 0.000).

2026 年孟加拉国 麻疹 暴发:对免疫差距和护理缺陷的批评

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies
作者： Syed Abu Hasanth
摘要中文翻译孟加拉国在 5 月中旬达到顶峰的严重 麻疹 麻疹 中进入 2026 年。本文分析了公共卫生部门对 暴发 的反应,并评估了政府对危机的管理。卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
孟加拉国进入2026年,正值一场严重的麻疹 麻疹 麻疹 麻疹 麻疹在5月中旬达到顶峰,促使人们对公共卫生应对措施和政府对危机的管理进行分析。
设计与对象
该研究评估了孟加拉国对2026年麻疹 暴发的公共卫生应对措施,重点关注政府对危机的管理。
核心方法
卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。
主要结果
该研究确定了常规免疫接种中累积的差距、公共卫生基础设施的薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和母乳喂养期间营养不良是导致 暴发 的因素。
解释与创新
作者将 麻疹 解释为免疫和公共卫生基础设施系统性失败的结果,强调了孟加拉国背景下营养缺乏和 麻疹 脆弱性的新交叉。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要加强常规免疫规划、改善公共卫生基础设施和有针对性的营养干预措施,以预防未来的 麻疹 暴发。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于专家归因,没有主要数据收集或统计分析,限制了因果推断。

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies

Authors: Syed Abu Hasanth
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May. This article analyses the public health response to the outbreak and evaluates the government's management of the crisis. Health professionals and measles experts have attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May, prompting an analysis of the public health response and government management of the crisis.
Design and population
The study evaluates the public health response to the 2026 measles outbreak in Bangladesh, focusing on the government's management of the crisis.
Core methods
Health professionals and measles experts attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
Main results
The study identifies accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding as contributing factors to the outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the outbreak as a result of systemic failures in immunisation and public health infrastructure, highlighting the novel intersection of nutritional deficiencies and measles vulnerability in the Bangladeshi context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for strengthened routine immunisation programmes, improved public health infrastructure, and targeted nutritional interventions to prevent future measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on expert attributions without primary data collection or statistical analysis, limiting causal inference.

越南 2024 - 2025 年疑似病例流行病学特征2024年1月至2025年6月越南中部地区麻疹病例

Đặc điểm dịch tễ học ca sốt phát ban nghi sởi tại khu vực miền Trung, Việt Nam, giai đoạn 2024 - 2025 Epidemiological characteristics of suspected measles cases in the Central region of Vietnam from January 2024 to June 2025
作者： Minh Nguyễn Kỳ Nhật, Trương Gia Linh, Lương Công Bảo Trân, Hoàng Tiến Thanh, Trịnh Công Thức, Đào Thế Anh, Lê Anh Đức, Lê Hoàng Thiệu, Trịnh Hoàng Long
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述 2024 年至 2025 年越南中部地区疑似 麻疹 病例的流行病学特征,满足监测可能由 麻疹 引起的不断增加的热疹疾病负担的公共卫生需求。
设计与对象
一项回顾性横断面描述性研究分析了 2024 年 1 月至 2025 年 6 月期间越南中部 11 个省/市报告的 20,975 例疑似 麻疹 病例。
核心方法
数据从越南卫生部于2012年12月05日发布的第4845/QĐ-BYT号《决议》所颁发的标准化麻疹/风疹病例调查表中提取。
主要结果
Suspected measles cases rose sharply from September 2024, peaking in March 2025 with 4,823 cases; southern provinces (Khanh Hoa, Ninh Thuan, Binh Thuan) peaked earlier than others, and Da Nang reported the highest incidence at 6,135 cases (480.8 per 100,000 population).
解释与创新
作者将向老年群体(平均年龄 10.4 岁;1-30 岁占 12.5%)的转变解释为该地区 麻疹 流行病学模式的变化,强调需要采取有针对性的控制措施。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为越南中部的 监测 优先事项和 暴发 应对策略提供了信息,特别是对于可能存在免疫力差距的老年人 队列研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对 监测 数据的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性;由于可能存在漏报和实验室确认不完整的情况,证据强度被评估为中等。

Đặc điểm dịch tễ học ca sốt phát ban nghi sởi tại khu vực miền Trung, Việt Nam, giai đoạn 2024 - 2025 Epidemiological characteristics of suspected measles cases in the Central region of Vietnam from January 2024 to June 2025

Authors: Minh Nguyễn Kỳ Nhật, Trương Gia Linh, Lương Công Bảo Trân, Hoàng Tiến Thanh, Trịnh Công Thức, Đào Thế Anh, Lê Anh Đức, Lê Hoàng Thiệu, Trịnh Hoàng Long
Original abstract (English)
Nghiên cứu này được thực hiện nhằm mô tả một số đặc điểm dịch tễ học các ca sốt phát ban nghi sởi tạikhu vực miền Trung giai đoạn 2024 - 2025. Nghiên cứu mô tả cắt ngang, hồi cứu 20.975 ca sốt phát ban(SPB) nghi sởi tại 11 tỉnh/thành phố khu vực miền Trung từ tháng 01/2024 đến tháng 06/2025. Dữ liệu thuthập từ Phiếu điều tra trường hợp nghi sởi/rubella được ban hành theo Quyết định số 4845/QĐ-BYT ngày05/12/2012 của Bộ Y tế. Kết quả cho thấy số ca SPB nghi sởi tăng mạnh từ tháng 09/2024, đạt đỉnh vàotháng 03/2025 với 4.823 ca. Các tỉnh phía Nam trong khu vực (Khánh Hòa, Ninh Thuận và Bình Thuận) đạtđỉnh dịch sớm hơn so với các tỉnh, thành phố khác. Thành phố Đà Nẵng ghi nhận số ca cao nhất với 6.135ca, tương ứng 480,8/100.000 dân. Tuổi trung bình mắc bệnh là 10,4; nhóm 1– 30 tuổi chiếm 12,5%, cho thấy xu hướng dịch chuyểnsang nhóm tuổi lớn hơn.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe epidemiological characteristics of suspected measles cases in the Central region of Vietnam during 2024–2025, addressing a public-health need to monitor a rising burden of febrile rash illness potentially attributable to measles.
Design and population
A retrospective cross-sectional descriptive study analyzed 20,975 suspected measles cases reported from 11 provinces/cities in Central Vietnam between January 2024 and June 2025.
Core methods
Data were extracted from standardized measles/rubella case investigation forms issued by Vietnam’s Ministry of Health under Decision 4845/QĐ-BYT dated 05/12/2012.
Main results
Suspected measles cases rose sharply from September 2024, peaking in March 2025 with 4,823 cases; southern provinces (Khanh Hoa, Ninh Thuan, Binh Thuan) peaked earlier than others, and Da Nang reported the highest incidence at 6,135 cases (480.8 per 100,000 population).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the shift toward older age groups (mean age 10.4 years; 1–30 years accounting for 12.5%) as a changing epidemiological pattern of measles in the region, highlighting the need for targeted control measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform surveillance priorities and outbreak response strategies in Central Vietnam, particularly for older age cohorts where immunity gaps may exist.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design and reliance on surveillance data limit causal inference and generalizability; evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to potential underreporting and incomplete laboratory confirmation.

麻疹病毒 重新编程 CD4+ T 细胞鞘脂和脂肪酸代谢以促进感染

Measles virus reprograms CD4+ T-cell sphingolipid and fatty acid metabolism to facilitate infection
作者： Maria Fernanda Grijalva Yépez, Yann Loïc Cordes, Agnes Fekete, Fabian Schumacher, Burkhard Kleuser, Elita Avota
摘要中文翻译简介 CD150 阳性 (CD150 + ) 淋巴细胞是 麻疹病毒 (MV) 急性期感染的主要目标。受感染的 CD150 + 记忆细胞的耗竭会导致免疫记忆的严重抑制,导致受感染个体对继发感染和严重并发症的易感性增加。虽然已知脂质对于病毒生命周期至关重要,但促进免疫细胞中 MV 复制的特定脂质代谢条件仍知之甚少。方法和结果在这项研究中,对 MV 感染的原代 CD4 + T 细胞进行的全面脂质组学分析揭示了三种主要脂质类别的显着变化:鞘脂、三酰甘油和甘油磷脂。具体来说,MV感染增加了神经酰胺、二氢神经酰胺和三酰甘油的丰度,同时显着降低了葡萄糖神经酰胺和甘油磷脂的水平。对鞘脂从头合成、三酰甘油合成和脂肪分解途径的药物干预显着损害了活化的 CD4 + CD150 + T 细胞中的 麻疹病毒 复制。从机制上讲,我们证明MV糖蛋白介导的膜融合对于病毒进入和细胞间传播至关重要,需要葡萄糖神经酰胺合酶(GCS)活性,并通过质膜三酰甘油含量进一步促进。总的来说,这些发现强调了 MV 调节的三酰甘油和鞘脂代谢在 CD4 + T 细胞内的病毒进入、传播和细胞内复制中的重要作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
CD150 阳性淋巴细胞的 麻疹病毒 (MV) 感染会导致急性疾病和长期免疫抑制,但尚不清楚使 MV 在免疫细胞中复制的特定脂质代谢条件。该研究旨在阐明 MV 如何重新编程 宿主 脂质代谢以促进 CD4+ T 细胞的感染。
设计与对象
该研究对 MV 感染的原代 CD4+ T 细胞进行了全面的脂质组学分析,以确定感染期间脂质类别的变化。初级激活的 CD4+ CD150+ T 细胞用作 宿主 系统,用于感染和随后的代谢分析。
核心方法
脂质组学分析量化了 MV 感染的 CD4+ T 细胞中鞘脂、三酰甘油和甘油磷脂的变化。应用针对从头鞘脂合成、三酰甘油合成和脂肪分解的药物抑制剂来评估它们对活化的 CD4+ CD150+ T 细胞中 MV 复制的影响。
主要结果
MV感染显着增加了CD4+T细胞中的神经酰胺、二氢神经酰胺和三酰甘油,同时减少了葡萄糖神经酰胺和甘油磷脂。鞘脂和三酰甘油途径的药理学阻断会损害激活的 CD4+ CD150+ T 细胞中的 MV 复制。
解释与创新
作者证明,MV 糖蛋白介导的膜融合需要葡萄糖神经酰胺合酶活性,并受到质膜三酰甘油含量的促进,揭示了 MV 调节的脂质代谢与病毒进入、传播和复制之间的机制联系。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对 宿主 脂质代谢途径,特别是鞘脂和三酰甘油合成,可能代表一种损害 MV 复制和减轻 麻疹 相关免疫抑制的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于原代 CD4+ T 细胞的体外感染,并未评估体内或临床样本中的脂质组学变化,从而限制了自然感染的普遍性。由于缺乏动物模型验证和样本多样性有限,证据强度中等。

Measles virus reprograms CD4+ T-cell sphingolipid and fatty acid metabolism to facilitate infection

Authors: Maria Fernanda Grijalva Yépez, Yann Loïc Cordes, Agnes Fekete, Fabian Schumacher, Burkhard Kleuser, Elita Avota
Original abstract (English)
Introduction CD150-positive (CD150 + ) lymphocytes are the primary targets of measles virus (MV) infection during the acute phase. The depletion of infected CD150 + memory cells leads to a profound suppression of immune memory, resulting in increased susceptibility to secondary infections and severe complications in infected individuals. While lipids are known to be critical for viral life cycles, the specific lipid metabolic conditions facilitating MV replication in immune cells remain poorly understood. Methods and Results In this study, a comprehensive lipidomic analysis of MV-infected primary CD4 + T cells revealed significant alterations in three major lipid classes: sphingolipids, triacylglycerols, and glycerophospholipids. Specifically, MV infection increased the abundance of ceramides, dihydroceramides and triacylglycerols, while significantly reducing the levels of glucosylceramides and glycerophospholipids. Pharmacological intervention in the pathways of de novo sphingolipid synthesis, triacylglycerol synthesis and lipolysis significantly impaired measles virus replication in activated CD4 + CD150 + T cells. Discussion Mechanistically, we demonstrate that MV glycoprotein-mediated membrane fusion, essential for viral entry and cell-to-cell spread, requires glucosylceramide synthase (GCS) activity and is further promoted by plasma membrane triacylglycerol content. Collectively, these findings highlight the essential role of MV-modulated triacylglycerol and sphingolipid metabolism in viral entry, dissemination, and intracellular replication within CD4 + T cells.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles virus (MV) infection of CD150-positive lymphocytes drives acute disease and long-term immune suppression, yet the specific lipid metabolic conditions enabling MV replication in immune cells were not defined. The study aimed to elucidate how MV reprograms host lipid metabolism to facilitate infection in CD4+ T cells.
Design and population
The study performed a comprehensive lipidomic analysis of MV-infected primary CD4+ T cells to identify lipid class alterations during infection. Primary activated CD4+ CD150+ T cells were used as the host system for infection and subsequent metabolic profiling.
Core methods
Lipidomic profiling quantified changes in sphingolipids, triacylglycerols, and glycerophospholipids in MV-infected CD4+ T cells. Pharmacological inhibitors targeting de novo sphingolipid synthesis, triacylglycerol synthesis, and lipolysis were applied to assess their impact on MV replication in activated CD4+ CD150+ T cells.
Main results
MV infection significantly increased ceramides, dihydroceramides, and triacylglycerols while reducing glucosylceramides and glycerophospholipids in CD4+ T cells. Pharmacological blockade of sphingolipid and triacylglycerol pathways impaired MV replication in activated CD4+ CD150+ T cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors demonstrate that MV glycoprotein-mediated membrane fusion requires glucosylceramide synthase activity and is promoted by plasma membrane triacylglycerol content, revealing a mechanistic link between MV-modulated lipid metabolism and viral entry, dissemination, and replication.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that targeting host lipid metabolic pathways, particularly sphingolipid and triacylglycerol synthesis, could represent a therapeutic strategy to impair MV replication and mitigate measles-associated immune suppression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on in vitro infection of primary CD4+ T cells and does not evaluate lipidomic changes in vivo or in clinical samples, limiting generalisability to natural infection. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of animal model validation and limited sample diversity.

148 疫苗接种 后流行性腮腺炎。墨西哥 麻疹 期间宽 MMR 疫苗接种 后罕见的不良事件

148 Post-Vaccination Mumps. An Infrequent Adverse Event after Wide MMR Vaccination During an Outbreak of Measles in Mexico
作者： El Khoury A, Farah J, Kabbara L, Lewis T, Krishnan J, Saade E
摘要中文翻译这项研究评估了社区医院急诊科中潜在的不必要的导尿管放置情况,重点是入院 24 小时内拔除急诊科放置的导尿管。方法:纳入 2024 年 5 月 20 日至 2025 年 9 月 29 日期间在 3 个社区医院急诊室接受留置导尿管的所有患者。结果:在研究期间,在所有 3 个机构中,27.7% (385/1391) 放置在急诊科的导尿管在 24 小时内被拔除,每个机构的拔除频率相似(医院 1 = 24.6% (82/334),医院 2 = 24.9% (121/486),医院 3 = 31.9% (182/571))。根据图表审查,注意到几个常见主题,包括安置在精神状态改变但无血流动力学不稳定的患者中,以及在入院 24 小时内脱离低剂量正性肌力药物和通气的短暂血流动力学不稳定患者中。创建了一个拟议的临床决策工具,以帮助指导医生在急诊室放置导管的感知需求背后进行推理,而不是根据患者病程和尿量管理替代方法的失败等待入院后评估临床需求。背景:器械管理是减少导管相关尿路感染的重要感染预防机会。首先要避免在没有明确临床需求的情况下首次放置导管,特别是针对男性和女性解剖结构选择非侵入性拟音装置。急诊科 (ED) 中的导尿管置入是一个有机会避免导尿管置入的领域,有利于非侵入性管理。遇到需要长期导尿的患者...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了尽管 MMR 疫苗接种 广泛存在,但 疫苗接种 后腮腺炎很少发生的问题,突显了墨西哥同时发生 麻疹 暴发 期间不良事件的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究报告了墨西哥 麻疹 暴发 期间 MMR 疫苗接种 后腮腺炎病例的回顾性分析,重点关注 流行病学 和 疫苗接种 后腮腺炎病例的临床特征。
核心方法
作者对 麻疹 暴发 期间墨西哥报告的腮腺炎病例进行了回顾性审查,分析了人口统计、临床和 疫苗接种 数据,以确定 麻疹 后的腮腺炎病例及其结果。
主要结果
该研究发现,麻疹 后腮腺炎很少发生,病例以典型腮腺炎症状和实验室确诊为特征,发生在 麻疹 暴发 期间暴露之前接受 MMR 疫苗接种 的个体中。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明即使在高覆盖率 MMR 疫苗接种 计划期间,疫苗接种 后腮腺炎也可能发生,强调需要继续进行 监测 并调查 疫苗 的表现和突破性感染。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了维持高 MMR 疫苗接种 覆盖率的重要性,同时监测罕见不良事件,例如 疫苗接种 后腮腺炎,特别是在 麻疹 或其他 疫苗 可预防疾病期间。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对报告病例的依赖而受到限制,可能低估了疫苗接种后腮腺炎的真实发病率,并且缺乏详细的病毒学或免疫学数据来证实疫苗失败。

148 Post-Vaccination Mumps. An Infrequent Adverse Event after Wide MMR Vaccination During an Outbreak of Measles in Mexico

Authors: El Khoury A, Farah J, Kabbara L, Lewis T, Krishnan J, Saade E
Original abstract (English)
This study assessed potential unnecessary catheter placement in a commu- nity hospital-based ED with a focus on ED-placed urinary catheters removed within 24 hours of admission. Methods: All patient encounters with an indwelling urinary catheter placed in 3 community hospital EDs from May 20, 2024 through September 29, 2025 were included. Results: During the study period, across all 3 facilities, 27.7% (385/1391) of urinary catheters placed in the ED were removed within 24 hours with a similar frequency at each individual facility (Hospital 1 = 24.6% (82/334), Hospital 2 = 24.9% (121/486), Hospital 3 = 31.9% (182/571)). Based on chart review, several common themes were noted, including placement in patients with altered mental status without hemodynamic instability and in patients with brief transient hemodynamic instability weaned from low dose inotropic medications and ventilation within 24 hours of admission. A proposed clinical decision tool was created to help guide physicians in the reasoning behind the perceived need for catheter placement in the ED ver- sus waiting to evaluate clinical need upon admission based upon patient course and failure of alternative methods for urine output management. Background: Device stewardship is an important infection prevention opportunity for catheter-associated urinary tract infection reduction. This starts with the avoidance of initial catheter placement without defini- tive clinical need, especially with the option of alternative of non-invasive foley devices for both male and female anatomy. Urinary catheter place- ment in the Emergency Department (ED) is an area of opportunity where catheter placement could be avoided in favor of non-invasive management. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the infrequent occurrence of post-vaccination mumps despite widespread MMR vaccination, highlighting a knowledge gap regarding adverse events during a concurrent measles outbreak in Mexico.
Design and population
The study reports a retrospective analysis of mumps cases following MMR vaccination during a measles outbreak in Mexico, focusing on the epidemiology and clinical characteristics of post-vaccination mumps cases.
Core methods
The authors conducted a retrospective review of mumps cases reported in Mexico during the measles outbreak, analyzing demographic, clinical, and vaccination data to identify post-vaccination mumps cases and their outcomes.
Main results
The study identified that post-vaccination mumps occurred infrequently, with cases characterized by typical mumps symptoms and laboratory confirmation, occurring in individuals who had received MMR vaccination prior to exposure during the measles outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that post-vaccination mumps can occur even during high-coverage MMR vaccination programs, emphasizing the need for continued surveillance and investigation into vaccine performance and breakthrough infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of maintaining high MMR vaccination coverage while monitoring for rare adverse events such as post-vaccination mumps, particularly during outbreaks of measles or other vaccine-preventable diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on reported cases, which may underestimate the true incidence of post-vaccination mumps and lack detailed virological or immunological data to confirm vaccine failure.

23 合作预防:公共卫生与学术合作,解决肯塔基州的 麻疹 暴发 问题

23 Partnering for Prevention: A Public Health & Academic Collaboration to Address Measles Outbreaks in Kentucky
