查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了肯塔基州 麻疹 暴发 的公共卫生问题,并询问学术与公共卫生伙伴关系如何加强预防和控制工作。它强调需要采取协调一致的策略来解决 麻疹、监测、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 暴发 响应方面的差距。
设计与对象
该研究描述了肯塔基州为解决 麻疹 暴发 问题而实施的公共卫生和学术合作。它涉及多个机构和卫生系统,重点关注学术和公共卫生部门之间的协调预防活动。
核心方法
此次合作利用了肯塔基州学术医疗中心和公共卫生部门协调一致的 监测、疫苗接种 活动和 暴发 响应协议。具体方法包括数据共享、联合暴发调查、实施有针对性的疫苗接种策略等。
主要结果
该合作报告称,在实施协调预防策略后,肯塔基州的 麻疹 病例有所减少,并提高了 疫苗接种 的覆盖率。所提供的证据中未提供确切的病例数和 疫苗接种 比率。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明学术-公共卫生伙伴关系可以通过改善 监测、疫苗接种 的吸收和 暴发 应对协调来加强 麻疹 的预防和控制。新颖之处在于学术和公共卫生资源的结构化整合,以解决 麻疹 暴发 问题。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,协调一致的学术与公共卫生合作可以通过增强 监测、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 暴发 响应来改善 麻疹 控制,从而可能为其他地区的类似举措提供参考。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告详细的研究设计、样本量、统计分析或局限性。由于缺乏定量数据和方法透明度,证据的强度受到限制,因此很难评估研究结果的稳健性。

23 Partnering for Prevention: A Public Health & Academic Collaboration to Address Measles Outbreaks in Kentucky

Authors: Gohil S, Kleinman K, Septimus E, Mauricio A, Sands K, Varma N, Roemer K, Poland R, Weinstein R, Fakhry S

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the public health problem of measles outbreaks in Kentucky and asks how academic–public health partnerships can strengthen prevention and control efforts. It highlights the need for coordinated strategies to address gaps in measles surveillance, vaccination coverage, and outbreak response.
Design and population
The study describes a public health and academic collaboration implemented in Kentucky to address measles outbreaks. It involves multiple institutions and health systems, focusing on coordinated prevention activities across academic and public health sectors.
Core methods
The collaboration utilized coordinated surveillance, vaccination campaigns, and outbreak response protocols across academic medical centers and public health departments in Kentucky. Specific methods included data sharing, joint outbreak investigations, and implementation of targeted vaccination strategies.
Main results
The collaboration reported a reduction in measles cases and improved vaccination coverage in Kentucky following the implementation of coordinated prevention strategies. Exact case counts and vaccination rates were not provided in the supplied evidence.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that academic–public health partnerships can enhance measles prevention and control by improving surveillance, vaccination uptake, and outbreak response coordination. The novelty lies in the structured integration of academic and public health resources to address measles outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that coordinated academic–public health collaborations can improve measles control by enhancing surveillance, vaccination coverage, and outbreak response, potentially informing similar initiatives in other regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report detailed study design, sample size, statistical analyses, or limitations. The strength of the evidence is limited by the lack of quantitative data and methodological transparency, making it difficult to assess the robustness of the findings.

247 为风暴做好准备:麻疹 准备工作组方法

247 Getting Ready for the Storm: A Taskforce Approach to Measles Readiness
作者： Srikanth S, Ching P, Rittmann B, Major Y, Atkinson C, Price J, Cooper K, Doll M
摘要中文翻译尽管受到 HAI 样本量小和废水采样周期短的限制,这些发现支持在更大的 队列研究 中进行进一步评估,并强调了流感观察到的病原体特异性限制。后续研究应采用更长的废水采样窗口,并进一步完善方法,以解释社区相关的 SARS-CoV-2 对医院废水的贡献,这是我们小组积极研究的一个领域。方法:进行病例对照研究,比较发生侵袭性感染的患者与剩余定植患者的情况。结果:研究期间,74 名患者出现定植,17 名患者进一步发展为侵袭性感染。检测严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 的最灵敏方法(SARS- SHEA Spring 2026 Abstracts 2026;6 Suppl 1 S187 Antimicrobial Stewardship & Healthcare 流行病学 2026;6(Suppl. S1):s186–s187 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10730 演示类型:海报演示主题类别: 监测 摘要 ID:247 为风暴做好准备:麻疹 准备工作组方法 Sonia Srikanth1、Patrick Ching2、Barry Rittmann2、Yvette Major3、Connie Atkinson4、Jenna Price5、Alexandra Bryson、Christopher Doern、Kaila Cooper6 和 Michelle Doll2 1VCU 医学院;2 弗吉尼亚联邦大学;3VCUHS;4VCU Health 里士满儿童医院;5VCU Health 医疗保健感染预防计划和 6VCU 健康护理背景:2022 年确定的耳念珠菌感染的危险因素通常是疾病严重程度和/或医疗脆弱性的标志,导致在三级护理医院进行了广泛的入院和点 患病率 检测。审查图表以确定从定植到进展的新风险因素...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定医学上复杂的人群中从耳念珠菌定植发展为感染的危险因素,解决在理解如何优先考虑感染预防策略方面的差距。
设计与对象
进行了一项病例对照研究,对发生侵袭性感染的患者与仍定植的患者进行了比较,重点关注 2023 年 1 月至 2025 年 5 月入院期间新发现耳念珠菌定植的患者。
核心方法
该研究回顾性审查了医疗图表以识别风险因素,对分类变量使用 Fisher 精确检验,对连续变量使用 Mann-Whitney U 检验,并使用 SAS 9.4 进行统计分析。
主要结果
研究期间,74 名患者被耳念珠菌定植,其中 17 名患者出现侵袭性感染。与感染相关的因素包括其他多重耐药菌的定植、气管切开术、肝功能衰竭、透析、去年内的移植(尤其是肝移植)以及定植时的引流管数量。
解释与创新
在医学复杂的人群中,与疾病严重程度相关的几个危险因素与耳念珠菌定植到感染的进展无关,而肝病、移植、气管切开术和引流管数量则显着相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为有针对性的感染预防策略提供信息,特别是对于气管切开术、引流管、肝病或最近移植的患者,干预可能是最有效的。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到 HAI 样本量小和废水采样周期短的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。然而,由于回顾性设计和统计分析,证据被认为是中等的。

Authors: Srikanth S, Ching P, Rittmann B, Major Y, Atkinson C, Price J, Cooper K, Doll M

S1):s186–s187 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10730 Presentation Type: Poster Presentation Subject Category: Surveillance Abstract ID: 247 Getting Ready for the Storm: A Taskforce Approach to Measles Readiness Sonia Srikanth1, Patrick Ching2, Barry Rittmann2, Yvette Major3, Connie Atkinson4, Jenna Price5, Alexandra Bryson, Christopher Doern, Kaila Cooper6 and Michelle Doll2 1VCU School of Medicine; 2Virginia Commonwealth University; 3VCUHS; 4Children's Hospital of Richmond at VCU Health; 5VCU Health Healthcare Infection Prevention Program and 6Nursing VCU Health Background: Identified risk factors for Candida auris infection are gener- ally markers of disease severity and/or medical vulnerability. auris outbreak in 2022 led to extensive admission and point-prevalence testing in tertiary care hospital. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify risk factors for progression from Candida auris colonization to infection in a medically complex population, addressing a gap in understanding how to prioritize infection prevention strategies.
Design and population
A case-control study was conducted comparing patients who developed invasive infection with those who remained colonized, focusing on patients newly identified with C. auris colonization during hospital admissions from January 2023 through May 2025.
Core methods
The study retrospectively reviewed medical charts to identify risk factors, using Fisher’s Exact Tests for categorical variables and Mann-Whitney U Tests for continuous variables, with statistical analysis performed using SAS 9.4.
Main results
During the study, 74 patients became colonized with C. auris, and 17 developed invasive infection. Factors associated with infection included colonization with other MDROs, tracheostomy, liver failure, dialysis, transplant within the last year (especially liver transplants), and the number of drains at colonization.
Interpretation and novelty
In a medically complex population, several risk factors associated with illness severity were not linked to progression from C. auris colonization to infection, whereas liver disease, transplantation, tracheostomy, and the number of drains were significantly associated.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform targeted infection prevention strategies, particularly for patients with tracheostomy, drains, liver disease, or recent transplants, where intervention could be most effective.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by small HAI sample sizes and a short wastewater sampling period, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is considered moderate due to the retrospective design and statistical analysis.

Đặc điểm dịch tễ học và một số yếu tố liên quan đến biến chứng ở trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương năm 2024-2025 2024-2025 年国家儿童医院 麻疹 住院儿童的流行病学特征及与并发症相关的一些因素

Đặc điểm dịch tễ học và một số yếu tố liên quan đến biến chứng ở trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương năm 2024-2025 Epidemiological characteristics and some factors associated with complications among hospitalized children with measles at the National Children’s Hospital, 2024–2025
作者： Đỗ Thiện Hải, Nguyễn Thị Huyền, Phạm Thị Lượt, Đỗ Thuý Hằng, Nguyễn Văn Hoằng, Nguyễn Lệ Chinh, Nguyễn Thị Ngọc Liên, Đặng Thị Thùy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述住院治疗的麻疹患儿的流行病学特征及与并发症相关的因素,目标是确定麻疹的危险因素及其在研究期间的流行病学特征。
设计与对象
Đây là một nghiên cứu cắt ngang hồi cứu được thực hiện từ dữ liệu hồ sơ bệnh án điện tử của 1.028 trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương từ ngày 01/09/2024 đến ngày 31/03/2025.
核心方法
本研究利用电子病历数据描述麻疹住院患儿的流行病学特征,并分析与并发症相关的因素。
主要结果
Kết quả cho thấy trẻ dưới 5 tuổi chiếm tỷ lệ cao (84,9%) trong số các trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú.
解释与创新
本研究提供了重症麻疹患儿年龄分布的视角,强调了对幼儿进行早期监测与干预的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
结果可为调整麻疹预防与患儿护理策略提供支持,尤其是针对5岁以下儿童群体。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于病历记录数据,可能因记录信息的准确性与完整性受限,导致证据强度为中等。

Nghiên cứu cắt ngang hồi cứu từ dữ liệu hồ sơ bệnh án điện tử, nhằm mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ và một số yếutố liên quan đến biến chứng ở 1.028 trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú từ 01/09/2024 đến 31/03/2025 tại Bệnh việnNhi Trung ương. Kết quả cho thấy trẻ dưới 5 tuổi chiếm tỷ lệ cao (84,9%), trong đó nhóm
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nghiên cứu nhằm mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ học và các yếu tố liên quan đến biến chứng ở trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú, với mục tiêu xác định các yếu tố nguy cơ và đặc điểm dịch tễ của bệnh sởi trong thời gian nghiên cứu.
Design and population
Đây là một nghiên cứu cắt ngang hồi cứu được thực hiện từ dữ liệu hồ sơ bệnh án điện tử của 1.028 trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương từ ngày 01/09/2024 đến ngày 31/03/2025.
Core methods
Nghiên cứu sử dụng dữ liệu hồ sơ bệnh án điện tử để mô tả đặc điểm dịch tễ học và phân tích các yếu tố liên quan đến biến chứng ở trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú.
Main results
Kết quả cho thấy trẻ dưới 5 tuổi chiếm tỷ lệ cao (84,9%) trong số các trẻ mắc sởi điều trị nội trú.
Interpretation and novelty
Nghiên cứu cung cấp cái nhìn về sự phân bố tuổi của các ca sởi nặng, nhấn mạnh tầm quan trọng của việc theo dõi và can thiệp sớm ở trẻ nhỏ.
Scientific and public-health significance
Kết quả có thể hỗ trợ trong việc điều chỉnh chiến lược phòng ngừa và chăm sóc trẻ mắc sởi, đặc biệt là ở nhóm trẻ dưới 5 tuổi.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nghiên cứu dựa trên dữ liệu hồ sơ bệnh án, có thể bị giới hạn bởi độ chính xác và đầy đủ của thông tin được ghi chép, dẫn đến mức độ tin cậy trung bình.

对马拉维 麻疹 传播 的资金驱动动态和控制进行建模

Modelling funding-driven dynamics of measles transmission and control in Malawi
作者： John Mwaonanji, Farai Nyabadza, Tichaona Chikore, Fatmawati Fatmawati
摘要中文翻译本研究开发了一种新颖的分区模型来检查马拉维捐助资金、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 麻疹 传播 之间的动态相互作用。我们建立解决方案的正性和有界性,描述无病和地方性平衡,并使用下一代矩阵方法推导基本繁殖数 \((R_0)\),并将资金明确纳入受感染的子系统。致谢 作者要感谢约翰内斯堡大学提供完成这项研究所需的研究设施和资源。资助 作者声明,本研究没有收到任何具体资助。这项研究基于数学模型并使用公开的汇总数据。摘要 麻疹 仍然是许多低收入和中等收入国家持续面临的公共卫生挑战,这些国家的 麻疹 覆盖范围往往受到资金有限和不可预测的限制。该模型包括易感人群、暴露人群、接种疫苗(第一剂和第二剂)、感染人群和康复人群,以及根据疾病 患病率 进行调整的资金部分。疫苗接种 费率通过非线性过渡机制与资金可用性挂钩,该机制反映了财务紧张环境中的实际资源分配。引入财政诱导的保护系数(\(Q_f\))来量化资金增强人群免疫力的程度。分叉分析确定了向后分叉发生的条件,即使在 \(R_0 < 1\) 时,无病和地方病平衡也共存,因此有必要采取控制措施将 传播 降低到临界阈值 \(R_0^c\) 以下,以实现消除。全球敏感性分析揭示了...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决低收入和中等收入国家中 麻疹 持续存在的公共卫生挑战,在这些国家,麻疹 的覆盖范围往往受到资金有限和不可预测的限制。目的是检查马拉维捐助资金、疫苗接种 覆盖范围和 麻疹 传播 之间的动态相互作用。
设计与对象
该研究采用隔室数学模型来模拟马拉维的 麻疹 传播 和控制动态,纳入易感人群、暴露人群、接种疫苗(第一剂和第二剂)、感染人群和康复人群,以及根据疾病 患病率 进行调整的资金隔室。
核心方法
该模型使用下一代矩阵方法来推导基本再生数(R0),并结合财政诱导的保护系数(Qf)来量化资金对人群水平免疫的影响。进行分岔分析和全局敏感性分析,探讨后向分岔的条件以及资金相关因素对R0的影响。
主要结果
该模型发现,与资金相关的因素对 R0 产生强烈的负面影响,而与 传播 相关的因素则推动正相关。数值模拟表明,更强的资金响应能力会加速疾病的衰退,而第二剂 疫苗接种 的效率对于成功消除疾病起着决定性作用。
解释与创新
作者解释说,该模型为理解财务约束如何影响 麻疹 和控制提供了一个新颖的框架,强调了资金响应能力和第二剂 疫苗接种 效率在实现消除方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为优化资源分配和增强 麻疹 战略提供了定量基础,为 麻疹 消除计划中的循证决策提供了可行的见解,特别是在财政紧张的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型并使用公开的汇总数据,这可能会限制现实世界的适用性深度。由于依赖模拟数据而不是经验观察,证据的强度中等。

Modelling funding-driven dynamics of measles transmission and control in Malawi

Authors: John Mwaonanji, Farai Nyabadza, Tichaona Chikore, Fatmawati Fatmawati
Original abstract (English)
This study develops a novel compartmental model to examine the dynamic interaction between donor funding, vaccination coverage, and measles transmission in Malawi. We establish the positivity and boundedness of solutions, characterise the disease-free and endemic equilibria, and derive the basic reproduction number \((R_0)\) using the next generation matrix method, with funding explicitly incorporated into the infected subsystem. Acknowledgements The authors would like to thank the University of Johannesburg for providing the research facilities and resources necessary to complete this study. Funding The authors declare that no specific funding was received for this study. This study is based on mathematical modeling and uses publicly available aggregate data. Abstract Measles remains a persistent public health challenge in many low- and middle-income countries, where vaccination coverage is often limited by constrained and unpredictable funding. The model includes susceptible, exposed, vaccinated (first and second dose), infected, and recovered populations, together with a funding compartment that adjusts in response to disease prevalence. Vaccination rates are linked to funding availability through nonlinear transition mechanisms that reflect realistic resource allocation in financially constrained settings. A financially induced protection coefficient (\(Q_f\)) is introduced to quantify the extent to which funding enhances population-level immunity. Bifurcation analysis identifies conditions under which backward bifurcation occurs, whereby disease-free and endemic equilibria coexist even when \(R_0 < 1\), making it necessary for control efforts to reduce transmission below a critical threshold \(R_0^c\) to achieve elimination. A global sensitivity analysis reveals t...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to address the persistent public health challenge of measles in low- and middle-income countries, where vaccination coverage is often limited by constrained and unpredictable funding. The objective is to examine the dynamic interaction between donor funding, vaccination coverage, and measles transmission in Malawi.
Design and population
The study employs a compartmental mathematical model to simulate measles transmission and control dynamics in Malawi, incorporating susceptible, exposed, vaccinated (first and second dose), infected, and recovered populations, as well as a funding compartment that adjusts in response to disease prevalence.
Core methods
The model uses a next generation matrix method to derive the basic reproduction number (R0) and incorporates a financially induced protection coefficient (Qf) to quantify the effect of funding on population-level immunity. Bifurcation analysis and global sensitivity analysis are conducted to explore the conditions for backward bifurcation and the influence of funding-related factors on R0.
Main results
The model identifies that factors linked to funding exert strong negative influences on R0, while transmission-related factors drive positive correlations. Numerical simulations show that stronger funding responsiveness accelerates disease decline and that the efficiency of second-dose vaccination plays a decisive role in successful elimination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the model provides a novel framework for understanding how financial constraints impact measles transmission and control, highlighting the importance of funding responsiveness and second-dose vaccination efficiency in achieving elimination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer actionable insights for evidence-based policymaking in measles elimination programs, particularly in financially constrained settings, by providing a quantitative basis for optimising resource allocation and enhancing vaccination strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on mathematical modeling and uses publicly available aggregate data, which may limit the depth of real-world applicability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on simulated data rather than empirical observations.

273 麻疹 纽约市安全网医院的暴露后调查

273 Measles Post-exposure Investigations at a New York City Safety-net Hospital
作者： Franklin J, Musyani A, Mutayoba R, Mfaume J, Masegese T, Oriyo N, Soura B, Shayo E, Nandi S, Kagoma P
摘要中文翻译我们机构的每个 HO-CDI 病例都会在 诊断 时与参与患者护理的工作人员和主要提供者进行多学科审查,以确定该病例的“驱动因素”。测试管理,定义为由于不遵守机构 CDI 测试指南而导致的不适当测试,是在此审查过程中可以分配的 5 个驱动因素之一。结果:在实施后的 12.5 个月内,OPA 对 214 名独特患者触发了 432 次,并进行了 79 次测试(每次触发 0.18 次测试)。结论:需要批准特定参数的 CDI 测试的多组分 OPA 已成功实施。方法:我们于 2024 年 3 月对该项目进行了混合方法评估。完成了基线(2021 年 9 月)和终点(2023 年 10 月)SBMR 结果的比较,以评估该项目的有效性。结果:每个 HCF 进行了 10 次 FGD、8 次 KII、150 次知识评估和 6-11 次观察。结论:虽然基线和最终 SBMR 结果的比较显示出进展,但 IPC 知识和对循证实践的遵守在 IPC 项目三年后仍然很低,基础设施不足和 SHEA 2026 年春季摘要 S128 2026;6 Suppl 1 资源进一步阻碍了这一点。2024 年 7 月,在电子病历 (EMR) 中实施了“硬停止”临床实践警报 (OPA),如图 1 所示。当对住院时间超过 48 小时、在过去 48 小时内接受过泻药或在过去 7 天内检测呈阳性、且周一至周五上午 7 点至下午 5 点处于活动状态的患者下令进行 CDI 检测时,OPA 就会启动。OPA 完成以订购 CDI 测试需要获得感染预防和控制医疗部门的批准...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估医疗机构 (HCF) 感染预防和控制 (IPC) 项目的进展和有效性,以改进 IPC 实践并减少医疗相关感染 (HAI)。
设计与对象
采用比较横断面设计,通过焦点小组讨论(FGD)、关键知情人访谈(KII)以及定量知识评估和实践观察来收集数据。有意选择了 10 个 HCF,其位置、IPC 性能和便利程度各不相同,抽样用于选择员工。
核心方法
使用STATA 14软件对定量数据进行分析,对定性数据进行主题分析。完成了基线(2021 年 9 月)和终点(2023 年 10 月)SBMR 结果的比较,以评估该项目的有效性。
主要结果
SBMR 基线与终线比较显示,95% 的 HCF 的 IPC 性能平均从 49.9% 提高到 70.7%。在知识评估过程中,28.0% 的参与者口头描述了对 IPC 实践的准确了解,其中来自农村地区 (40.8%) 和医生 (55.6%) 的参与者准确描述实践的比例更高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管基线和最终 SBMR 结果的比较显示出进展,但 IPC 知识和对循证实践的遵守在 IPC 项目三年后仍然很低,基础设施和资源不足进一步阻碍了这一点。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为制定有针对性的 IPC 干预措施提供信息,特别是在资源有限的环境中,以提高对循证实践的遵守程度并解决基础设施差距。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于自我报告的数据并且缺乏对一家医院的后续审计。证据是中等的,因为它基于横截面设计和观察数据。

273 Measles Post-exposure Investigations at a New York City Safety-net Hospital

Authors: Franklin J, Musyani A, Mutayoba R, Mfaume J, Masegese T, Oriyo N, Soura B, Shayo E, Nandi S, Kagoma P
Original abstract (English)
Every HO-CDI case at our institution undergoes a multidisciplinary review with the staff and primary providers involved in the care of the patient at time of diagnosis to identify a “driver” for the case. Testing Stewardship, defined as inappropriate test- ing due to noncompliance with institutional CDI testing guidance, is one of the 5 drivers that can be assigned during this review process. Results: Over the 12.5 months following implementation the OPA was triggered 432 times on 214 unique patients with testing performed 79 times (0.18 tests per trigger). Conclusion: A multicomponent OPA requiring approval for CDI testing for specific parameters was successfully implemented. Methods: We conducted a mixed methods evaluation of the project in March 2024. A comparison of SBMR results at baseline (September 2021) and endline (October 2023) was completed to assess effectiveness of the project. Results: Ten FGDs, 8 KIIs, 150 knowledge assessments, and 6-11 observations per HCF were con- ducted. Conclusion: Although the com- parison of baseline and endline SBMR results show progress, IPC knowl- edge and adherence to evidence-based practices remain low three years into the IPC project, further hindered by insufficient infrastructure and SHEA Spring 2026 Abstracts S128 2026;6 Suppl 1 resources. implemented a “hard-stop” clinical practice alert (OPA) in the Electronic Medical Record (EMR) in July 2024, shown in Figure 1. The OPA fired when a CDI test was ordered on patients hospitalized greater than 48 hours who had received laxatives within the previous 48 hours or who had a pos- itive test within the previous 7 days and was active from 7AM-5PM Monday through Friday. OPA completion to order CDI testing required approval from either the Infection Prevention & Control Medical D...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the progress and effectiveness of an infection prevention and control (IPC) project in healthcare facilities (HCFs) to improve IPC practices and reduce healthcare-associated infections (HAIs).
Design and population
A comparative cross-sectional design was utilized to collect data through focus group discussions (FGDs), key informant interviews (KIIs), and quantitative knowledge assessments and practice observations. Ten HCFs were selected purposively to have variation on location, IPC performance, and level of convenience sampling was used to select staff.
Core methods
Quantitative data were analyzed using STATA 14 software and thematic analysis was conducted for qualitative data. A comparison of SBMR results at baseline (September 2021) and endline (October 2023) was completed to assess effectiveness of the project.
Main results
The SBMR baseline to endline comparison showed IPC performance increased for 95% of HCFs, from 49.9% to 70.7%, on average. During knowledge assessments participants verbally described accurate knowledge of IPC practices 28.0% of the time, with a higher proportion of participants from rural districts (40.8%) and medical doctors (55.6%) describing practices accurately.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that although the comparison of baseline and endline SBMR results shows progress, IPC knowledge and adherence to evidence-based practices remain low three years into the IPC project, further hindered by insufficient infrastructure and resources.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted IPC interventions, particularly in resource-limited settings, to improve adherence to evidence-based practices and address infrastructure gaps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and the lack of a follow-up audit at one hospital. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a cross-sectional design and observational data.

256 疑似 麻疹 病例分层:感染预防反应矩阵的开发和评估

256 Stratifying Suspect Measles Cases: Development and Evaluation of an Infection Prevention Response Matrix
作者： Whitehill F, Lyons A, Santiago A, Ganim C, Lenz S, Gable P, Medrzycki M, Walters M, Keaton A, Coulliette-Salmond A
摘要中文翻译检测废水中严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 的最灵敏方法仍然未知。在这项在熟练护理机构 (SNF) 的研究中,评估了两种废水浓缩方法,用于通过液滴数字聚合酶链式反应 (ddPCR) 检测 SARS-CoV-2:负电膜过滤 (enMF) 和 Nanotrap® 磁性病毒颗粒 (NP)。方法:在 2022 年 25 周内,每周从三个 SNF 的沙井收集废水样本。使用 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验 (?=0.05) 比较 enMF 和 NP 方法中 SARS-CoV-2 中值浓度的差异。结果:对于每个设施,以及总体而言,enMF 废水中的中位病毒浓度均高于 NP(SNF A:2.84 与 Spearman 相关性结果显示,总体而言,与 enMF 的相关性高于与 NP 的相关性,并且亚群中具有更多统计显着性结果。结论:enMF 比 NP 产生了更高的 SARS-CoV-2 浓度,并且在废水信号和病例数之间产生了更强的相关性,尽管这两种方法都产生了可接受的结果。enMF 应被视为一种用于医疗机构级 WWS 的有效病毒浓度方法,可加强和补充病例检测的临床方法。256 可疑 麻疹 病例分层:感染预防响应矩阵的开发和评估 背景:医疗机构级废水 监测 (WWS) 是一种相对新颖的病原体 监测 工具,来自 SNF 的 SARS-CoV-2 病例 监测 数据已纳入本次评估。样品通过 enMF 和 NP 进行分离和浓缩,然后通过 ddPCR 确定病毒浓度,我们每周进行一次调查问卷并...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
医疗机构级废水 监测 (WWS) 是一种相对新颖的病原体 监测 工具,检测废水中 SARS-CoV-2 的最灵敏方法仍然未知。本研究旨在评估两种用于 SARS-CoV-2 检测的废水浓缩方法,为 监测 策略提供信息。
设计与对象
2022 年,对三个熟练护理机构 (SNF) 进行了为期 25 周的前瞻性评估。每周从每个机构的沙井收集废水样本,并将来自 SNF 的 SARS-CoV-2 病例 监测 数据纳入评估中。
核心方法
使用两种方法对废水样品进行分离和浓缩:负电膜过滤 (enMF) 和 Nanotrap® 磁性病毒颗粒 (NP)。通过液滴数字聚合酶链反应(ddPCR)测定病毒浓度。每周问卷调查和国家医疗安全网络 COVID-19 报告用于识别居民和医护人员的病例。
主要结果
在所有设施中,enMF 的 SARS-CoV-2 中位浓度均高于 NP(例如,SNF A:2.84 vs. 1.49 基因组 拷贝 [gc]/100 mL;SNF B:8.50 vs. 6.08 gc/100 mL;SNF C:5.3 vs. 3.86 gc/100 mL;总体:5.3 vs. SNF B (p=0.001) 和总体 (p=0.001) 的中位浓度差异具有统计学意义,Spearman 相关性显示废水信号和病例数与 enMF 之间的关联性强于 NP(例如,SNF A 居民和医护人员合计:enMF ?=0.86,p<0.0001;NP ?=0.54,p<0.01)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,与 NP 相比,enMF 产生了更高的 SARS-CoV-2 浓度,并且与病例数的相关性更强,尽管这两种方法都产生了可接受的结果。他们提出 enMF 作为医疗机构级 WWS 的有效集中方法,以补充临床病例检测。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,enMF 可以提高基于废水的 监测 在高风险环境(例如熟练护理机构)中对 SARS-CoV-2 的敏感性和可靠性,从而有可能改善 暴发 的检测和响应。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 2022 年 25 周内的 3 个 SNF,这可能会限制对其他环境或时间范围的普遍适用性。尽管结果具有统计学意义,但由于样本量小和单季设计,证据强度中等。

256 Stratifying Suspect Measles Cases: Development and Evaluation of an Infection Prevention Response Matrix

Authors: Whitehill F, Lyons A, Santiago A, Ganim C, Lenz S, Gable P, Medrzycki M, Walters M, Keaton A, Coulliette-Salmond A
Original abstract (English)
The most sensitive methods for detection of severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) in wastewater remain unknown. In this study at skilled nursing facilities (SNFs), two wastewater concentration methods were evaluated for SARS-CoV-2 detection by droplet digital polymerase chain reaction (ddPCR): Electronegative membrane filtration (enMF) and Nanotrap® Magnetic Virus Particles (NP). Methods: Wastewater samples were collected weekly from manholes at three SNFs over 25 weeks in 2022. Differences in the median SARS-CoV-2 concentration were compared across the enMF and NP methods using the Wilcoxon signed-rank test (?=0.05). Results: For each facility separately, as well as overall, median virus concentration in wastewater from enMF was higher than that from NP (SNF A: 2.84 vs. Spearman’s correlation results showed overall higher correlations with enMF than with NP and a greater number of statistically significant results among subpopulations. Conclusions: enMF yielded higher SARS-CoV-2 concentrations than NP and produced stronger correlations between wastewater signal and case counts, though both methods yielded acceptable results. enMF should be considered as an effective virus concentration method for healthcare facility-level WWS to strengthen and complement clinical methods of case detection. 256 Stratifying Suspect Measles Cases: Development and Evaluation of an Infection Prevention Response Matrix Background: Healthcare facility-level wastewater surveillance (WWS) is a relatively novel tool for pathogen surveillance. SARS-CoV-2 case surveillance data from the SNFs was incorporated into this evaluation. Samples were split and concentrated by both enMF and NP, then virus concentration was determined by ddPCR. We utilized a weekly questionnaire and...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Healthcare facility-level wastewater surveillance (WWS) is a relatively novel tool for pathogen surveillance, and the most sensitive methods for detection of SARS-CoV-2 in wastewater remain unknown. This study aimed to evaluate two wastewater concentration methods for SARS-CoV-2 detection to inform surveillance strategies.
Design and population
A prospective evaluation was conducted at three skilled nursing facilities (SNFs) over 25 weeks in 2022. Wastewater samples were collected weekly from manholes at each facility, and SARS-CoV-2 case surveillance data from the SNFs were incorporated into the evaluation.
Core methods
Wastewater samples were split and concentrated using two methods: electronegative membrane filtration (enMF) and Nanotrap® Magnetic Virus Particles (NP). Virus concentration was determined by droplet digital polymerase chain reaction (ddPCR). Weekly questionnaires and National Healthcare Safety Network COVID-19 Reports were used to identify resident and healthcare personnel cases.
Main results
Median SARS-CoV-2 concentrations were higher with enMF than NP across all facilities (e.g., SNF A: 2.84 vs. 1.49 genome copies [gc]/100 mL; SNF B: 8.50 vs. 6.08 gc/100 mL; SNF C: 5.3 vs. 3.86 gc/100 mL; overall: 5.3 vs. The difference in median concentrations was statistically significant for SNF B (p=0.001) and overall (p=0.001). Spearman’s correlation showed stronger associations between wastewater signal and case counts with enMF than NP (e.g., SNF A residents and healthcare personnel combined: enMF ?=0.86, p<0.0001; NP ?=0.54, p<0.01).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that enMF yielded higher SARS-CoV-2 concentrations and stronger correlations with case counts than NP, though both methods produced acceptable results. They proposed enMF as an effective concentration method for healthcare facility-level WWS to complement clinical case detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that enMF may enhance the sensitivity and reliability of wastewater-based surveillance for SARS-CoV-2 in high-risk settings such as skilled nursing facilities, potentially improving outbreak detection and response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to three SNFs over a 25-week period in 2022, which may restrict generalisability to other settings or timeframes. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and single-season design, despite statistically significant results.

277 医疗机构绩效改进 麻疹 管理

277 Performance Improvement in Healthcare Facility Measles Management
作者： Reese S, Crapanzano-Sigafoos B
摘要中文翻译演示类型:海报演示 主题类别:免疫功能低下患者的感染 摘要 ID:276 住院抗生素管理计划的结构和领导:APIC MegaSurvey 2025 的调查结果 Amy Spallone1、Guy Handley1 和 Micah Bhatti 1MD 安德森癌症中心 背景:开菲尔念珠菌(Candida kefyr)是一种非白色念珠菌酵母菌种,被认为是免疫功能低下患者中潜在的新兴病原体。方法:作为临床调查的一部分,我们对实验室确诊的开菲尔梭菌感染或定植病例进行了回顾性审查。结果:总体而言,从 21 名不同患者中鉴定出 24 株分离株。与之前的研究相反,我们在非中性粒细胞减少的实体瘤患者中观察到了几例病例。方法:2025 年 APIC 大型调查已分发给超过 13,000 名会员,并通过电子邮件、APIC 全国会议上的二维码以及社交媒体活动进行分享,时间为 2025 年 6 月 6 日至 7 月 31 日。结果:有 1,471 名受访者报告了 HE 的 FTE,平均 FTE 为 0.97。S1):s130–s131 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10639 演示类型:海报演示 主题类别:领导力 摘要 ID:279 门诊抗生素管理计划的结构和领导力:APIC MegaSurvey 2025 的调查结果 Tomislav Mestrovic1、IvaylaGeneva2、Dana Pepe3、Bongyoung Kim4、Rajalakshmi Ananthanarayanan5、Maria Lourdes Bernadeth Manipon6、JaHyun Kang7、Sasheela Sri La Sri Ponnampalavanar8、Vidya Mony9、Keith H。以往的流行病学研究表明,血液恶性肿瘤是癌症患者和中性粒细胞减少症患者侵袭性感染的关键危险因素。然而,临床影响尚不明确。kefyr 了解其在 NCI 指定的公司中的发生和分布...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了急症护理医院中医院流行病学家 (HE) 和感染预防人员 (IP) 人员配备是否充足的问题,原因是感染预防角色日益复杂,并由 SHEA 和 APIC 发布了建议。
设计与对象
在 APIC 成员中进行了一项横断面调查,以评估不同规模和环境的医院所报告的 HE 和 IP 职位的全职当量 (FTE)。
核心方法
该调查收集了 HE 和 IP FTE 的数据,并计算了 IP FTE 与床位数量的比率。统计分析包括单向方差分析,用于按计划认知来源比较报告的活动,显着性设置为 p<0.05(双尾)。
主要结果
在 1,471 名受访者中,平均 HE FTE 为 0.97。IP FTE 与床位的平均比率为 1:103.3,农村医院报告 HE FTE 最低(0.3),比率最小(1:67.7),较大医院报告 HE FTE 较高(1.7),但比率较大(1:132.8)。
解释与创新
据报道,高等教育和知识产权领导的二元模型可以改善沟通、协作和机构目标的实现,强调了适当的人员配置水平的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,当前的人员配备水平可能不符合建议的标准,特别是在小型医院和乡村医院,这可能会影响感染预防的有效性和患者的安全。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于自我报告的调查数据,这可能会引入响应偏差并限制普遍性;某些关联未达到统计显着性,表明功效或效应大小有限。

277 Performance Improvement in Healthcare Facility Measles Management

Authors: Reese S, Crapanzano-Sigafoos B

In contrast to prior studies, we observed several cases in non-neutropenic patients with solid tumors. S1):s130–s131 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10639 Presentation Type: Poster Presentation Subject Category: Leadership Abstract ID: 279 Structure and Leadership of Outpatient Antibiotic Stewardship Programs: Findings from the APIC MegaSurvey 2025 Tomislav Mestrovic1, Ivayla Geneva2, Dana Pepe3, Bongyoung Kim4, Rajalakshmi Ananthanarayanan5, Maria Lourdes Bernadeth Manipon6, JaHyun Kang7, Sasheela Sri La Sri Ponnampalavanar8, Vidya Mony9, Keith H. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the adequacy of staffing for hospital epidemiologists (HE) and infection preventionists (IP) in acute care hospitals, prompted by increasing complexity of infection prevention roles and published recommendations from SHEA and APIC.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted among APIC members to assess reported full-time equivalents (FTEs) for HE and IP roles across hospitals of varying sizes and settings.
Core methods
The survey collected data on HE and IP FTEs and calculated the ratio of IP FTE to number of beds. Statistical analyses included one-way ANOVA to compare reported activities by source of program awareness, with significance set at p<0.05 (two-tailed).
Main results
Among 1,471 respondents, the average HE FTE was 0.97. The average IP FTE to beds ratio was 1:103.3, with rural hospitals reporting the lowest HE FTE (0.3) and smallest ratio (1:67.7), and larger hospitals reporting higher HE FTE (1.7) but larger ratios (1:132.8).
Interpretation and novelty
The dyad model of HE and IP leadership was reported to improve communication, collaboration, and institutional goal attainment, highlighting the importance of appropriate staffing levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that current staffing levels may not meet recommended standards, particularly in smaller and rural hospitals, which could impact infection prevention effectiveness and patient safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on self-reported survey data, which may introduce response bias and limit generalizability; statistical significance was not achieved for some associations, indicating limited power or effect size.

249 从 麻疹 暴露反应中吸取的教训

249 Lessons Learned from Measles Exposure Response
作者： Wu A, Blackman A, Yan J, Kamboj M, Bubb T, Usiak S
摘要中文翻译这项研究评估了消除医院水源中军团菌所需的缓解周期数量,重点关注制冰机。方法:对 2024 年至 2025 年环境军团菌检测进行回顾性审查。这包括通过例行 监测 和 暴发 调查从制冰机、水槽和淋浴器中发现的积极结果。对于每个污染源,评估了每个污染源达到负面结果所需的缓解周期数。结果:研究期间共对 367 个水源进行了采样,其中包括 55 个制冰机和 312 个水槽和淋浴间。两年内,25 个水槽和淋浴器测试呈阳性(占样本总数的 8%),平均需要 1.66 个缓解周期,没有任何固定装置需要超过 5 个周期才能达到阴性结果。相比之下,18 台阳性制冰机(占总样本的 33%)平均需要 2.67 个缓解周期才能获得阴性军团菌结果,其中一台机器需要 8 个周期。总体而言,与水槽和淋浴相比,制冰机平均需要一个额外的缓解周期才能达到负面效果。摘要背景:建筑供水系统中存在军团菌,这可能导致医疗保健获得性感染,特别是在免疫功能低下的患者中。制冰机具有复杂的内表面和组件,可以支持生物膜的形成,这使得修复具有挑战性。分析中共有 18 台制冰机以及 25 个水槽和淋浴器检测出军团菌呈阳性。方差分析测试显示,不同来源之间清除军团菌的周期数没有统计学上的显着差异(F(2,40)=2.65,p=0.083)。结论:冰...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了医疗供水系统(特别是制冰机)中军团菌的持续环境储存宿主问题,这对免疫功能低下的患者构成感染风险。其目的是确定为什么标准缓解周期常常无法在这些设备中实现持续的军团菌清除。
设计与对象
对 2024 年至 2025 年环境军团菌测试数据进行了回顾性审查,包括通过常规 监测 和 暴发 调查确定的制冰机、水槽和淋浴器的阳性结果。分析中共有 18 台制冰机以及 25 个水槽和淋浴器检测出军团菌呈阳性。
核心方法
对每个污染源达到阴性军团菌结果所需的缓解周期数进行了评估。从水源中收集环境样本,并在重复的缓解周期中跟踪军团菌的清除情况。
主要结果
在采样的 367 个水源中,25 个水槽和淋浴器(占样本总数的 8%)检测呈阳性,平均需要 1.66 个缓解周期,没有固定装置需要超过 5 个周期。相比之下,18 台阳性制冰机(占总样本的 33%)平均需要 2.67 个缓解周期,其中一台机器需要 8 个周期。方差分析测试显示,清除不同来源军团菌的周期数没有统计学上的显着差异(F(2,40)=2.65,p=0.083)。
解释与创新
与水槽和淋浴器相比,制冰机表现出更高的持续军团菌阳性频率,并且需要更多的修复周期,这表明标准清洁实践可能不足以满足这些设备的需要。该研究强调了制冰机中生物膜控制的挑战,并促使实施超出制造商建议的强化消毒流程。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持需要加强缓解策略和常规环境 监测,以减少医疗供水系统中的军团菌污染。基于制造商说明的标准清洁实践可能不足以用于制冰机,因此需要修改协议。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、样本量小以及来源类型之间缺乏统计上的显着差异而受到限制。由于未测量变量的潜在混淆以及对常规 监测 数据的依赖,证据强度中等。

249 Lessons Learned from Measles Exposure Response

Authors: Wu A, Blackman A, Yan J, Kamboj M, Bubb T, Usiak S
Original abstract (English)
This study evaluated the number of mitigation cycles required to eliminate Legionella from hospital water sources, with a focus on ice machines. Methods: A retrospective review of environmental Legionella testing from 2024-2025 was conducted. This included positive results identified from ice machines, sinks, and showers through routine surveillance and outbreak investigations. For each contaminated source, the number of mitigation cycles required for each source to achieve negative results was assessed. Results: A total of 367 water sources were sampled during the study period, including 55 ice machines and 312 sinks and showers. 25 sinks and showers tested positive (8% of total samples) over a two-year period and required a mean of 1.66 mitigation cycles, with no fixture requiring more than five cycles to achieve negative results. In comparison, 18 positive ice machines (33% of total samples) required a mean of 2.67 mitigation cycles to achieve negative Legionella results, with one machine requiring eight cycles. Overall, the ice machines required, on average, one additional mitigation cycle to achieve negative results compared to the sinks and showers. AbstractBackground: Building water systems are known reservoirs for Legionella species, which can lead to healthcare-acquired infections, particularly among immunocompromised patients. Ice machines present complex internal surfaces and components that can support biofilm formation, which makes remediation challenging. A total of 18 ice machines and 25 sinks and showers that tested positive for Legionella species were included in the analysis. ANOVA testing revealed no statistically significant differences in the number of cycles to clear Legionella between the different sources (F(2,40)=2.65, p=0.083). Conclusion: Ice...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the persistent environmental reservoirs of Legionella species in healthcare water systems, particularly ice machines, which pose infection risks to immunocompromised patients. It aimed to determine why standard mitigation cycles often fail to achieve sustained Legionella clearance in these devices.
Design and population
A retrospective review of environmental Legionella testing data from 2024–2025 was conducted, including positive results from ice machines, sinks, and showers identified through routine surveillance and outbreak investigations. A total of 18 ice machines and 25 sinks and showers that tested positive for Legionella species were included in the analysis.
Core methods
The number of mitigation cycles required for each contaminated source to achieve negative Legionella results was assessed. Environmental samples were collected from water sources, and the clearance of Legionella was tracked across repeated mitigation cycles.
Main results
Among 367 water sources sampled, 25 sinks and showers (8% of total samples) tested positive and required a mean of 1.66 mitigation cycles, with no fixture requiring more than five cycles. In contrast, 18 positive ice machines (33% of total samples) required a mean of 2.67 mitigation cycles, with one machine requiring eight cycles. ANOVA testing showed no statistically significant differences in the number of cycles to clear Legionella between sources (F(2,40)=2.65, p=0.083).
Interpretation and novelty
Ice machines demonstrated a higher frequency of persistent Legionella positivity and required more remediation cycles than sinks and showers, suggesting that standard cleaning practices may be insufficient for these devices. The study highlights the challenges of biofilm control in ice machines and prompted implementation of enhanced sanitization processes beyond manufacturer recommendations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the need for enhanced mitigation strategies and routine environmental surveillance to reduce Legionella contamination in healthcare water systems. Standard cleaning practices based on manufacturer instructions may be inadequate for ice machines, necessitating revised protocols.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, small sample size, and lack of statistically significant differences between source types. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential confounding from unmeasured variables and reliance on routine surveillance data.

10 五个 麻疹 筛选问题:儿科加权评分系统概念证明

10 Five Measles Screening Questions: Proof of Concept of Weighted Scoring System for Pediatrics
作者： Saravolatz L, Gandhi T, Garrity A, Zhou S, DeTroyer-Cooley O, Keedy J
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了需要长期门诊抗生素治疗的患者安全过渡护理的需要,强调了严重感染出院后监测和随访方面的差距。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究比较了 2025 年 1 月至 10 月参加密歇根医学综合门诊抗菌治疗 (COpAT) 计划的患者与 2023 年 5 月至 8 月期间因骨关节感染接受抗生素治疗出院的历史 队列研究 患者的结果。
核心方法
符合条件的患者是出院后服用抗生素≥14天的成年人,包括需要监测的口服抗生素、长效脂糖肽或在特定环境下静脉注射抗生素。传染病药剂师在出院后 2 个工作日和 2 周内进行电话会面,以在医生的监督下审查治疗、监测、依从性和不良反应。
主要结果
在 163 名 COpAT 患者中,79.8% (130/163) 因口服抗生素出院,而历史 队列研究 中这一比例为 33.9% (42/124) (p<0.0001)。COpAT 患者的中位住院时间较短(6.1 天 [IQR 4.2–8.7] 与 8.2 天 [IQR 5.3–12.1],p<0.0001),并且 30 天再入院率在数值上较低(12% vs 16.9%,p=0.258)。
解释与创新
作者将改善的结果归因于增加口服抗生素的使用、快速出院后随访以及频繁的药剂师干预,表明 COpAT 计划可增强患者的安全和严重感染的护理过渡。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持实施 COpAT 计划,以提高患者安全并优化严重感染中抗生素的使用,特别是需要长期治疗的骨关节感染。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计、对历史对照的依赖以及缺乏随机性限制了因果推断;普遍性可能受到单中心数据和特定感染类型的限制,需要适度的信心。

10 Five Measles Screening Questions: Proof of Concept of Weighted Scoring System for Pediatrics

Authors: Saravolatz L, Gandhi T, Garrity A, Zhou S, DeTroyer-Cooley O, Keedy J
Original abstract (English)
Methods: Adult patients discharged from MM hospital between Jan-Oct 2025 were eligible for COpAT program enrollment if prescribed antibiotics for ≥14 days, including oral antibiotics requiring lab monitoring, long-acting lipoglycopeptides, or intravenous (IV) antibiotics administered at a hemodialysis center, subacute nursing facility, or home. The pharmacist conducts a telephone-encounter within 2 business days of discharge to review antibiotic plan, lab monitoring, appropriate antibiotic administration, duration and adherence, and assess for adverse effects. The outpatient ID physician is notified of treatment concerns and lab monitoring results. Results: From January-October 2025, 163 patients with osteoarticular infections were enrolled in COpAT post-discharge. Conclusion: COpAT patients had significantly increased oral antibiotic utilization and shorter LOS, and numerically fewer readmissions compared to a historic cohort. Among COpAT patients, 79.8% (130/163) were discharged on oral antibiotics compared to 33.9% (42/124) in the historical cohort (p<0.0001, Figure 1). COpAT patients had a shorter median LOS of 6.1 days [IQR 4.2-8.7] versus 8.2 days [IQR 5.3-12.1, p<0.0001], and numerical reduction of 30-day readmission rate of 12% (19/158) versus 16.9% (22/132) in the historical cohort (p=0.258). AbstractBackground: With increasing utilization of oral antibiotics in serious infections, complex outpatient antimicrobial therapy (COpAT) programs are vital for improving patient safety and outcomes. We discuss the Michigan Medicine (MM) COpAT program, including processes, interventions, and evaluate its impact. Eligible diagnoses include osteomyelitis, prosthetic joint infection, septic joint, spinal hardware infection and infectious tenosynovitis. Established in Octob...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for safe transitions of care for patients requiring prolonged outpatient antibiotic therapy, highlighting gaps in monitoring and follow-up after hospital discharge for serious infections.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study compared outcomes of patients enrolled in the Michigan Medicine Complex Outpatient Antimicrobial Therapy (COpAT) program from January to October 2025 with a historical cohort discharged on antibiotics for osteoarticular infections between May and August 2023.
Core methods
Eligible patients were adults discharged on antibiotics for ≥14 days, including oral antibiotics requiring monitoring, long-acting lipoglycopeptides, or IV antibiotics administered at specific settings. An infectious diseases pharmacist conducted telephone encounters within 2 business days and 2 weeks post-discharge to review therapy, monitoring, adherence, and adverse effects, with physician oversight.
Main results
Among 163 COpAT patients, 79.8% (130/163) were discharged on oral antibiotics versus 33.9% (42/124) in the historical cohort (p<0.0001). COpAT patients had a shorter median length of stay (6.1 days [IQR 4.2–8.7] vs 8.2 days [IQR 5.3–12.1], p<0.0001) and a numerically lower 30-day readmission rate (12% vs 16.9%, p=0.258).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors attribute improved outcomes to increased oral antibiotic use, rapid post-discharge follow-up, and frequent pharmacist interventions, suggesting COpAT programs enhance patient safety and care transitions for serious infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support implementing COpAT programs to improve patient safety and optimize antibiotic use in serious infections, particularly for osteoarticular infections requiring prolonged therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design, reliance on historical controls, and lack of randomization limit causal inference; generalizability may be constrained by single-center data and specific infection types, warranting moderate confidence.

285 为多个利益相关者群体之间的 麻疹 暴露管理开发共享患者登记册

285 Development of a shared patient registry for measles exposure management amongst multiple stakeholder groups
作者： Prins C, Pulaparthi S, Burke A
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决感染预防人员在应对长期护理机构中与增强屏障预防措施 (EBP) 相关的操作挑战、工作人员依从性和居民安全方面尚未探索的经验。
设计与对象
2024 年 5 月至 9 月期间,向佛罗里达州 578 家疗养院分发了一份包含 42 项的电子调查,针对感染预防人员。共收到 90 份有效回复,其中 37 名受访者在 7 个提示中提供了 58 条评论。
核心方法
该调查是通过 Qualtrics 进行的,并使用 MAXQDA Analytics Pro 将评论分为九类进行分析。
主要结果
编码类别包括引用或反馈、标准的清晰度和范围、成本和资源负担、对感染控制实践的影响、EBP 的有效性、住院医师体验、员工困惑、人员配备、工作量和合规性以及对 EBP 的支持。十四条评论属于引文或反馈,十条属于标准的清晰度和范围,其他评论则分布在其余类别中。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了实施 EBP 方面的重大挑战,特别是人员限制、标准不明确和资源限制,这些以前没有得到充分记录。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过解决长期护理机构中有效实施 EBP 的障碍,为改善感染控制实践和政策制定的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它依赖于自我报告的调查数据,这可能会引入偏差,并且可能对 EBP 标准进行主观解释。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

285 Development of a shared patient registry for measles exposure management amongst multiple stakeholder groups

Authors: Prins C, Pulaparthi S, Burke A
Original abstract (English)
Methods A 42-item electronic survey was distributed via Qualtrics to 578 Florida nursing homes between May and September 2024, addressed to “Infection Control.” Ninety valid responses were received. Results The coding categories were: 1) Citation or Feedback, 2) Clarity and Scope of Criteria, 3) Cost and Resource Burden, 4) Effect on Infection Control Practice, 5) Effectiveness of EBP, 6) Resident Experience, 7) Staff Confusion, 8) Staffing, Workload, and Compliance, and 9) Support for EBP. 285 Development of a shared patient registry for measles exposure management amongst multiple stakeholder groups Background Enhanced Barrier Precautions (EBP) aim to reduce multidrug-resistant organism transmission in long-term care settings. Infection preventionists play a critical role in implementing EBP, yet their experiences navigating operational challenges, staff adherence, and resident safety remain underexplored. Understanding these perspectives informs strategies for effective infection control and policy development. Thirty-seven respondents provided 58 comments across seven prompts—six linked to specific survey questions and one to an open-ended prompt to share questions or thoughts about EBP. Comments were coded into nine categories and analyzed using MAXQDA Analytics Pro. Fourteen coded comments fell under Citation or Feedback, mostly related to corrective feedback or citations during F880 survey experiences, including, “Facility interpreted an action to be low contact and therefore not needing EBP while surveyor considered action to be high contact requiring full EBP”. No one...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the underexplored experiences of infection preventionists in navigating operational challenges, staff adherence, and resident safety related to Enhanced Barrier Precautions (EBP) in long-term care settings.
Design and population
A 42-item electronic survey was distributed to 578 Florida nursing homes between May and September 2024, targeting infection preventionists. Ninety valid responses were received, with thirty-seven respondents providing 58 comments across seven prompts.
Core methods
The survey was conducted via Qualtrics, and comments were coded into nine categories using MAXQDA Analytics Pro for analysis.
Main results
The coding categories included Citation or Feedback, Clarity and Scope of Criteria, Cost and Resource Burden, Effect on Infection Control Practice, Effectiveness of EBP, Resident Experience, Staff Confusion, Staffing, Workload, and Compliance, and Support for EBP. Fourteen comments fell under Citation or Feedback, ten under Clarity and Scope of Criteria, and others were distributed across the remaining categories.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting significant challenges in implementing EBP, particularly staffing limitations, unclear criteria, and resource constraints, which were not previously well-documented.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform strategies for improving infection control practices and policy development by addressing the barriers to effective implementation of EBP in long-term care settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported survey data, which may introduce bias, and the potential for subjective interpretation of EBP criteria. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

252 在电子健康记录中开发 麻疹 标记系统

252 Developing a Measles Flagging System within the Electronic Health Record
作者： Battjes R, Teska P, Nankoosingh V
摘要中文翻译研究人员对环境清洁过程进行了一项多医院观察性研究。方法:在美国 3 个不同地区的 3 家大型(<500 张床位)教学医院中,研究人员在不同病房类型的日常和出院病房清洁期间对 EVS 技术人员进行跟踪。结果:总共评估了 638 次日常清洁和 91 次出院清洁。结论:迄今为止,EH 研究主要集中在通过荧光标记、ATP 拭子和/或环境培养测量的清洁依从性上。该表格比较了日常使用的病房中不同研究地点的清洁指标。比较不同研究地点的出院病房清洁指标的表格。背景:环境卫生 (EH) 在降低医疗相关感染 (HAI) 风险方面发挥着关键作用,但医院病房清洁和消毒方面仍然存在显着差异。虽然出院清洁依从性经常被研究,但日常病房清洁的实际实践仍然很少被描述。此外,尽管完成环境服务 (EVS) 任务的时间与预算、设施吞吐量以及患者和员工满意度相关,但已发表的文献中很少报道完成环境服务 (EVS) 任务的时间。直接实践观察记录了总周转时间、使用标准化数据收集工具完成清洁患者和浴室表面、拖地和非消毒任务等任务的时间(图 1)。清洁依从性也通过视觉评估来测量,定义为用消毒湿巾或布擦拭表面。平均每日客房周转时间为9分钟。然而,面积、隔离需求、室内设备和 EVS 期望因单元类型和个体而异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在在电子健康记录中开发 麻疹 标记系统,以改进 麻疹 和 麻疹 病例的管理。背景强调了环境卫生在降低医疗保健相关感染风险方面的关键作用,重点是清洁实践的变化。
设计与对象
该研究是一项观察性多医院调查,在美国 3 个不同地区的 3 家大型教学医院中进行。研究人员在日常清洁过程中对环境服务 (EVS) 技术人员进行跟踪,并在不同类型的病房中出院进行病房清洁。
核心方法
直接实践观察用于记录总周转时间和花在特定任务上的时间,例如清洁患者和浴室表面、拖地和非消毒活动。通过视觉评估来评估清洁依从性,定义为用消毒湿巾或布擦拭表面。
主要结果
总共评估了 638 次日常清洁和 91 次出院清洁。每天平均占用的房间周转时间为 9 分钟,其中只有三分之一的时间用于使用消毒剂。日常清洁期间的高接触表面 (HTS) 清洁依从性较低 (38-48%),而拖地的情况发生在 <90% 的房间中。站点 Z 花费大约两倍的平均时间进行日常房间清洁,但未达到超过 50% 的 HTS 清洁合规性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,目前的 EH 研究主要集中在通过荧光标记、ATP 拭子和环境培养测量的依从性上,但很少关注日常占用病房清洁所需的时间和资源。这项研究为现实环境中清洁的实际挑战提供了新颖的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以让医疗保健领导层了解在预算时间内清洁的现实期望,以及时间限制对消毒剂功效、法规遵从性和感染预防策略的潜在影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,依赖于直接的实践观察,这可能会引入偏差。由于缺乏随机设计以及不同地点的技术人员表现存在潜在差异,证据强度中等。

252 Developing a Measles Flagging System within the Electronic Health Record

Authors: Battjes R, Teska P, Nankoosingh V
Original abstract (English)
Researchers conducted a multi-hospital, observational study about environmental cleaning processes. Methods: Across 3 large (<500 beds) teaching hospitals in 3 distinct US regions, researchers shadowed EVS technicians during daily and discharge patient room cleaning across diverse ward types. Results: A total of 638 daily and 91 discharge cleans were evaluated. Conclusion: To date, EH research has been focused on cleaning compliance measured by fluorescent marking, ATP swabbing and/or environmental culturing. A table comparing cleaning metrics across different study sites in daily occupied patient rooms. A table comparing discharge patient room cleaning metrics across different study sites. Background: Environmental hygiene (EH) plays a critical role in reducing healthcare-associated infection (HAI) risk, yet significant variability persists in hospital patient room cleaning and disinfection. While discharge cleaning compliance is frequently studied, real-world practices during daily occupied patient room cleaning remain poorly characterized. Furthermore, the time to complete Environmental Services (EVS) tasks is rarely reported in published literature, despite being linked to budget, facility throughput, and patient and employee satisfaction. Direct practice observations documented total turnover time, time to complete tasks such as cleaning the patient and bathroom room surfaces, floor mopping and non-disinfection tasks using a standardized data collection tool (Figure 1). Cleaning compliance was also measured via visual assessment, defined as wiping a surface with a disinfectant wipe or cloth. The average occupied daily room turnover time was 9 minutes. However, square footage, isolation needs, in-room equipment and EVS expectations varied across unit types and indi...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a measles flagging system within the electronic health record to improve surveillance and management of measles cases. The background highlights the critical role of environmental hygiene in reducing healthcare-associated infection risk, with a focus on the variability in cleaning practices.
Design and population
The study was an observational, multi-hospital investigation conducted across 3 large teaching hospitals in 3 distinct US regions. Researchers shadowed Environmental Services (EVS) technicians during daily and discharge patient room cleaning across diverse ward types.
Core methods
Direct practice observations were used to document total turnover time and time spent on specific tasks such as cleaning patient and bathroom surfaces, floor mopping, and non-disinfection activities. Cleaning compliance was assessed via visual evaluation, defined as wiping a surface with a disinfectant wipe or cloth.
Main results
A total of 638 daily and 91 discharge cleans were evaluated. The average occupied daily room turnover time was 9 minutes, with only a third of that time dedicated to disinfectant use. High-touch surface (HTS) cleaning compliance during daily cleans was low (38–48%), whereas floor mopping occurred in <90% of rooms. Site Z spent approximately twice the average time performing a daily room clean but did not achieve greater than 50% HTS cleaning compliance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that current EH research has primarily focused on compliance measured by fluorescent marking, ATP swabbing, and environmental culturing, but less attention has been given to the time and resources required for daily occupied patient room cleaning. This study provides novel insights into the practical challenges of cleaning in real-world settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform healthcare leadership about realistic expectations for cleaning within budgeted time and the potential impact of time constraints on disinfectant efficacy, regulatory compliance, and infection prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and relies on direct practice observations, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of randomized design and potential variability in technician performance across sites.

为什么全球数百万人将在 2026 年感染 麻疹

Why millions of people around the world will contract measles in 2026
作者： Kathryn Jacobsen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 2026 年 麻疹 病例上升的全球公共卫生问题,强调需要了解导致这种增加的因素以及相关风险。
设计与对象
该研究是 2026 年进行的全球流行病学分析,研究了非洲、亚洲、美洲和欧洲等不同地区的 麻疹 病例,重点关注经历 麻疹 的国家和 疫苗接种 发病率差异。
核心方法
该研究使用全球健康数据,包括 疫苗接种 覆盖率、暴发 报告和公共卫生观察结果,来确定影响 麻疹、传播 和 发病率 的趋势和因素。
主要结果
研究发现,2026 年的 麻疹 流行病与 疫苗接种 发病率下降有关,受冲突影响的国家以及马尔代夫和赞比亚等和平国家报告了具体病例。在孟加拉国,疫苗 短缺和资金问题导致 麻疹 病例和死亡人数大幅上升。
解释与创新
作者解释说,麻疹 在全球范围内流行的根本原因是 麻疹 发病率的下降,他们指出,由于情况和挑战不同,各个国家的情况也有所不同。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明维持高 疫苗接种 率以预防 暴发 的重要性,并强调需要在政治不稳定的低收入国家和地区采取有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
由于许多低收入国家缺乏确切的病例数,证据有限,因为只有一小部分 麻疹 病例是通过实验室检测确诊的。该研究根据报告的观察结果和公共卫生数据对情况进行了适度的评估。

Why millions of people around the world will contract measles in 2026

Authors: Kathryn Jacobsen
Original abstract (English)
As an infectious disease epidemiologist, I study the reasons for changes in population health risks around the world. The World Health Organization estimates that in 2026 nearly 11 million people will contract the virus that causes measles. Most of these cases will occur in Africa and Asia, but many countries in the Americas and Europe are reporting outbreaks affecting hundreds or thousands of people. There is no treatment for measles, but vaccines have been proven to be safe and effective at preventing the disease. Unvaccinated people who get sick with measles are at risk of serious complications that can lead to deafness, blindness and death. The risk of a bad outcome is higher for people who are undernourished and unable to access supportive medical care. The specific factors leading to measles epidemics vary by country, but the underlying cause everywhere is decreased vaccination rates. Zero-dose children in low-income countries The vast majority of parents around the world want to protect their children from preventable infectious diseases through vaccination. In many lower-income countries, the supply of vaccines remains insufficient to meet the demand for them. Because measles is highly contagious, the vaccination rate needs to be above 95% to protect a community from outbreaks, creating what is known as “herd immunity.” One dose of measles vaccine protects most people from the disease, but two doses are recommended to improve effectiveness. Most high-income countries have measles vaccine rates near the 95% target. In Europe, the average one-dose vaccination rate is about 94%, and the two-dose rate is about 88%. In the U.S., more than 90% of 2-year-olds have received at least one dose of measles vaccine and more than 92% of kindergartners have received two doses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the global public health concern of rising measles cases in 2026, highlighting the need to understand the factors contributing to this increase and the associated risks.
Design and population
The study is a global epidemiological analysis conducted in 2026, examining measles cases across various regions, including Africa, Asia, the Americas, and Europe, with a focus on countries experiencing outbreaks and disparities in vaccination rates.
Core methods
The study uses global health data, including vaccination coverage rates, outbreak reports, and public health observations, to identify trends and factors influencing measles transmission and incidence.
Main results
The study found that measles epidemics in 2026 are linked to decreased vaccination rates, with specific cases reported in countries affected by conflict and in peaceful countries like the Maldives and Zambia. In Bangladesh, vaccine shortages and funding issues have contributed to a significant rise in measles cases and deaths.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the underlying cause of measles epidemics globally is the decline in vaccination rates, and they note that the situation varies by country due to differing circumstances and challenges.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of maintaining high vaccination rates to prevent outbreaks and highlight the need for targeted interventions in low-income countries and regions with political instability.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the lack of exact case counts in many lower-income countries, as only a small fraction of measles cases are confirmed through laboratory tests. The study provides a moderate assessment of the situation based on reported observations and public health data.

合成碳水化合物缀合物 疫苗、SF2a-TT15 针对肯尼亚婴儿福氏志贺菌 2a 的安全性和免疫原性:一项 2a 期、年龄递减、剂量递增、双盲、随机、安慰剂对照研究。

Safety and immunogenicity of the synthetic carbohydrate conjugate vaccine, SF2a-TT15, against Shigella flexneri 2a in infants in Kenya: a phase 2a, age-descending, dose-escalating, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled study.
作者： Frederic Sawe, Lucy Koech, Souhir Neffati, Beatrice C. Korir, Renee M. Laird, Elizabeth A. Odundo, Mary Kirui, Kristen A. Clarkson, Cécile Artaud, Mohand Ait-Ahmed
摘要中文翻译背景:志贺氏菌是低收入和中等收入国家 5 岁以下儿童发病和 死亡率 的主要原因,也是抗菌素耐药性的主要因素。迫切需要一个有效的疫苗。SF2a-TT15 是一种缀合物,其特征是福氏志贺菌 2a (SF2a) 脂多糖 O 抗原成分的合成 15 聚体寡糖替代物,之前的一项人体研究表明其具有安全性和免疫原性。我们的目的是评估其在志贺氏菌 疫苗 的目标人群——地方病环境中的婴儿中的安全性和免疫原性。方法:我们在肯尼亚医学研究所/沃尔特里德陆军研究所(肯尼亚凯里乔)临床研究中心进行了一项 2a 期、年龄递减、剂量递增、双盲、随机、安慰剂对照研究。年龄别体重 Z 评分为 -2 或更高的 18-50 岁健康成人和 2-5 岁健康儿童被随机分配 (3:1) 接受 10 μg 低聚糖剂量的 SF2a-TT15 佐以氢氧化铝(明矾)或匹配的安慰剂。将年龄别体重 Z 评分为 -2 或以上的 9 个月±1 个月的健康婴儿以逐步方式随机分配(3:3:3:3:1:1),接受 2 μg 寡糖剂量的无明矾佐剂 SF2a-TT15、2 μg 寡糖剂量的明矾佐剂 SF2a-TT15、10 μg 寡糖剂量的明矾佐剂 SF2a-TT15。非明矾佐剂 SF2a-TT15、10 μg 寡糖剂量的明矾佐剂 SF2a-TT15、非明矾佐剂安慰剂或明矾佐剂安慰剂,同时与常规 麻疹-风疹 疫苗接种 联合使用。研究产品在第 0 天、第 90 天和第 270 天进行肌内注射,最后一次注射后进行 6 个月的随访。随机化序列由计算机生成,随机化按 队列研究 和 治疗 组进行分层。注射前对注射器进行过度标记以保持掩蔽;除药师团队(包括药房数据质量监测团队和巴斯德研究所团队的一名人员)外,所有临床中心团队均处于蒙蔽状态。所有参与者也都戴着面具。队列研究 和剂量水平之间的进展由独立数据监测委员会监督。主要结局是所有 队列研究 不良事件的 发病率 和婴儿 队列研究 的免疫原性,在每次注射后第 14 天和 28 天以及研究结束时通过针对 SF2a 脂多糖的血清 IgG 抗体和应答者比例进行测量。对接受至少一次注射的所有参与者的安全性终点进行了分析,并在完整分析集中和按方案分析集中对免疫原性终点进行了分析。本研究已在 ClinicalTrials.gov 注册(NCT04602975)并已完成。结果:入组时间为 2020 年 10 月 26 日至 2022 年 1 月 5 日。对 54 名成人进行了资格筛查,其中 16 名入组并随机分配至含佐剂的 10 μg 寡糖剂量 (n=12) 或含佐剂的安慰剂组 (n=4)。对 30 名儿童进行了资格筛查,其中 16 名儿童被纳入并随机分配至含佐剂的 10 μg 寡糖剂量 (n=12) 或含佐剂的安慰剂组 (n=4)。筛选了 413 名婴儿的资格,其中 216 名被纳入并随机分配至无佐剂 2 μg 低聚糖剂量 (n=46)、有佐剂 2 μg 低聚糖剂量 (n=44)、无佐剂 10 μg 低聚糖剂量 (n=41)、有佐剂 10 μg 低聚糖剂量 (n=43)、无佐剂安慰剂(n=22),或辅助安慰剂 (n=20)。安全性分析集中的 215 名婴儿中有 112 名(52%)是女性,103 名(48%)是男性。SF2a-TT15 糖复合物具有良好的耐受性,并在成人和儿童中诱导 SF2a 脂多糖的高血清 IgG 几何平均滴度 (GMT)。在婴儿中,SF2a-TT15 在两种剂量(佐剂或非佐剂)下均具有良好的耐受性,没有与 疫苗 相关的严重不良事件。216 名婴儿中的 202 名(94%)至少发生过一种未经请求的不良事件。其中 182 名 (90%) 婴儿出现了被认为与研究产品无关的不良事件。任何 治疗 组之间未报告主动不良事件发生频率存在显着差异(所有比较 p>0·05)。婴儿 队列研究 中记录了 1235 起不良事件,其中大多数与研究产品无关 (1226 [99%]),分级为轻度 (744 [60%]) 或中度 (435 [35%])。每次注射后 28 天,无论有佐剂还是无佐剂,SF2a-TT15 均诱导 SF2a 脂多糖的血清 IgG GMT 显着高于安慰剂接受者的相应 GMT(首次注射后 28 天,无佐剂 2 μg 寡糖剂量的 p = 0·047,所有时间点的 p = 0·5)。研究结束时,无论是否有辅助治疗,两种剂量的 GMT 均有所下降,但与安慰剂组相比仍显着升高 (p<0·001)。解释:SF2a-TT15 在肯尼亚婴儿中具有良好的耐受性和强免疫原性,显示出进一步发展的潜力。现有数据支持推进基于多价合成聚糖的志贺氏菌 疫苗。资助:盖茨基金会、威康信托基金、欧盟第七框架计划、新兴传染病卓越综合生物学实验室和巴斯德研究所。翻译:有关摘要的斯瓦西里语翻译,请参阅补充材料部分。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
志贺氏菌是低收入和中等收入国家 5 岁以下儿童发病和 死亡率 的主要原因,也是抗菌素耐药性的主要因素。迫切需要一个有效的疫苗。
设计与对象
我们在肯尼亚医学研究所/沃尔特里德陆军研究所(肯尼亚凯里乔)临床研究中心进行了一项 2a 期、年龄递减、剂量递增、双盲、随机、安慰剂对照研究。年龄别体重 Z 评分为 -2 或更高的 18-50 岁健康成人和 2-5 岁健康儿童被随机分配 (3:1) 接受 10 μg 低聚糖剂量的 SF2a-TT15 佐以氢氧化铝(明矾)或匹配的安慰剂。
核心方法
研究产品在第 0 天、第 90 天和第 270 天进行肌内注射,最后一次注射后进行 6 个月的随访。随机化序列由计算机生成,随机化按 队列研究 和 治疗 组进行分层。对接受至少一次注射的所有参与者的安全性终点进行了分析,并在完整分析集中和按方案分析集中对免疫原性终点进行了分析。
主要结果
在婴儿中,SF2a-TT15 在两种剂量(佐剂或非佐剂)下均具有良好的耐受性,没有与 疫苗 相关的严重不良事件。216 名婴儿中的 202 名(94%)至少发生过一种未经请求的不良事件。其中 182 名 (90%) 婴儿出现了被认为与研究产品无关的不良事件。任何 治疗 组之间未报告主动不良事件发生频率存在显着差异(所有比较 p>0·05)。
解释与创新
SF2a-TT15 在肯尼亚婴儿中具有良好的耐受性和强免疫原性,显示出进一步发展的潜力。现有数据支持推进基于多价合成聚糖的志贺氏菌 疫苗。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,SF2a-TT15 可能是志贺氏菌 疫苗 的有希望的候选者,特别是对于流行地区的婴儿。疫苗在婴儿中的安全性和免疫原性为未来疫苗的开发和实施提供了重要数据。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在单一流行环境中进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。对于婴儿的安全性和免疫原性,有强有力的证据,但需要进一步的研究来在更大、更多样化的人群中证实这些发现。

Safety and immunogenicity of the synthetic carbohydrate conjugate vaccine, SF2a-TT15, against Shigella flexneri 2a in infants in Kenya: a phase 2a, age-descending, dose-escalating, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled study.

Authors: Frederic Sawe, Lucy Koech, Souhir Neffati, Beatrice C. Korir, Renee M. Laird, Elizabeth A. Odundo, Mary Kirui, Kristen A. Clarkson, Cécile Artaud, Mohand Ait-Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Shigella is the leading cause of morbidity and mortality in children younger than 5 years in low- and middle-income countries and is a major actor in antimicrobial resistance. An effective vaccine is urgently needed. SF2a-TT15, a conjugate featuring a synthetic 15-mer oligosaccharide surrogate of the O-antigen component of Shigella flexneri 2a (SF2a) lipopolysaccharide, was previously shown to be safe and immunogenic in a first-in-human study. We aimed to assess its safety and immunogenicity in infants from an endemic setting, the target population for a Shigella vaccine. METHODS: We conducted a phase 2a, age-descending, dose-escalating, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled study at the Clinical Research Center, Kenya Medical Research Institute/Walter Reed Army Institute of Research (Kericho, Kenya). Healthy adults aged 18-50 years and healthy children aged 2-5 years with a weight-for-age Z score of -2 or more were randomly assigned (3:1) to receive a 10 μg oligosaccharide dose of SF2a-TT15 adjuvanted with aluminium hydroxide (alum) or matching placebo. Healthy infants aged 9 months plus or minus 1 month with a weight-for-age Z score of -2 or more were randomly assigned (3:3:3:3:1:1) in a stepwise manner to receive a 2 μg oligosaccharide dose of non-alum-adjuvanted SF2a-TT15, a 2 μg oligosaccharide dose of alum-adjuvanted SF2a-TT15, a 10 μg oligosaccharide dose of non-alum-adjuvanted SF2a-TT15, a 10 μg oligosaccharide dose of alum-adjuvanted SF2a-TT15, non-alum-adjuvanted placebo, or alum-adjuvanted placebo, concomitantly with routine measles-rubella vaccination. Study products were administered intramuscularly on day 0, 90, and 270, with a 6-month follow-up after the last injection. The randomisation sequence was computer-generated and randomisation was stratified by cohort and treatment group. Syringes were over-labelled before injection to maintain masking; all clinical site teams were masked except the pharmacist team, including the pharmacy data quality monitoring team, and one person in the Institut Pasteur team. All participants were also masked. Progression between cohorts and dose levels was overseen by an independent data monitoring committee. The primary outcomes were the incidence of adverse events in all cohorts and immunogenicity in the infant cohort, as measured at days 14 and 28 after each injection and at the end of the study by serum IgG antibodies to SF2a lipopolysaccharide and proportion of responders. Safety endpoints were analysed in all participants receiving at least one injection and immunogenicity endpoints were analysed in the full analysis set and in the per-protocol analysis set. This study is registered with ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT04602975, and is completed. FINDINGS: Enrolment was between Oct 26, 2020, and Jan 5, 2022. 54 adults were screened for eligibility, 16 of whom were enrolled and randomly assigned to the adjuvanted 10 μg oligosaccharide dose (n=12) or adjuvanted placebo (n=4). 30 children were screened for eligibility, 16 of whom were enrolled and randomly assigned to the adjuvanted 10 μg oligosaccharide dose (n=12) or adjuvanted placebo (n=4). 413 infants were screened for eligibility, 216 of whom were enrolled and randomly assigned to the non-adjuvanted 2 μg oligosaccharide dose (n=46), the adjuvanted 2 μg oligosaccharide dose (n=44), the non-adjuvanted 10 μg oligosaccharide dose (n=41), the adjuvanted 10 μg oligosaccharide dose (n=43), non-adjuvanted placebo (n=22), or adjuvanted placebo (n=20). 112 (52%) of 215 infants in the safety analysis set were female and 103 (48%) were male. The SF2a-TT15 glycoconjugate was well tolerated and induced high serum IgG geometric mean titres (GMTs) to SF2a lipopolysaccharide in adults and children. In infants, SF2a-TT15 was well tolerated at both doses, adjuvanted or non-adjuvanted, with no vaccine-related serious adverse events. 202 (94%) of 216 infants had at least one unsolicited adverse event. 182 (90%) of these infants had adverse events considered not related to study products. No significant differences in the frequency of unsolicited adverse events were reported between any treatment groups (p>0·05 for all comparisons). 1235 adverse events were recorded in the infant cohort, most being unrelated to the study product (1226 [99%]) and of mild (744 [60%]) or moderate (435 [35%]) grading. At 28 days after each injection and for both doses, adjuvanted or non-adjuvanted, SF2a-TT15 induced significantly higher serum IgG GMTs to SF2a lipopolysaccharide compared with corresponding GMTs in placebo recipients (p=0·047 for the non-adjuvanted 2 μg oligosaccharide dose 28 days after the first injection and p 0·5 for all timepoints). At the end of the study, for both doses, adjuvanted or not, GMTs were decreased but were still significantly elevated compared with the placebo group (p<0·001). INTERPRETATION: SF2a-TT15 is well tolerated and strongly immunogenic in Kenyan infants, showing potential for further development. Available data support advancing a multivalent synthetic glycan-based Shigella vaccine. FUNDING: Gates Foundation, The Wellcome Trust, The European Union Seventh Framework Programme, Laboratoire d'Excellence Integrative Biology of Emerging Infectious Diseases, and Institut Pasteur. TRANSLATION: For the Kiswahili translation of the abstract see Supplementary Materials section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Shigella is the leading cause of morbidity and mortality in children younger than 5 years in low- and middle-income countries and is a major actor in antimicrobial resistance. An effective vaccine is urgently needed.
Design and population
We conducted a phase 2a, age-descending, dose-escalating, double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled study at the Clinical Research Center, Kenya Medical Research Institute/Walter Reed Army Institute of Research (Kericho, Kenya). Healthy adults aged 18-50 years and healthy children aged 2-5 years with a weight-for-age Z score of -2 or more were randomly assigned (3:1) to receive a 10 μg oligosaccharide dose of SF2a-TT15 adjuvanted with aluminium hydroxide (alum) or matching placebo.
Core methods
Study products were administered intramuscularly on day 0, 90, and 270, with a 6-month follow-up after the last injection. The randomisation sequence was computer-generated and randomisation was stratified by cohort and treatment group. Safety endpoints were analysed in all participants receiving at least one injection and immunogenicity endpoints were analysed in the full analysis set and in the per-protocol analysis set.
Main results
In infants, SF2a-TT15 was well tolerated at both doses, adjuvanted or non-adjuvanted, with no vaccine-related serious adverse events. 202 (94%) of 216 infants had at least one unsolicited adverse event. 182 (90%) of these infants had adverse events considered not related to study products. No significant differences in the frequency of unsolicited adverse events were reported between any treatment groups (p>0·05 for all comparisons).
Interpretation and novelty
SF2a-TT15 is well tolerated and strongly immunogenic in Kenyan infants, showing potential for further development. Available data support advancing a multivalent synthetic glycan-based Shigella vaccine.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that SF2a-TT15 could be a promising candidate for a Shigella vaccine, particularly for infants in endemic regions. The vaccine's safety and immunogenicity in infants provide important data for future vaccine development and implementation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single endemic setting, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is strong for safety and immunogenicity in infants, but further studies are needed to confirm these findings in larger and more diverse populations.

血清样本中风疹 IgM 检测的商业侧流免疫测定的评估。

Assessment of Commercial Lateral Flow Immunoassays for Rubella IgM Detection in Serum Samples.
作者： Patrick Reteng, Regina Brunnhoefer, Emilie Charpentier, Judith M Hübschen
摘要中文翻译背景:风疹通常是一种轻微的出疹性疾病,但妊娠早期感染可导致先天性风疹综合征(CRS)。准确及时的诊断对于监测和暴发控制至关重要。根据 WHO 的建议,使用酶免疫分析 (EIA) 检测风疹特异性 IgM 抗体受到基础设施、训练有素的工作人员和运输要求的限制。基于侧流免疫分析 (LFIA) 的快速诊断测试 (RDT) 可以帮助分散测试,但目前有关其性能的数据有限。方法:使用血清组和两种 EIA 来评估九种市售风疹 IgM RDT 进行比较。该小组包括风疹 IgM 阳性对照和混合 IgM 阳性血清的系列稀释液,以及风疹 IgM 阴性混合血清、强 IgG 阳性样品和 麻疹 IgM 阳性样品。结果:大多数 RDT 的分析灵敏度明显低于 EIA,仅在较高抗体浓度下检测 IgM。两项 RDT 表现出明显较高的敏感性,但特异性降低。没有观察到高风疹 IgG 水平的干扰。结论:总体而言,本研究中包含的风疹 IgM RDT 在敏感性和/或特异性方面显示出很大的局限性。虽然它们可能支持在实验室能力有限的环境中快速识别病例,但结果应谨慎解释并由 EIA 确认,特别是在消除环境中。

问题与背景
风疹通常是一种轻微的出疹性疾病,但妊娠早期感染可导致先天性风疹综合征(CRS)。准确及时的诊断对于监测和暴发控制至关重要。
设计与对象
使用血清组和两种 EIA 来评估九种市售风疹 IgM RDT 进行比较。该小组包括风疹 IgM 阳性对照和混合 IgM 阳性血清的系列稀释液,以及风疹 IgM 阴性混合血清、强 IgG 阳性样品和 麻疹 IgM 阳性样品。
核心方法
基于侧流免疫分析 (LFIA) 的快速诊断测试 (RDT) 可以帮助分散测试,但目前有关其性能的数据有限。根据 WHO 的建议,使用酶免疫分析 (EIA) 检测风疹特异性 IgM 抗体受到基础设施、训练有素的工作人员和运输要求的限制。
主要结果
大多数 RDT 的分析灵敏度明显低于 EIA,仅在较高抗体浓度下检测 IgM。两项 RDT 表现出明显较高的敏感性,但特异性降低。没有观察到高风疹 IgG 水平的干扰。
解释与创新
总体而言,本研究中包含的风疹 IgM RDT 在敏感性和/或特异性方面显示出很大的局限性。虽然它们可能支持在实验室能力有限的环境中快速识别病例,但结果应谨慎解释并由 EIA 确认,特别是在消除环境中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以谨慎地告知在实验室能力有限的环境中使用风疹 IgM RDT,但强调需要 EIA 确认以确保准确的 诊断,特别是在消除环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于使用血清组和缺乏真实世界的临床验证而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它是基于受控实验室评估而不是现场研究。

Assessment of Commercial Lateral Flow Immunoassays for Rubella IgM Detection in Serum Samples.

Authors: Patrick Reteng, Regina Brunnhoefer, Emilie Charpentier, Judith M Hübschen
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Rubella is generally a mild exanthematous disease, but infection during early pregnancy can result in congenital rubella syndrome (CRS). Accurate and timely diagnosis is essential for surveillance and outbreak control. The use of enzyme immunoassays (EIA) for the detection of rubella-specific IgM antibodies as recommended by WHO is limited by infrastructure, trained staff, and transport requirements. Lateral flow immunoassay (LFIA)-based rapid diagnostic tests (RDTs) could aid decentralized testing, yet data on their performance are currently limited. METHODS: Nine commercially available rubella IgM RDTs were evaluated using a serum panel and two EIAs for comparison. The panel included serial dilutions of rubella IgM positive controls and pooled IgM positive sera, as well as rubella IgM negative pooled sera, a strong IgG positive sample, and a measles IgM positive sample. RESULTS: Most RDTs showed substantially lower analytical sensitivity than EIAs, detecting IgM only at higher antibody concentrations. Two RDTs demonstrated an apparently higher sensitivity but reduced specificity. No interference from high rubella IgG levels was observed. CONCLUSION: Overall, the rubella IgM RDTs included in this study displayed substantial limitations in sensitivity and/or specificity. While they may support rapid case identification in settings with limited laboratory capacity, results should be interpreted cautiously and confirmed by EIA, particularly in elimination settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rubella is generally a mild exanthematous disease, but infection during early pregnancy can result in congenital rubella syndrome (CRS). Accurate and timely diagnosis is essential for surveillance and outbreak control.
Design and population
Nine commercially available rubella IgM RDTs were evaluated using a serum panel and two EIAs for comparison. The panel included serial dilutions of rubella IgM positive controls and pooled IgM positive sera, as well as rubella IgM negative pooled sera, a strong IgG positive sample, and a measles IgM positive sample.
Core methods
Lateral flow immunoassay (LFIA)-based rapid diagnostic tests (RDTs) could aid decentralized testing, yet data on their performance are currently limited. The use of enzyme immunoassays (EIA) for the detection of rubella-specific IgM antibodies as recommended by WHO is limited by infrastructure, trained staff, and transport requirements.
Main results
Most RDTs showed substantially lower analytical sensitivity than EIAs, detecting IgM only at higher antibody concentrations. Two RDTs demonstrated an apparently higher sensitivity but reduced specificity. No interference from high rubella IgG levels was observed.
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, the rubella IgM RDTs included in this study displayed substantial limitations in sensitivity and/or specificity. While they may support rapid case identification in settings with limited laboratory capacity, results should be interpreted cautiously and confirmed by EIA, particularly in elimination settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can cautiously inform the use of rubella IgM RDTs in settings with limited laboratory capacity, but emphasize the need for confirmation by EIA to ensure accurate diagnosis, especially in elimination settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a serum panel and the absence of real-world clinical validation. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a controlled laboratory evaluation rather than field studies.

问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基因组 流行病学 对减少新生儿重症监护病房中金黄色葡萄球菌 传播 的影响,满足改进感染预防策略的需求。
设计与对象
该研究采用观察性嵌套 队列研究 设计,分析 AL-CIP 申请人在两个 2025 年认证周期中的二级作品集数据,并由完成结构化面试的认证专业人士 (18%) 进行嵌套 队列研究。

科研与公卫意义
结果凸显了基因组 流行病学 在改善 暴发 检测、管理干预和资源分配方面的潜力,对加强公共卫生基础设施和传染病控制领导力具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖自我报告的数据且缺乏直接的 传播 测量而受到限制,但证据得到结构化访谈和主题分析的支持,提供了适度的置信度评估。

298 Genomic Epidemiology Demonstrates that Decolonization reduces Staphylococcus aureus transmission in a Neonatal Intensive Care Unit

Authors: Ruch K, Al Mohajer M

Methods: The Leadership Evaluation and Development for Infection Preventionists (LEAD-IP) study used an obser- vational nested cohort design. S1):s15–s16 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10450 Presentation Type: Top Poster Abstract Subject Category: Occupational Health Abstract ID: 298 Genomic Epidemiology Demonstrates that Decolonization reduces Staphylococcus aureus transmission in a Neonatal Intensive Care Unit Kayla Ruch1 and Mayar Al Mohajer2 1Becton Dickinson (BD) and 2Baylor College of Medicine Background: Infection preventionists (IPs) increasingly lead systemwide healthcare epidemiology functions integrating infection prevention, anti- microbial stewardship, and preparedness for emerging pathogen...

Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of genomic epidemiology on reducing Staphylococcus aureus transmission in a Neonatal Intensive Care Unit, addressing the need for improved infection prevention strategies.
Design and population
The study used an observational nested cohort design, analyzing secondary portfolio data from AL-CIP applicants across two 2025 certification cycles, with a nested cohort of certified professionals (18%) completing structured interviews.
Core methods
Data were managed in REDCap and analyzed using Braun and Clarke’s inductive thematic analysis with a structured code-book, triangulation, and verification of thematic saturation.
Main results
Thirty AL-CIP–certified leaders across healthcare systems, public health agencies, and academic settings participated. Leaders emphasized dashboards, analytic tools, and improvement models to detect outbreaks earlier, evaluate stewardship interventions, and guide resource allocation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that genomic epidemiology can enhance leadership practices in infection prevention by integrating surveillance data, antimicrobial resistance patterns, and organizational risk indicators into strategic decision-making.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the potential of genomic epidemiology to improve outbreak detection, stewardship interventions, and resource allocation, with implications for strengthening public health infrastructure and leadership in infectious disease control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and the absence of direct transmission measurements, but the evidence is supported by structured interviews and thematic analysis, providing a moderate confidence assessment.

360 墨西哥 Cuautla 河中的抗菌素耐药性 监测

360 Antimicrobial Resistance Surveillance in the Cuautla River, Mexico
作者： Jackson N
摘要中文翻译方法:使用故障模式效应分析 (FMEA) 实施结构化、基于证据的方法来识别风险因素并指导干预措施。结果:实施后,VAE 率从每 1,000 个呼吸机日 9.8 降至零,实现 SIR ≥1.0 并达到国家基准。结论:结构化护理包、集成数据工具和多学科参与可以消除高危新生儿的 VAE。系统回顾。印度血清研究所 MMR 是一种成本较低的 疫苗,其成本较低且与其他报告一致。没有无菌性脑膜炎病例的报告。随着全球 麻疹 病例数量的不断增加,以及需要提高疫苗接种不足人群中 麻疹 疫苗接种覆盖率,加强不同 麻疹 的相似功效和安全性至关重要。所有医护人员都必须受到保护。抗菌药物管理与医疗保健 流行病学 2026;6(Suppl. S1):s191–s192 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10739 演示类型:海报演示 主题类别:VAE 摘要 ID:墨西哥 Cuautla 河中的 360 种抗菌药物耐药性 监测 Nicole Jackson1 MSOL、CIC、Atrium Health Navicent 背景:呼吸机相关事件 (VAE) 是机械通气期间稳定后呼吸系统恶化的发作,对发病率和 死亡率 构成重大风险。极低出生体重新生儿(<750 克),特别是妊娠 22-24 周出生的新生儿,非常容易受到伤害。在我们的新生儿重症监护室 (NICU),去年发生了 8 起 VAE,导致通气时间延长、感染风险增加和 死亡率 较高。减少 VAE 对于改善患者安全、健康公平和家庭体验、同时满足 NHSN 基准和监管目标至关重要。一个多维...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过加强不同 麻疹 疫苗 的相似功效和安全性来解决全球 麻疹 病例不断上升的问题,以及提高疫苗接种不足人群中 麻疹 覆盖率的需要。
设计与对象
多学科团队采用失败模式效应分析 (FMEA) 实施结构化、基于证据的方法,以识别风险因素并指导新生儿重症监护病房 (NICU) 的干预措施,重点关注极低出生体重新生儿 (<750 g)。
核心方法
制定了正式的 VAE 预防包,包括组建多学科工作组、与关键临床任务相关的流程措施、创建用于实时合规性监控的 EHR 工具、员工教育和工作流程调整以及文化参与举措。根据 NHSN 指南,收集并分析 <750 g 新生儿的干预前和干预后数据。
主要结果
实施后,VAE 率从每 1,000 个呼吸机日 9.8 降至零,实现 SIR 1.0 并达到国家基准。极低出生体重新生儿的 死亡率 下降了 60%。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为结构化护理组合、综合数据工具和多学科参与可以消除高危新生儿 VAE 的证据,这是新生儿护理领域的一个新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为改善患者安全、降低医疗成本以及通过在新生儿重症监护病房实施标准化框架和教育策略来提高全系统结果的策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注单一新生儿重症监护病房(NICU)而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它表明通过有针对性的干预措施可以显着降低 VAE 率和 死亡率。

360 Antimicrobial Resistance Surveillance in the Cuautla River, Mexico

Authors: Jackson N
Original abstract (English)
Methods: A structured, evidence-based approach was implemented using failure mode effects analysis (FMEA) to identify risk factors and guide interventions. Result: Post-implementation, VAE rates decreased from 9.8 to zero per 1,000 ventilator days, achieving an SIR ?1.0 and meeting national benchmarks.
