All HCWs must be protected. Antimicrobial Stewardship & Healthcare Epidemiology 2026;6(Suppl. S1):s191–s192 doi:10.1017/ash.2026.10739 Presentation Type: Poster Presentation Subject Category: VAE Abstract ID: 360 Antimicrobial Resistance Surveillance in the Cuautla River, Mexico Nicole Jackson1 MSOL, CIC, Atrium Health Navicent Background: Ventilator-associated events (VAEs) are episodes of respira- tory deterioration after stability during mechanical ventilation, posing sig- nificant risks for morbidity and mortality. Extremely low birth weight neonates (<750 g), particularly those born at 22–24 weeks gestation, are highly vulnerable. In our Neonatal Intensive Care Unit (NICU), eight VAEs occurred in the previous year, leading to prolonged ventilation, increased infection risk, and higher mortality. Reducing VAEs is essential for improving patient safety, health equity, and family experience while meeting NHSN benchmarks and regulatory goals. A multi- di...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the rising global measles cases and the need to increase vaccination coverage in under-vaccinated populations by reinforcing the similar efficacy and safety of different measles vaccines.
Design and population
A multi-disciplinary team implemented a structured, evidence-based approach using failure mode effects analysis (FMEA) to identify risk factors and guide interventions in a Neonatal Intensive Care Unit (NICU) focusing on extremely low birth weight neonates (<750 g).
Core methods
A formalized VAE prevention bundle was developed, including the formation of a multidisciplinary task force, process measures tied to critical clinical tasks, creation of an EHR tool for real-time compliance monitoring, staff education and workflow alignment, and cultural engagement initiatives. Pre- and post-intervention data were collected and analyzed for neonates <750 g per NHSN guidelines.
Main results
Post-implementation, VAE rates decreased from 9.8 to zero per 1,000 ventilator days, achieving an SIR ?1.0 and meeting national benchmarks. Mortality among extremely low birth weight neonates declined by 60%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as evidence that structured care bundles, integrated data tools, and multidisciplinary engagement can eliminate VAEs in high-risk neonates, which is a novel finding in the context of neonatal care.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies to improve patient safety, reduce healthcare costs, and enhance outcomes systemwide by implementing standardized frameworks and education strategies across NICUs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single NICU, which may affect generalisability. However, the evidence is strong as it demonstrates a significant reduction in VAE rates and mortality through targeted interventions.

第284章 多干净算干净?儿科医院与龟分枝杆菌 暴发 有关的冰/饮水机的缓解措施

284 How Clean is Clean? Mitigation Efforts of Ice/water Dispensers Linked to a Mycobacterium chelonae Outbreak at a Pediatric Hospital
作者： Olson A, Barth J, Rasmusson J, Kriewall N, OHoro J

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了儿科医院管理大规模 麻疹 暴露的挑战,需要快速识别和跟踪暴露患者,以防止错误并确保适当的暴露后预防 (PEP)。
设计与对象
该研究描述了在一家儿科医院的电子健康记录 (EHR) 系统中实施共享暴露管理登记处,以跟踪和管理在影响 626 名患者的大规模 麻疹 期间暴露于 麻疹 的患者。
核心方法
该登记系统由感染预防和控制 (IPAC) 与初级保健人员和 EHR 开发人员合作创建,旨在根据年龄和免疫力优先考虑暴露患者的沟通和随访。通过审查非注册报告并标记重叠暴露时间范围内的患者,将患者添加到注册系统中,从而能够确认 疫苗 状态、免疫状态识别和 PEP 标准分配。
主要结果
共享注册表提高了利益相关者之间的文档准确性和透明度,在整个 暴发 中提供暴露患者的实时状态,并实现对时间敏感的患者沟通和快速识别需要 PEP 的患者。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,大规模暴露难以管理,并且可以通过使用单个共享暴露管理注册表来防止错误,从而减轻操作负担并提高 暴发 响应的效率。
科研与公卫意义
该登记系统有助于对暴露患者进行实时监测、优先排序和管理,支持儿科环境中 麻疹 麻疹 期间的及时沟通和 PEP 管理。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于单中心、回顾性实施,没有与标准实践的比较数据,这可能会影响结论的普遍性和强度。

284 How Clean is Clean? Mitigation Efforts of Ice/water Dispensers Linked to a Mycobacterium chelonae Outbreak at a Pediatric Hospital

Authors: Olson A, Barth J, Rasmusson J, Kriewall N, OHoro J
Original abstract (English)
Conclusion Adoption of BDD was associated with a 50% (9.7-4.8%) reduction in BCC, with the lowest rates following imple- mentation of automatic BDD#2. These findings underscore the importance of optimizing BCC practices as a key component of sepsis management, antimicrobial stewardship, and patient safety efforts. Methods: Infection Prevention and Control (IPAC) worked with primary care personnel and EHR developers to create a shared registry that would prioritize com- munication and follow-up for exposed patients based on age and immun- ity. Result: The shared registry enhanced documentation accuracy and transparency between stakehold- ers. Conclusion: Large scale exposures are a challenge to manage and ensure appropriate action and communication occurs. Methods: Quality Improvement, Data Intelligence, and the interdisciplinary clinical team partnered to develop an evidence-based predictive model tailored to institutional risk factors. Result: Using the XGBoost model for nontunneled line, the test AUC was 0.831, demonstrating strong discriminative power. Conclusion: The CLABSI prediction model enables real-time identification and intervention to support proactive prevention, SHEA Spring 2026 Abstracts 2026;6 Suppl 1 S189 (ANOVA) demonstrated a statistically significant difference in BCC rates across the time periods, with post hoc comparisons showing a significantly lower contamination rate when using BDD#2 as compared with both no BDD and manual BDD#1. Additionally, manual BDD#1 was used in 48% (30/62) of the blood culture collections versus to 78% for automatic BDD#2, with staff feedback indicating greater ease of use with the auto- matic BDD#2 system. Compliance with BDD use was also high- est with automatic BDD#2, and staff reported greater ease of use. Additional...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the challenge of managing large-scale measles exposures in a pediatric hospital where rapid identification and follow-up of exposed patients were needed to prevent errors and ensure appropriate post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP).
Design and population
The study described the implementation of a shared exposure management registry within an electronic health record (EHR) system at a pediatric hospital to track and manage patients exposed to measles during a large-scale outbreak affecting 626 patients.
Core methods
The registry was created by Infection Prevention and Control (IPAC) in collaboration with primary care personnel and EHR developers to prioritize communication and follow-up for exposed patients based on age and immunity. Patients were added to the registry by reviewing non-registry reports and tagging those within overlapping exposure timeframes, enabling confirmation of vaccine status, immune status identification, and assignment of PEP criteria.
Main results
The shared registry enhanced documentation accuracy and transparency between stakeholders, provided real-time status of exposed patients throughout the outbreak, and enabled time-sensitive patient communication and rapid identification of those needing PEP.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that large-scale exposures are difficult to manage and that errors can be prevented by using a single shared exposure management registry, which reduces operational burdens and improves efficiency in outbreak response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The registry facilitates real-time monitoring, prioritization, and management of exposed patients, supporting timely communication and PEP administration during measles outbreaks in pediatric settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single-center, retrospective implementation without comparative data to standard practices, which may affect generalizability and strength of conclusions.

问题与背景
2025 年,麻疹 暴发 导致了美国 87% 的确诊病例,这凸显了医疗机构需要快速扩展的准备措施。
设计与对象
该研究在 Mayo Clinic 机构进行,感染预防和控制 (IPAC) 与护理信息学合作,为疑似、确诊和暴露患者开发 麻疹 特定的电子健康记录 (EHR) 标记。
核心方法
EHR 标志设计得高度可见,并与登记功能集成,以实现对暴露病例和确诊病例的纵向跟踪,支持感染预防和控制调查以及临床操作。
主要结果
EHR 中开发了三个不同的 麻疹 特定标志,每个标志都有表 1 中概述的具体使用标准。
解释与创新
作者将 EHR 标志解释为一种新颖且可实施的工具,可在 麻疹 暴发 期间协助设施进行病例跟踪、暴露管理以及感染预防和控制。
科研与公卫意义
EHR 中的 麻疹 标志可以通过改善医疗机构中的病例跟踪、暴露管理和实时响应能力来为感染预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告 EHR 标志在减少 麻疹 传播 或改善临床结果方面的有效性,从而将证据的强度限制为描述性实施研究。

Question and background
In 2025, measles outbreaks drove 87% of confirmed cases in the United States, highlighting the need for rapidly scalable preparedness measures in healthcare facilities.
Design and population
The study was conducted at Mayo Clinic facilities, where Infection Prevention and Control (IPAC) collaborated with nursing informatics to develop measles-specific electronic health record (EHR) flags for suspected, confirmed, and exposed patients.
Core methods
The EHR flags were designed to be highly visible and integrated with registry functionality to enable longitudinal tracking of exposed and confirmed cases, supporting infection prevention and control investigations and clinical operations.
Main results
Three distinct measles-specific flags were developed in the EHR, each with specific criteria for use as outlined in Table 1.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the EHR flags as a novel and implementable tool to assist facilities in case tracking, exposure management, and infection prevention and control during measles outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
Measles flags within the EHR can inform infection prevention strategies by improving case tracking, exposure management, and real-time response capabilities in healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report the effectiveness of the EHR flags in reducing measles transmission or improving clinical outcomes, limiting the strength of the evidence to a descriptive implementation study.

EpiFlowML:用于标准化和可重复流行病学预测的模块化框架

EpiFlowML: A modular framework for standardized and reproducible epidemiological forecasting
作者： Amanda J. Meadows, James Schlitt, Vikram Sridharan, Nicole Stephenson, Nita Madhav
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过开发可适应不同病原体和健康指标的模块化框架,满足对标准化和可重复的流行病学预测日益增长的需求,特别是在 麻疹病毒 的背景下。
设计与对象
EpiFlowML 是一个模块化框架,旨在标准化跨多个地理位置和数据源的流行病学时间序列的准备、建模和评估方式。它是使用全球 麻疹 预测任务进行演示的,该任务涉及在同一工作流程中对 111 个国家/地区进行建模。
核心方法
该系统提供了一个统一的工作流程,用于将异构数据集转换为一致的、可分析的格式,拟合和比较一系列机器学习 (ML) 模型,并生成结构化的、可重复的输出。每个模块都可以独立配置,允许用户在不修改核心代码的情况下使工作流程适应不同的病原体或健康指标。
主要结果
该框架集成了标准化数据摄取、预处理、模型拟合和评估例程,生成适合比较和集成开发的统一输出。
解释与创新
通过配置文件和元数据跟踪来调整这些组件,EpiFlowML 支持在不同的流行病学背景下进行透明、可重复的预测,从而提供了一种标准化和提高流行病学模型可靠性的新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
该框架有潜力提高流行病学预测的可重复性和可解释性,这对于公共卫生规划和 麻疹 暴发 的响应至关重要。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告框架的具体局限性,但该研究基于单个用例(麻疹 预测),并且没有提供详细的统计或方法验证。

EpiFlowML: A modular framework for standardized and reproducible epidemiological forecasting

Authors: Amanda J. Meadows, James Schlitt, Vikram Sridharan, Nicole Stephenson, Nita Madhav
Original abstract (English)
EpiFlowML is a modular forecasting framework designed to standardize how epidemiological time series are prepared, modeled, and evaluated across multiple geographies and data sources. The system provides a harmonized workflow for transforming heterogeneous datasets into consistent, analysis-ready formats, fitting and comparing a range of machine learning (ML) models, and producing structured, reproducible outputs. Each module can be configured independently, allowing users to adapt the workflow to different pathogens or health indicators without modifying core code. The framework integrates standardized data ingestion, preprocessing, model fitting, and evaluation routines that generate uniform outputs suitable for comparison and ensemble development. By aligning these components through configuration files and metadata tracking, EpiFlowML supports transparent, reproducible forecasting across diverse epidemiological contexts. The increasing complexity and volume of forecasting models underscores the need for such standardization (1, 2). A global measles forecasting task was used to demonstrate the framework's functionality. This use case highlights the value of harmonized data structures and standardized evaluation in improving reproducibility and interpretability, as we were able to model 111 countries in the same workflow. The framework and supporting code are fully open source under the Creative Commons 4.0 License, enabling adaptation to other diseases or forecasting applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing need for standardized and reproducible epidemiological forecasting, particularly in the context of measles virus, by developing a modular framework that can be adapted across different pathogens and health indicators.
Design and population
EpiFlowML is a modular framework designed to standardize how epidemiological time series are prepared, modeled, and evaluated across multiple geographies and data sources. It was demonstrated using a global measles forecasting task, which involved modeling 111 countries in the same workflow.
Core methods
The system provides a harmonized workflow for transforming heterogeneous datasets into consistent, analysis-ready formats, fitting and comparing a range of machine learning (ML) models, and producing structured, reproducible outputs. Each module can be configured independently, allowing users to adapt the workflow to different pathogens or health indicators without modifying core code.
Main results
The framework integrates standardized data ingestion, preprocessing, model fitting, and evaluation routines that generate uniform outputs suitable for comparison and ensemble development.
Interpretation and novelty
By aligning these components through configuration files and metadata tracking, EpiFlowML supports transparent, reproducible forecasting across diverse epidemiological contexts, offering a novel approach to standardizing and improving the reliability of epidemiological models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework has the potential to improve the reproducibility and interpretability of epidemiological forecasts, which is critical for public health planning and response to measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the framework, but the study is based on a single use case (measles forecasting) and does not provide detailed statistical or methodological validation.

38 使用数字工具对斯洛伐克急症护理医院的清洁质量评估对医院相关感染率的影响

38 Impact of Quality Assessment of Cleaning on Hospital Associated Infection Rates Using Digital Tool in Acute Care Hospital in Slovakia
作者： Hayes E, Cohn K, Grimes L, Ercolani J, Scott T, Filograna K, Smith D, Toto R, Sammons J, Smathers S
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由于 传播 持续存在和 疫苗接种 发病率下降而引起的对 麻疹 卷土重来的日益担忧,强调需要改进准备策略,以尽量减少继发病例并加强预防工作。
设计与对象
该研究在斯洛伐克的一家急症护理医院进行,重点是实施 麻疹 准备工作组方法。该工作组包括四个工作流程,针对早期 疫苗接种、暴露管理、筛选流程以及大规模 暴发 的应急计划。
核心方法
该工作组利用电子病历 (EMR) 提示来筛查儿童就诊期间的旅行情况,并与 疫苗 临床决策支持相结合。他们还开发了用于识别暴露的非免疫患者的工具,优化了大容量检测和结果跟踪的流程,并在入境点创建了简化的筛查程序。
主要结果
从 2025 年 5 月到 12 月 28 日,旅行儿童接种了 1325 份早期 MMR 疫苗。基于电子病历的报告能够快速识别暴露个体及其免疫状态,并建立了大规模测试的预先批准、结果跟踪和患者通知的流程。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为工作组方法在增强 麻疹 准备工作方面的有效性的证据,特别是通过数字工具和 疫苗接种 和暴露管理的结构化工作流程的集成。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 麻疹 准备的数字工具和结构化框架的开发提供了信息,可用于改进医疗机构中的 麻疹、响应和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其只关注单个医院并且缺乏定量结果或统计分析。然而,由于具体准备措施的实施及其直接报告,证据是有力的。

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing concern of measles resurgence due to ongoing transmission and declining vaccination rates, emphasizing the need for improved preparedness strategies to minimize secondary cases and enhance prevention efforts.
Design and population
The study was conducted in an acute care hospital in Slovakia, focusing on implementing a taskforce approach to measles preparedness. The taskforce included four workstreams targeting early vaccination, exposure management, screening processes, and contingency planning for large-scale outbreaks.
Core methods
The taskforce utilized an electronic medical record (EMR) prompt to screen for travel during child visits, integrated with vaccine clinical decision support. They also developed tools for identifying exposed non-immune patients, optimized processes for high-volume testing and result tracking, and created streamlined screening procedures at points of entry.
Main results
From May through December 28, 2025, 1325 early MMR vaccines were administered to traveling children. An EMR-based report enabled rapid identification of exposed individuals and their immune status, and processes for pre-emptive approval of large-scale testing, result tracking, and patient notification were established.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of the effectiveness of the taskforce approach in enhancing measles preparedness, particularly through the integration of digital tools and structured workflows for vaccination and exposure management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of digital tools and structured frameworks for measles preparedness, which can be applied to improve surveillance, response, and prevention strategies in healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single hospital and the absence of quantitative outcomes or statistical analysis. However, the evidence is strong due to the implementation of specific preparedness measures and their direct reporting.

300 起医院发生的脓毒症事件:可预防性和改进机会

300 Hospital-Onset Sepsis Events: Preventability and Opportunities for Improvement
作者： Van Sickels N, Kobayashi T, Howard R, Sands H, Winter K, Hodge B, Forster D, Cotner S
摘要中文翻译方法:列克星敦费耶特县卫生局 (LFCHD)、肯塔基州公共卫生部 (KDPH) 和肯塔基大学 (UKY) 于 2025 年 2 月合作制定了一项全面计划,以解决肯塔基州的 麻疹 问题。结果:从 2025 年 3 月到 10 月,为 398 名公共卫生和医疗专业人员进行了标准化教育讲座(表 1)。摘要背景:2025 年 麻疹 暴发 已影响美国各地 1900 多人。公共卫生实体和培训机构之间的密切合作可以增加公众的知识和信任,推广疫苗接种,优化检测选项并减少暴露。合作伙伴关系的优先事项是:社区和提供者教育、卫生部门和 UKY 之间的角色/责任划分、简化检测和暴露后管理的途径以及集中沟通。制定并广泛部署了标准化教育课程,以概述测试和暴露后预防 (PEP) 资源。感染预防和控制 (IPAC) 医生与卫生部门合作,使用 CDC 检测标准对所有检测请求进行分类。开展了 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 活动,重点是预防和暴露后管理。对于 UKY 的医护人员 (HCW),我们开发并广泛分发了概述 CDC 和 KDPH 算法的 Epic® 电子健康记录 (EHR) 模板和订单集。六个公共卫生和医疗保健系统根据其中心调整了公共卫生学术中心伙伴关系和协议(表 2)。联合媒体宣传和教育在肯塔基州和田纳西州拍摄了 32 个故事。开发并广泛部署了三个 Epic® EHR 模板(图...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过评估公共卫生实体和学术机构之间的合作方法在提高 麻疹 认知度和 疫苗接种 率方面的有效性,来解决影响全美 1900 多人的 2025 年 麻疹 暴发 问题。
设计与对象
该研究于 2025 年 2 月至 10 月在肯塔基州进行,涉及列克星敦费耶特县卫生局 (LFCHD)、肯塔基州公共卫生部 (KDPH) 和肯塔基大学 (UKY)。它的重点是公共卫生和医疗专业人员,有 398 名参与者接受了标准化教育课程。
核心方法
该合作伙伴开发了标准化教育课程、Epic® 电子健康记录 (EHR) 模板以及概述用于 麻疹 测试和暴露后管理的 CDC 和 KDPH 算法的订单集。这些工具被广泛部署,以增强测试路径和沟通。
主要结果
From March through October 2025, 398 public health and medical professionals received standardized educational sessions. Six public health and healthcare systems adapted the partnership protocol. One measles test returned positive, and there were zero healthcare worker exposures. Immune globulin for PEP was offered to two exposed patients, though they declined it. MMR vaccinations increased by 16% in the 0-6 year age group and 53% in the 6–18-year age group from 2024 to 2025, while decreasing slightly (4.5%) in persons over 18 years.
解释与创新
作者解释说,公共卫生系统和学术机构之间的合作提高了 麻疹 意识和 疫苗接种 率,特定年龄组的增幅显着。这种方法的新颖之处在于它在 暴发 期间整合了培训、沟通和资源优化。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,公共卫生实体和学术中心之间的伙伴关系可以加强 麻疹 预防工作,提高 麻疹 发生率,并改善暴露后管理策略,这对 麻疹 控制和公共卫生政策有直接影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它只关注单一州并且依赖医护人员的自我报告数据。证据是中等的,因为它提供了 疫苗接种 率和测试程序的具体结果,但缺乏更广泛的普遍性和长期随访。

300 Hospital-Onset Sepsis Events: Preventability and Opportunities for Improvement

Authors: Van Sickels N, Kobayashi T, Howard R, Sands H, Winter K, Hodge B, Forster D, Cotner S
Original abstract (English)
Methods: The Lexington Fayette County Health Department (LFCHD), Kentucky Department for Public Health (KDPH), and the University of Kentucky (UKY) partnered in February 2025 to develop a comprehensive plan to address measles in Kentucky. Results: From March through October 2025, the standardized educational talk was given to 398 public health and medical professionals (Table 1). AbstractBackground: The 2025 Measles outbreak has affected over 1900 people across the United States. Close collaborations between public health entities and training institutions can increase public knowledge and trust, promote vaccination, optimize options for testing, and reduce exposures. Priorities for the partnership were: community and provider education, delineation of roles/responsibilities between the health department and UKY, streamlined pathways for testing and post-exposure management, and centralized communication. Standardized educational sessions were developed and deployed widely to outline testing and post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) resources. Measles, Mumps, and Rubella (MMR) vaccination campaigns were conducted, focusing on prevention and post-exposure management. For healthcare workers (HCW) at UKY, Epic® electronic health record (EHR) templates and order sets outlining CDC and KDPH algorithms were developed and distributed widely. Six public health and healthcare systems adapted the public health-academic center partnership and protocol to their centers (Table 2). View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the 2025 Measles outbreak, which affected over 1900 people across the United States, by evaluating the effectiveness of a collaborative approach between public health entities and academic institutions in improving measles awareness and vaccination rates.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Kentucky from February to October 2025, involving the Lexington Fayette County Health Department (LFCHD), Kentucky Department for Public Health (KDPH), and the University of Kentucky (UKY). It focused on public health and medical professionals, with 398 participants receiving standardized educational sessions.
Core methods
The partnership developed standardized educational sessions, Epic® electronic health record (EHR) templates, and order sets outlining CDC and KDPH algorithms for measles testing and post-exposure management. These tools were deployed widely to enhance testing pathways and communication.
Main results
From March through October 2025, 398 public health and medical professionals received standardized educational sessions. Six public health and healthcare systems adapted the partnership protocol. One measles test returned positive, and there were zero healthcare worker exposures. Immune globulin for PEP was offered to two exposed patients, though they declined it. MMR vaccinations increased by 16% in the 0-6 year age group and 53% in the 6–18-year age group from 2024 to 2025, while decreasing slightly (4.5%) in persons over 18 years.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the collaboration between public health systems and academic institutions improved measles awareness and vaccination rates, with notable increases in specific age groups. This approach was novel in its integration of training, communication, and resource optimization during an outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that partnerships between public health entities and academic centers can enhance measles prevention efforts, increase vaccination rates, and improve post-exposure management strategies, which have direct implications for outbreak control and public health policy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single state and the reliance on self-reported data from healthcare workers. The evidence is moderate, as it provides specific results on vaccination rates and testing procedures but lacks broader generalizability and long-term follow-up.

54 从基准到最佳实践:减少脓毒症护理中的血培养污染

54 From Benchmark to Best Practice: Reducing Blood Culture Contamination in Sepsis Care
作者： Krier B, Barth J, Huskins W
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种工具,供感染预防人员按风险级别对可疑 麻疹 病例进行分层,以便在等待检测结果时就干预优先顺序做出明智的决定。这一背景强调需要立即采取行动,以确定医疗机构中暴露的个人并对其进行预防。
设计与对象
研究人群包括在实施期间接受 麻疹 测试的患者。该工具是使用另外 19 名可疑患者开发和完善的,然后部署到由 56 名患者组成的生产 队列研究 中,进行 麻疹 测试。
核心方法
开发了一个二维风险矩阵,结合了流行病学和临床风险因素,每个因素都根据是或否问题进行数字评分。这些分数的总和决定了最终的风险级别。采用描述性统计总结风险类别的分布,并应用Firth逻辑回归评估风险评分与麻疹 诊断之间的关联。
主要结果
在接受麻疹检测的56名患者中,10.7%(n=6)被归为高风险,32.1%(n=18)为中风险,57.1%(n=32)为低风险。4名患者麻疹检测阳性,其中3例被归为高风险,1例为中风险。流行病学风险每增加一个单位,麻疹 诊断阳性的几率增加2倍(优势比=2.0;95%置信区间,1.3–3.6)。低风险类别的阴性预测值为1,表明所有低风险患者检测结果均为阴性。
解释与创新
麻疹 暴露反应风险矩阵有效地对病例进行了分层,并支持感染预防优先工作。该矩阵将风险评分从任意分配转换为经过统计分析确认的经过验证的指标。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持在感染预防中使用风险分层工具来指导在管理 麻疹 病例时采取快速、基于证据的行动。这些工具对于减少医疗机构中的污染和改善脓毒症护理至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖描述性统计和逻辑回归而受到限制,可能无法完全捕捉风险因素的复杂性。证据是中等的,因为结果基于单个 队列研究 并且该工具的性能是使用一组有限的指标进行评估的。

54 From Benchmark to Best Practice: Reducing Blood Culture Contamination in Sepsis Care

Authors: Krier B, Barth J, Huskins W
Original abstract (English)
Infection preventionists (IPs) often balance proactive contact tracing with waiting for a positive test result. Objective: To develop a tool that enables IPs to stratify suspect measles cases by risk level, thereby supporting the decision to prioritize or defer active intervention while awaiting test results. Methods: In spring 2025, we developed a preliminary matrix and used 19 additional suspect patients to further refine the scoring of the tool. The study population included patients tested for measles during the implementation period. Results: Since implementation, the matrix was applied to 56 patients tested for measles. Conclusion: The Measles Exposure Response Risk Matrix effectively stratified cases and supported IP prioritization efforts. Each one-unit increase in epidemiological risk was associated with a 2 folds increase in the odds of a positive measles diagnosis (odds ratio = 2.0; 95% CI, 1.3–3.6). 54 From Benchmark to Best Practice: Reducing Blood Culture Contamination in Sepsis Care Background: A measles case requires immediate action to identify and administer prophylaxis to exposed individuals in healthcare settings. In August 2025, we deployed a finalized tool. The two-dimensional risk matrix incorporated both epidemiological risk factors and clinical risk factors, each scored numerically based on yes or no questions. The sum of both categories was used to assign a final risk level that determined the recommendations for IPs (Figure 1). Descriptive statistics were used to summarize the distribution of risk categories and outcomes. Among the whole cohort, Firth’s logistic regression was used to evaluate the association between clinical or epidemiological risk score and measles diagnosis. Among the production cohort, sensitivity, specificity, positive p...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a tool for infection preventionists to stratify suspect measles cases by risk level, enabling informed decisions on intervention prioritization while awaiting test results. The background highlighted the need for immediate action to identify and administer prophylaxis to exposed individuals in healthcare settings.
Design and population
The study population included patients tested for measles during the implementation period. The tool was developed and refined using 19 additional suspect patients and then deployed to a production cohort of 56 patients tested for measles.
Core methods
A two-dimensional risk matrix was developed, incorporating both epidemiological and clinical risk factors, each scored numerically based on yes or no questions. The sum of these scores determined the final risk level. Descriptive statistics were used to summarize the distribution of risk categories, and Firth’s logistic regression was applied to evaluate the association between risk scores and measles diagnosis.
Main results
Among the 56 patients tested for measles, 10.7% (n=6) were categorized as high risk, 32.1% (n=18) as medium, and 57.1% (n=32) as low. Four patients were positive for measles, with 3 classified as high risk and 1 as medium risk. Each one-unit increase in epidemiological risk was associated with a 2-fold increase in the odds of a positive measles diagnosis (odds ratio = 2.0; 95% CI, 1.3–3.6). The low-risk category had an NPV of 1, indicating all low-risk patients tested negative.
Interpretation and novelty
The Measles Exposure Response Risk Matrix effectively stratified cases and supported infection prevention prioritization efforts. The matrix transformed risk scores from arbitrary assignments to validated metrics, confirmed by statistical analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of risk stratification tools in infection prevention to guide rapid, evidence-based actions in managing measles cases. These tools are essential for reducing contamination and improving sepsis care in healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its reliance on descriptive statistics and logistic regression, which may not fully capture the complexity of risk factors. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on a single cohort and the tool's performance was evaluated using a limited set of metrics.

372 清白:加强 MRSA 非殖民化协议

372 Clean Slate: Enhancing MRSA Decolonization Protocols
作者： McAlinn J, Barton M, Montgomery A, Bruker R, Nava G, Young H, Beitscher A
摘要中文翻译结果:在演练期间,已记录发热和皮疹,且警报适时触发。背景:2025年在美国开展了一次名为“麻疹 暴发”的演练,医疗机构对可能接触或感染该疾病的患者保持高度警惕。一旦发现感染病例,接触者追踪调查将极为广泛且需投入大量人力。识别、隔离、通报(III)已成为特殊病原体准备工作的标准工具。本机构为报告发热和皮疹的患者设置了电子病历(EMR)警报。本研究的目的是比较演练与临床实践中的经验。丹佛健康医院是一家急性护理安全网医院,设有成人和儿科急诊科,共用同一安检入口,并在患者分诊进入独立候诊室前进行登记和到达确认。2025年3月,丹佛健康在儿科急诊科与急诊护理中心(PEDUC)开展演练,为一名模拟患者(表现为发热、皮疹和咳嗽)提供护理。演练观察员收集了戴口罩和隔离所需的时间。2025年4月,一名有近期国际旅行史的婴儿在PEDUC就诊,主诉咳嗽、发热和皮疹,随后经PCR检测确诊为麻疹。隔离时间通过EMR中的时间戳收集。模拟患者在入院6分钟内戴上口罩,9分钟内进入负压隔离室。而在临床实践中,皮疹被记录,但患者在分诊时无发热,EMR警报未触发。该患者在候诊室停留62分钟后才进入隔离室。接触者追踪涉及两名...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在接触者追踪调查需要大量人力和资源的医疗保健环境中,在 麻疹 麻疹 期间实施识别、隔离、通知 (III) 框架的实际挑战。
设计与对象
该研究将 2025 年 3 月在丹佛健康中心进行的模拟演习与 2025 年 4 月的真实临床病例进行了比较。丹佛健康中心是一家急症护理安全网医院,设有共用的成人和儿科急诊室以及共同的安全检查站。
核心方法
演练使用观察员测量了掩蔽和隔离的时间,而临床病例则通过电子病历 (EMR) 时间戳记录了隔离的时间。针对报告发烧和皮疹的患者实施了电子病历警报。
主要结果
在演练过程中,模拟患者在六分钟内被戴上口罩,并在进入负压室后九分钟内被隔离。在临床实践中,患者在隔离前在候诊室呆了 62 分钟,尽管在分诊时记录了皮疹和非发热状态,但 EMR 警报并未触发。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,尽管演练成功,但现实世界的实施揭示了在早期识别和隔离 麻疹 病例方面的结构和技术差距,凸显了持续改进准备工作的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了完善基于 EMR 的警报和设施工作流程的重要性,以确保及时识别和隔离 麻疹 病例,从而降低院内 传播 的风险和接触者调查的规模。
局限与证据强度
证据受到单中心设计、缺乏随机性以及对观察数据的依赖的限制,这限制了普遍性和因果推理;证据强度被评估为中等。

372 Clean Slate: Enhancing MRSA Decolonization Protocols

Authors: McAlinn J, Barton M, Montgomery A, Bruker R, Nava G, Young H, Beitscher A
Original abstract (English)
Results: During the drill, fever and rash were documented, and the alert fired appropriately. AbstractBackground: During a measles outbreak in the United States in 2025, health care settings were on high alert for patients who may have been exposed to or infected with this disease. When an infection is found, contact tracing investigations are extensive and require significant labor. Identify, Isolate, Inform (III) has become a standard tool for special pathogen preparedness. Our facility implemented an electronic medical record (EMR) alert for patients reporting fever and rash. The objective of this work is to compare and contrast our experience in drill vs clinical practice. Denver Health is an acute-care safety net hospital with both adult and pediatric emergency departments, sharing a common security checkpoint for entrance, and a check in and arrival space before patients are triaged into individual waiting rooms. In March 2025, Denver Health conducted a drill to test the III process in the pediatric emergency department and urgent care center (PEDUC) where care was provided for a mock patient with fever, rash, and cough. Time to masking and time to isolation were collected by a drill observer. In April 2025, an infant with recent international travel presented to the PEDUC with cough, fever, and rash and was subsequently diagnosed with measles by PCR. Time to isolation was collected via time stamps in the EMR. The mock patient was masked within six minutes of entry and roomed in a negative pressure room within nine minutes of entry. During clinical practice, rash was documented, but the patient was afebrile at triage, and the EMR alert did not fire. The patient spent 62 minutes in the waiting room before being roomed. Contact tracing involved individuals in both...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the practical challenges of implementing the Identify, Isolate, Inform (III) framework during a measles outbreak in a healthcare setting where contact tracing investigations are labor-intensive and resource-demanding.
Design and population
The study compared a simulated drill conducted in March 2025 with a real clinical case in April 2025 at Denver Health, an acute-care safety net hospital with shared adult and pediatric emergency departments and a common security checkpoint.
Core methods
The drill measured time to masking and isolation using an observer, while the clinical case recorded time to isolation via electronic medical record (EMR) timestamps. An EMR alert was implemented for patients reporting fever and rash.
Main results
During the drill, the mock patient was masked within six minutes and isolated in a negative pressure room within nine minutes of entry. In clinical practice, the patient spent 62 minutes in the waiting room before isolation, and the EMR alert did not fire despite documented rash and afebrile status at triage.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that despite a successful drill, real-world implementation revealed structural and technological gaps in early identification and isolation of measles cases, highlighting the need for continuous improvement in preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of refining EMR-based alerts and facility workflows to ensure timely identification and isolation of measles cases, thereby reducing the risk of nosocomial transmission and the scale of contact investigations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the single-center design, lack of randomization, and reliance on observational data, which constrains generalizability and causal inference; the strength of evidence is assessed as moderate.

337 尽量减少过度使用的喜悦 (JOMO):针对糖尿病足感染的抗生素使用的抗菌药物管理干预的结果

337 Joy of Minimizing Overuse (JOMO): Outcomes of Antimicrobial Stewardship Interventions on Antibiotic Use for Diabetic Foot Infections
作者： Vilar-Compte D, P豥z-Campos I, Guti豲ez C, Ramirez-Hinojosa J
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究是必要的,因为 2025 年美洲的 麻疹 病例激增,由于未接种疫苗或疫苗接种不足的人群中 麻疹 率较低,导致加拿大、墨西哥和美国丧失 麻疹 的无状态。
设计与对象
在墨西哥卫生部附属的六家国家转诊三级护理医院进行了一项回顾性研究,调查 50 岁以下医护人员 (HCW) 中 疫苗接种 后流行性腮腺炎病例。
核心方法
进行了描述性分析,并计算了印度血清研究所 MMR 疫苗 每 1000 剂 疫苗 相关腮腺炎的 发病率 率。使用的 MMR 疫苗 包含列宁格勒-萨格勒布 (L-Z) 菌株。
主要结果
Between 08/21/2025 and 11/30/2025, 10,590 HCWs received the MMR vaccine; 27 (2.5 per 1000 doses) developed parotid swelling (7 bilateral, 20 unilateral), with fever in 7 (25.9%), lymphadenopathy in 10 (37%), pharyngitis in 11 (40.7%), arthralgias in 8 (29.6%), and headache in 11 (40.7%). The median time from vaccination to mumps was 16.8 days, and the average duration of symptoms was 8.2 days.
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,尽管与 Jeryl-Lynn 菌株相比,Leningrad-Zagreb 菌株的相关风险更高,但印度血清研究所 MMR 疫苗 的 疫苗接种 后流行性腮腺炎很少见,并且与其他报告一致。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在全球 麻疹 病例不断增加的情况下,加强不同 MMR 麻疹 的有效性和安全性对于支持增加 麻疹 麻疹 疫苗接种不足人群的覆盖率至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、缺乏对照组以及以医护人员为重点而受到限制,可能无法推广到其他人群;由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Results: Between 08/21/2025 and 11/30/2025, 10,590 HCWs received the Serum Institute of India MMR vaccine; 27 (2.5 X MMR 1000 vaccines) developed parotid swelling (7 bilateral, 20 unilateral), with concomitant fever in 7 (25.9%); lymphadenopathy, 10 (37%); pharyngitis, 11 (40.7%); arthralgias, 8 (29.6%); and headache, 11 (40.7%). 337 Joy of Minimizing Overuse (JOMO): Outcomes of Antimicrobial Stewardship Interventions on Antibiotic Use for Diabetic Foot Infections Background: Measles cases increased significantly in the Americas in 2025; the region lost its measles-free status due to the return of endemic transmission in Canada, Mexico, and the U.S., primarily driven by low vaccination rates among unvaccinated or under-vaccinated populations. In August 2025, Mexico City experienced a surge in measles cases, prompting an urgent call to boost immunization coverage among targeted groups through mass vaccination of children, adolescents, healthcare workers (HCWs), and adults under 50. We report post-vaccination mumps cases among HCWs from six hospitals. We included all individuals under 50 years who, 10-30 days after vaccination, developed salivary gland swelling with or without malaise, headache, lymphadenopathy, or fever. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed because measles cases surged in the Americas in 2025, leading to the loss of measles-free status in Canada, Mexico, and the U.S. due to low vaccination rates among unvaccinated or under-vaccinated populations.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted at six national referral tertiary care hospitals affiliated with the Mexican Ministry of Health to investigate post-vaccination mumps cases among healthcare workers (HCWs) under 50 years of age.
Core methods
A descriptive analysis was performed, and the incidence rate of vaccine-related mumps per 1000 doses of the Serum Institute of India MMR vaccine was calculated. The MMR vaccine used contained the Leningrad-Zagreb (L-Z) strain.
Main results
Between 08/21/2025 and 11/30/2025, 10,590 HCWs received the MMR vaccine; 27 (2.5 per 1000 doses) developed parotid swelling (7 bilateral, 20 unilateral), with fever in 7 (25.9%), lymphadenopathy in 10 (37%), pharyngitis in 11 (40.7%), arthralgias in 8 (29.6%), and headache in 11 (40.7%). The median time from vaccination to mumps was 16.8 days, and the average duration of symptoms was 8.2 days.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing that post-vaccination mumps with the Serum Institute of India MMR vaccine is rare and consistent with other reports, despite the higher risk associated with the Leningrad-Zagreb strain compared to the Jeryl-Lynn strain.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that reinforcing the efficacy and safety of different MMR vaccines is crucial to support increased measles vaccination coverage in under-vaccinated populations during rising global measles cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, lack of a control group, and focus on HCWs, which may not generalize to other populations; the evidence strength is moderate due to these constraints.

326 医生对不确定艰难梭菌测试结果的方法

作者： Mazade M, Aguire Y
摘要中文翻译方法:我们选择了五个 麻疹 筛选问题,采用可调整的评分系统,其中总分为 5 分,触发阳性登记筛选、快速隔离和进一步的临床医生评估,以确定实际测试需求。结果:第一次迭代筛选于 2025 年 3 月 31 日在全网范围内启动(表 1)。结论:基于暴露和症状的 麻疹 筛查可以避免 URI 低危发热新生儿的阳性筛查。摘要背景:库克儿童医院在德克萨斯州北部拥有两家医院,诊所网络延伸至西部,该医院于 2025 年 1 月 20 日开始治疗总计 762 例的 麻疹 麻疹。麻疹 的症状包括麻疹样皮疹发作前的发烧、咳嗽、鼻炎和结膜炎。建议麻疹及时隔离。隔离应从皮疹出现前四天持续到皮疹出现后四天。我们假设接触、旅行和基于症状的筛查可以防止 麻疹 在我们的医疗机构中放大,并避免对大多数发热 URI 新生儿进行阳性筛查。从管理角度来说,筛查并不受欢迎,因为筛查会导致登记时间更长、人力需求更多以及患者满意度得分更低。我们通过反复试验对实践患者进行评分,以确定一个方案,然后在所有登记点部署筛查。我们根据当地麻疹 流行病学的演变调整了问题并重新部署了问题。我们监控吞吐量。计分包括:前往/居住在 麻疹 区域的 1 分;案件曝光2分;无 麻疹 疫苗接种 得 1 分;发烧扣2分;全部 3 C 项得 1 分——咳嗽、充血和红眼。没想到家长会对某些问题回答“未知”,这一点算数...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决医疗机构中管理不确定的艰难梭菌测试结果的挑战,特别是在皮疹发作前预防 麻疹 传播。
设计与对象
该研究在德克萨斯州北部的库克儿童医院和诊所进行,包括自 2025 年 1 月 20 日开始的全网络 麻疹 麻疹 共 762 例病例。研究人群包括接受 麻疹 筛查的发热性上呼吸道感染 (URI) 患者。
核心方法
研究人员使用包含五个问题的可调整评分系统开发了 麻疹 筛选方案。他们基于本地 流行病学 迭代完善了评分系统,并将其重新部署到各个注册点。他们使用 麻疹 PCR 作为评估筛选性能的黄金标准。
主要结果
初次筛查的阳性率为 0.32%(80,071 次筛查中的 256 次),但没有发现 PCR 阳性病例。调整后,第二次迭代的阳性率下降至0.01%,第三次迭代的阳性率上升至0.03%。在第四次迭代中,阳性率再次为 0.01%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,暴露和基于症状的筛查可以有效避免 URI 低危发热新生儿的阳性筛查,并建议该模型可以应用于其他领域,以预防 麻疹 传播。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,实施有针对性的筛查方法有助于在医疗机构中预防 麻疹 传播,特别是在皮疹发作前管理病例并减少不必要的检测。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它依赖 PCR 作为黄金标准,并涉及对筛选方案的迭代调整。结果可能无法完全推广到其他人群或环境。

326 Physician Approach to Indeterminate Clostridioides difficile Test Results

Authors: Mazade M, Aguire Y
Original abstract (English)
Methods: We selected five measles screening questions employing an adjustable scoring system in which a total score of 5 triggered a positive registration screen, rapid isolation, and further clinician assessment to determine actual testing needs. Results: Screening for the first iteration started network-wide on March 31, 2025 (Table 1). Conclusion: Exposure and symptom-based measles screening can avoid positive screens among low-risk febrile neonates with URI. AbstractBackground: Cook Children's has two hospitals in North Texas and a network of clinics extending to the West, where a measles outbreak totalling 762 cases began on January 20, 2025. Measles symptoms include fever, cough, coryza, and conjunctivitis before the onset of the morbilliform rash. Timely isolation is recommended for measles. Isolation should continue from four days before to four days after the onset of rash. We hypothesized that contact, travel, and symptom-based screening could prevent amplification of measles in our healthcare settings and avoid positive screens in most neonates with febrile URI. Administratively, screening is unpopular because screening leads to longer registration times, more manpower requirements, and lower patient satisfaction scores. We scored practice patients by trial and error to settle upon a scheme, then deployed screening at all registration points. We adjusted questions based on evolving local measles epidemiology and redeployed the questions. We monitored throughput. Scoring included: 1 point for travel to/living in a measles area; 2 points for case exposure; 1 point for no measles vaccination; 2 points for fever; 1 point for ALL 3 C's – cough, congestion, and red eyes. Not anticipating that parents would answer "UNKNOWN" to some questions, a point counted for th...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of managing indeterminate Clostridioides difficile test results in healthcare settings, particularly in preventing measles transmission before rash onset.
Design and population
The study was conducted at Cook Children's hospitals and clinics in North Texas, including a network-wide measles outbreak with 762 cases starting on January 20, 2025. The population included patients with febrile upper respiratory infections (URI) who were screened for measles.
Core methods
The researchers developed a measles screening protocol using an adjustable scoring system with five questions. They iteratively refined the scoring system based on local epidemiology and redeployed it across registration points. They used measles PCR as the gold standard for evaluating screening performance.
Main results
The initial screening iteration had a positivity rate of 0.32% (256 of 80,071 screens), but no PCR-positive cases were identified. After adjustments, the positivity rate dropped to 0.01% in the second iteration and increased to 0.03% in the third iteration. In the fourth iteration, the positivity rate was again 0.01%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that exposure and symptom-based screening could effectively avoid positive screens among low-risk febrile neonates with URI, and they suggested that this model could be applied in other areas to prevent measles transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that implementing a targeted screening approach can help prevent measles transmission in healthcare settings, particularly in managing cases before rash onset and reducing unnecessary testing.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate, as it relied on PCR as the gold standard and involved iterative adjustments to the screening protocol. The results may not be fully generalizable to other populations or settings.

270 社区获得性肺炎住院患者 3 天持续时间:68 家医院调查结果

270 3-day Duration for Hospitalized Patients with Community-acquired Pneumonia: Results of a 68 Hospital Survey
作者： Lamour J, Stoute A, Albuquerque K, Policar M, Iavicoli L
摘要中文翻译270 社区获得性肺炎住院患者的 3 天持续时间:68 家医院调查背景的结果。本报告介绍了与两项 麻疹 暴露后调查 (MPEI) 相关的实施、结果、促进因素和挑战。我们承认单中心研究的局限性和回顾性研究设计的固有偏差。麻疹 的临床鉴定提示暴露后预防 (PEP) 和 监测。2025 年,在拥有 545 个床位的纽约市公立健康+医院进行了两次 MPEI。麻疹 案例评估利用了识别-隔离-通知 (I3) 工具,MPEI 采用了识别-通知-启动 PEP-隔离流程 (I4)。麻疹 暴露被定义为指示病例两小时内急诊室 (ED) 中的人员。非免疫暴露的 PEP 为 72 小时内 MMR 麻疹(免疫功能正常)、六天内 麻疹 静脉注射免疫球蛋白 (IVIG)(怀孕、免疫功能低下),或通知未接受 PEP 的非免疫人员自我隔离 21 至 28 天 监测。曝光窗口是在对 ED 索引案件条目进行安全磁带审查后确定的。Epic(威斯康星州维罗纳)促进了曝光召回;每个图表都上传了“麻疹 排除”横幅。疑似病例隔离后,通知内部和外部利益相关者。召回是使用根据最初反应和确定的陪同人员量身定制的标准化脚本进行的。MMR 疫苗接种 状态已在 Epic 和 NYC 疫苗 注册表中进行筛选。计算 MPEI 的描述性分析。病例 1 于 2025 年春季提出,16 分钟内就被安置在空气传播感染隔离室 (AIIR) 中。案例 2 于 2025 年夏季提出,并在 10 分钟内完成 AIIR 安置。每个案例都有 麻疹 血清学...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了医疗机构中 麻疹 标准化暴露后管理的需求,其中临床识别触发暴露后预防 (PEP) 和 监测 以预防继发性 传播。其目的是评估在大型城市医院系统中进行的两项 麻疹 暴露后调查 (MPEI) 的实施、结果、促进因素和挑战。
设计与对象
2025 年,在纽约市一家拥有 545 个床位的公立健康+医院设施中进行了两次 麻疹 暴露后调查 (MPEI)。春季 MPEI 确定了 70 名患者、11 名陪同人员和 34 名工作人员,而夏季 MPEI 确定了 22 名患者、3 名陪同人员和 6 名工作人员,总共 145 名成人和 1 名儿童。
核心方法
麻疹 案例评估使用了识别-隔离-通知 (I3) 工具,MPEI 采用了识别-通知-启动 PEP-隔离 (I4) 流程。暴露窗口是通过对急诊室 (ED) 索引病例条目的安全磁带审查来定义的,Epic 通过“麻疹 排除”横幅促进暴露召回。MMR 疫苗接种 状态已在 Epic 和 NYC 疫苗 注册表中进行筛选。
主要结果
每例病例均进行了麻疹检测及鼻咽拭子PCR检测;纽约市公共卫生实验室在病例1和病例2分别于就诊后28小时和11小时确认了麻疹感染。在146例暴露者中,59例(40%)为麻疹免疫,30例接种了MMR疫苗,3例非免疫孕妇及1例免疫功能低下的宿主患者接受了麻疹静脉免疫球蛋白治疗,53人被通知进行21–28天的自我隔离。未发现继发性麻疹病例。
解释与创新
作者将继发病例的缺失解释为快速识别、明确的暴露窗口和结构化 PEP 方案有效预防 传播 的证据。他们强调了现有的准备计划、Epic 整合和双向公共卫生沟通等促进因素,同时指出了召回期间患者流程管理方面的挑战。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用标准化 I3/I4 框架和电子健康记录工具来简化医院环境中的 麻疹 暴发 响应。他们强调了快速病例确认、有针对性的 PEP 部署以及协调 监测 以减轻院内 传播 风险的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认单中心回顾性设计的局限性和固有偏差,限制了普遍性。尽管存在这些限制,但通过针对具体目标的方法、明确的暴露定义和记录的结果加强了证据,保证了适度的置信度评估。

270 3-day Duration for Hospitalized Patients with Community-acquired Pneumonia: Results of a 68 Hospital Survey

Authors: Lamour J, Stoute A, Albuquerque K, Policar M, Iavicoli L
Original abstract (English)
270 3-day Duration for Hospitalized Patients with Community-acquired Pneumonia: Results of a 68 Hospital Survey Background. This report informs on implementation, results, facilitators and challenges associated with two measles post-exposure investigations (MPEI). We acknowledge limitations of a single-site study and inherent biases of retrospective study design. Clinical identification of measles prompts post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) and surveillance. Two MPEI were conducted in 2025 at a 545-bed public NYC Health+Hospitals. Measles case evaluations utilized the Identify-Isolate-Inform (I3) tool and the MPEI employed Identify-Inform-Initiate PEP-Isolate process (I4). Measles exposure was defined as a person in the Emergency Department (ED) within two hours of the index case. PEP for non-immune exposures was MMR vaccination within 72 hours (immunocompetent), measles intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIG) within six days (pregnant, immunocompromised), or notification to self-isolate for a 21- to 28-day surveillance period for non-immune persons who did not receive PEP. The exposure window was defined after security tape reviews of ED index-case entries. Epic (Verona, WI) facilitated exposure recalls; a ‘Measles rule out’ banner was uploaded to each chart. Internal and external stakeholders were informed after suspected case isolation. Recalls were conducted using standardized scripts tailored to initial responses and identified accompanying persons. MMR vaccination status was screened for in Epic and NYC vaccine registry. Descriptive analyses were calculated for MPEI. Case 1 presented in Spring 2025 with airborne infection isolation room (AIIR) placement within 16 minutes. Case 2 presented in Summer 2025 with AIIR placement within 10 minutes. Each case had measles serology...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need for standardized post-exposure management of measles in healthcare settings, where clinical identification triggers post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) and surveillance to prevent secondary transmission. It aimed to evaluate the implementation, outcomes, facilitators, and challenges of two measles post-exposure investigations (MPEI) conducted in a large urban hospital system.
Design and population
Two measles post-exposure investigations (MPEI) were conducted in 2025 at a 545-bed public NYC Health+Hospitals facility. The Spring MPEI identified 70 patients, 11 accompanied persons, and 34 staff, while the Summer MPEI identified 22 patients, three accompanied persons, and six staff, totaling 145 adults and one child.
Core methods
Measles case evaluations used the Identify-Isolate-Inform (I3) tool, and MPEI employed the Identify-Inform-Initiate PEP-Isolate (I4) process. Exposure windows were defined via security tape reviews of Emergency Department (ED) index-case entries, and Epic facilitated exposure recalls with a 'Measles rule out' banner. MMR vaccination status was screened in Epic and the NYC vaccine registry.
Main results
Each case had measles serology and nasopharyngeal PCR obtained; the NYC Public Health Laboratory confirmed measles for Case 1 and Case 2 at 28- and 11-hours post-presentation, respectively. Fifty-nine of 146 (40%) exposures were measles-immune, 30 received MMR, 3 non-immune pregnant women and one immunocompromised host received measles IVIG, and 53 persons were notified to self-isolate for 21–28 days. No secondary measles cases were identified.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the absence of secondary cases as evidence that rapid identification, defined exposure windows, and structured PEP protocols effectively prevented transmission. They highlighted facilitators such as pre-existing preparedness plans, Epic integration, and bidirectional public health communication, while noting challenges in patient flow management during recalls.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of standardized I3/I4 frameworks and electronic health record tools to streamline measles outbreak response in hospital settings. They underscore the importance of rapid case confirmation, targeted PEP deployment, and coordinated surveillance to mitigate nosocomial transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations of a single-site retrospective design and inherent biases, which constrain generalizability. Despite these constraints, the evidence is strengthened by target-specific methods, clear exposure definitions, and documented outcomes, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

35 种需要住院治疗的非新冠社区获得性肺炎的病原体 – 分析随时间的变化

35 Etiologic Agents of Non-COVID Community-Acquired Pneumonia Requiring Hospitalization – Analyzing Changes Over Time
作者： Barth J, Siple L, Olson A, Rasmusson J, Faller R, Meyer M, Baxter B, Loberg E, Apenhorst D, Higbe L
摘要中文翻译单个病例可能会导致医疗机构内发生大量暴露,需要立即采取行动以防止继发病例。方法:IPAC 利用电子病历来识别可能暴露的患者。结果:在五次接触中,指示患者暴露了 539 名其他患者。结论:及时、协调的多学科努力使我们能够快速响应第一例 麻疹 病例并控制继发性 传播。35 种需要住院治疗的非新冠社区获得性肺炎的病原体 – 分析随时间的变化 背景:麻疹 是一种高度传播的病毒。2025 年 10 月,感染预防和控制 (IPAC) 接到通知,我们机构收治了一名患者,该患者近期有国际旅行经历且症状符合 麻疹。该患者未接种疫苗,在怀疑并随后确诊 麻疹 之前曾去过五次医疗机构。目标:描述我们机构对接触 麻疹 的个体的有效识别、优先排序和预防。患者的年龄和免疫状态被快速评估,然后优先考虑并分配到初级保健团队。这些团队及时联系患者并协调 PEP 管理。由于通知延迟,我们的团队只有不到四天的时间来为那些暴露在 PEP 窗口内的人实施 PEP。PEP 给药在指示病例检测呈阳性后数小时内开始,患者在三天内接受 PEP(表 1)。我们的设施内没有发生继发性 麻疹 病例。尽管指示病例导致大量暴露,但已暴露且符合 PEP 条件的患者很快就被优先考虑,以预防继发性 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在单一未接种疫苗病例多次接触后,在医疗机构中预防继发性 麻疹 传播 的公共卫生需求。它询问机构如何快速识别、确定优先级并为接触者提供接触后预防 (PEP),以避免院内感染 暴发。
设计与对象
这是一项于 2025 年 10 月进行的单机构 麻疹 调查和反应研究。指标患者未接种疫苗,并在怀疑和确诊 麻疹 之前进行了五次医疗就诊。感染预防和控制 (IPAC) 团队评估了暴露患者的年龄和免疫状态,并将他们分配给初级保健团队进行 PEP 协调。
核心方法
IPAC 使用电子病历来识别可能暴露的患者,并根据年龄和免疫状态对他们进行优先排序。初级保健团队联系患者并在建议的窗口内协调 PEP 管理。
主要结果
指示患者在五次接触中暴露了其他 539 名患者。PEP 给药在检测呈阳性后数小时内开始,患者在三天内接受 PEP。设施内没有发生继发性 麻疹 病例。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明及时、协调的多学科反应可以在医疗机构中快速控制 麻疹 传播。他们强调了优先考虑符合条件的患者进行 PEP 以预防继发病例的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持医疗机构中快速识别、优先排序和 PEP 管理对于预防 麻疹 暴发 的重要性。它们为管理未接种疫苗人群中 麻疹 暴露的感染控制实践提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单一设施设计和缺乏对照组而受到限制,从而限制了普遍性。由于缺乏比较数据以及暴露确定中潜在的无法测量的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

35 Etiologic Agents of Non-COVID Community-Acquired Pneumonia Requiring Hospitalization – Analyzing Changes Over Time

Authors: Barth J, Siple L, Olson A, Rasmusson J, Faller R, Meyer M, Baxter B, Loberg E, Apenhorst D, Higbe L
Original abstract (English)
A single case can result in significant exposures within a healthcare facility and prompt action is needed to prevent secondary cases. Methods: IPAC leveraged the electronic medical record to identify potentially exposed patients. Results: Across five encounters, the index patient exposed 539 other patients. Conclusion: Timely and coordinated multidisciplinary effort enabled us to rapidly respond to our first measles case and control secondary transmission. 35 Etiologic Agents of Non-COVID Community-Acquired Pneumonia Requiring Hospitalization – Analyzing Changes Over Time Background: Measles is a highly transmissible virus. In October 2025, Infection Prevention and Control (IPAC) was notified of a patient being admitted to our facility with recent international travel and symptoms consistent with measles. The patient was unvaccinated and had five healthcare facility visits prior to measles being suspected and subsequently confirmed. Objective: To describe our facility’s effective identification, prioritization, and prophylaxis of individuals exposed to measles. Patients were quickly assessed for age and immune status, then prioritized and assigned to primary care teams. These teams promptly contacted patients and coordinated PEP administration. Due to delayed notification, our teams had less than four days to administer PEP for those exposed and still within PEP window. PEP administration began within hours of the index case’s positive test and patients received PEP within three days (Table 1). No secondary measles cases occurred within our facility. Although the index case resulted in a large exposure, patients that were exposed and eligible for PEP were quickly prioritized to prevent secondary transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need to prevent secondary measles transmission in healthcare settings after a single unvaccinated case with multiple exposures. It asked how a facility could rapidly identify, prioritize, and provide post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) to exposed individuals to avert nosocomial outbreaks.
Design and population
This was a single-facility outbreak investigation and response study conducted in October 2025. The index patient was unvaccinated and had five healthcare visits before measles was suspected and confirmed. Infection Prevention and Control (IPAC) teams assessed exposed patients for age and immune status and assigned them to primary care teams for PEP coordination.
Core methods
IPAC used the electronic medical record to identify potentially exposed patients and prioritized them based on age and immune status. Primary care teams contacted patients and coordinated PEP administration within the recommended window.
Main results
The index patient exposed 539 other patients across five encounters. PEP administration began within hours of the positive test and patients received PEP within three days. No secondary measles cases occurred within the facility.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that a timely and coordinated multidisciplinary response can rapidly control measles transmission in healthcare settings. They highlighted the effectiveness of prioritizing eligible patients for PEP to prevent secondary cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the importance of rapid identification, prioritization, and PEP administration in healthcare facilities to prevent measles outbreaks. They inform infection control practices for managing measles exposures in unvaccinated populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-facility design and lack of a control group, which constrains generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the absence of comparative data and potential unmeasured confounding in exposure ascertainment.

338 达巴万星:退伍军人事务部 (VA) 提供者的国家看法和经验

338 Dalbavancin: National Perceptions and Experiences Among Veterans Affairs (VA) Providers
作者： Siple L, Barth J, Faller R, Olson A, Rasmusson J, Meyer M, Baxter B, Loberg E, Higbe L, Apenhorst D
摘要中文翻译方法:借鉴之前发表的工作 (doi:10.1017/ice.2025.49),感染预防和控制 (IPAC) 团队组建了一个多学科团队,并制定了包含以下领域的 麻疹 准备计划:指导和教育;及早发现和测试;隔离和患者移动;接触者追踪和暴露管理(表 1)。结果:第一例导致539名暴露患者。结论:积极的规划使我们能够快速响应第一个病例并控制第二个病例 传播。338 达巴万星:退伍军人事务部 (VA) 提供者的全国看法和经验 背景:先进的规划和适应性响应策略对于预防医疗机构中的 麻疹 传播 至关重要。目标:描述 麻疹 管理计划以及针对一系列 麻疹 案例的后续修改。2025 年 10 月,一名患者在第五次就诊期间入院,并被诊断为 麻疹。两名家庭成员随后因 麻疹 住院。对第一个病例的延迟识别促使我们加强了准备框架(表 1)。第二次结果有 87 次曝光,第三次结果没有曝光。尽管存在广泛的暴露,但二次 麻疹 的报告为零。尽管进行了广泛的计划,但第一例病例的延迟识别导致了重大暴露。随着感染群的展开,我们完善了计划,尽管有超过 850 例暴露,但最后一名患者的暴露为零,继发病例也为零。这些努力可能会指导其他医疗机构改善其 麻疹 的准备和响应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在医疗机构中预防 麻疹 传播 的先进规划和适应性响应策略的需求,其动机是 麻疹 的重新出现以及退伍军人事务部 (VA) 系统等高暴露环境中 麻疹 的风险。
设计与对象
感染预防和控制 (IPAC) 团队针对 VA 医疗系统内的 麻疹 病例群制定并实施了 麻疹 管理计划,并随着病例群的展开而进行了修改。
核心方法
IPAC 团队采取了多学科方法,并制定了 麻疹 准备计划,包括指导和教育、早期检测和测试、隔离和患者移动以及接触者追踪和暴露管理。
主要结果
第一个案例导致 539 名患者暴露,第二个案例导致 87 名患者暴露,第三个案例没有暴露;尽管接触广泛,但继发 麻疹 病例的报告为零。
解释与创新
主动规划能够快速响应首例病例并控制继发性 传播,而首例病例的延迟识别导致重大暴露,凸显了及时检测和迭代计划细化的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能会指导其他医疗机构改善 麻疹 的准备和响应,强调结构化框架和实时适应对于减轻 传播 风险的价值。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏暴露个体的详细人口统计或临床特征,并且缺乏量化特定干预措施影响的统计分析,证据有限,需要对零继发病例进行谨慎解释。

338 Dalbavancin: National Perceptions and Experiences Among Veterans Affairs (VA) Providers

Authors: Siple L, Barth J, Faller R, Olson A, Rasmusson J, Meyer M, Baxter B, Loberg E, Higbe L, Apenhorst D
Original abstract (English)
Methods: Drawing on previously published work (doi:10.1017/ice.2025.49), the Infection Prevention and Control (IPAC) team initiated a multi-disciplinary team and developed a measles preparedness plan with the following domains: Guidance and education; early detection and testing; isolation and patient movement; contact tracing and exposure management (Table 1). Results: The first case resulted in 539 exposed patients. Conclusion: Proactive planning enabled rapid response to our first case and controlled secondary transmission. 338 Dalbavancin: National Perceptions and Experiences Among Veterans Affairs (VA) Providers Background: Advanced planning and adaptable response strategies are essential for preventing measles transmission in healthcare facilities. Objective: To describe a measles management plan and subsequent modifications in response to a cluster of measles cases. In October 2025, a patient was admitted during their fifth healthcare encounter and was diagnosed with measles. Two family members were later hospitalized with measles. Delayed recognition of the first case prompted enhancements to our preparedness framework (Table 1). The second resulted in 87 exposures, and the third resulted in no exposure. Despite the extensive exposure, zero secondary measles were reported. Despite extensive planning, delayed identification of the first case caused significant exposures. As the cluster unfolded, we refined our plan, resulting in zero exposures from the last patient and zero secondary cases, despite over 850 exposures. These efforts may guide other healthcare facilities in improving their measles preparedness and response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need for advanced planning and adaptable response strategies to prevent measles transmission in healthcare facilities, motivated by the re-emergence of measles and the risk of outbreaks in high-exposure settings such as the Veterans Affairs (VA) system.
Design and population
A measles management plan was developed and implemented by an Infection Prevention and Control (IPAC) team in response to a cluster of measles cases within the VA healthcare system, with modifications made as the cluster unfolded.
Core methods
The IPAC team initiated a multi-disciplinary approach and created a measles preparedness plan encompassing guidance and education, early detection and testing, isolation and patient movement, and contact tracing and exposure management.
Main results
The first case resulted in 539 exposed patients, the second in 87 exposures, and the third in no exposures; despite extensive exposure, zero secondary measles cases were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
Proactive planning enabled rapid response to the first case and controlled secondary transmission, while delayed identification of the first case caused significant exposures, highlighting the importance of timely detection and iterative plan refinement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may guide other healthcare facilities in improving measles preparedness and response, emphasizing the value of structured frameworks and real-time adaptations to mitigate transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the absence of detailed demographic or clinical characteristics of exposed individuals and the lack of statistical analysis to quantify the impact of specific interventions, warranting cautious interpretation of the zero secondary cases.

rMV-SLAM诱导的免疫应答盲溶瘤疗法对癌症治疗发挥协同作用

Immune responses elicited by rMV-SLAMblind oncolytic therapy exert a synergistic effect on cancer therapy
作者： Kanako Moritoh, Tomoko Fujiyuki, Yosuke Amagai, Hiroki Sato, Misako Yoneda, Chieko Kai
摘要中文翻译摘要 溶瘤病毒疗法已成为一种有前途的癌症治疗策略。rMV-SLAMblind 是一种重组 麻疹病毒,缺乏与 SLAM 相互作用的能力,并且特异性感染和裂解 NECTIN4 + 癌细胞。我们之前报道过rMV-SLAM盲溶瘤病毒疗法(rMV-OVT)通过激活病毒治疗的肿瘤中的免疫细胞来发挥抗肿瘤免疫力。据认为,溶瘤病毒疗法通过两种机制促进抗肿瘤作用:感染引起的直接肿瘤细胞裂解和诱导免疫细胞介导的肿瘤细胞死亡。然而,rMV-OVT激活的免疫细胞是否对未治疗的肿瘤发挥抗肿瘤作用以及rMV-SLAMblind是否增强免疫检查点阻断的效果尚不清楚。这项研究表明,rMV-OVT 可抑制双侧肿瘤小鼠模型中未治疗肿瘤的生长,并增强与抗 PD1 抗体联合治疗的抗肿瘤效果。值得注意的是,在某些情况下,CD4 + T 细胞耗竭导致 rMV-OVT 具有良好的抗肿瘤作用,同时伴随着激活的 CD8 + T 细胞数量的增加。这些结果表明,rMV-OVT 通过免疫细胞介导的肿瘤细胞死亡发挥功效,并通过与免疫检查点抑制剂的联合治疗得到改善。这些发现为提高 rMV-OVT 癌症治疗功效提供了宝贵的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
溶瘤病毒疗法已显示出治疗癌症治疗的希望,但rMV-SLAMblind溶瘤疗法引发的免疫应答是否可以对未治疗的肿瘤发挥抗肿瘤作用以及这种方法是否增强免疫检查点阻断的功效仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究使用双侧肿瘤小鼠模型来评估rMV-SLAMblind溶瘤病毒疗法(rMV-OVT)及其与抗PD1抗体联合的抗肿瘤效果。
核心方法
作者评估了 rMV-OVT 给药后治疗和未治疗肿瘤的肿瘤生长抑制情况,并评估了 CD4+ T 细胞耗竭对抗肿瘤反应的影响。
主要结果
rMV-OVT 可抑制双侧肿瘤小鼠模型中未治疗肿瘤的生长,并与抗 PD1 抗体联合使用时增强抗肿瘤效果。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,rMV-OVT 通过免疫细胞介导的肿瘤细胞死亡发挥抗肿瘤作用,与免疫检查点抑制剂联合治疗可进一步改善这种作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为优化癌症 治疗 的 rMV-SLAMblind 溶瘤病毒疗法提供了见解,特别是在增强全身抗肿瘤免疫方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,由于缺乏人类数据以及免疫细胞耗竭效应的潜在混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Immune responses elicited by rMV-SLAMblind oncolytic therapy exert a synergistic effect on cancer therapy

Authors: Kanako Moritoh, Tomoko Fujiyuki, Yosuke Amagai, Hiroki Sato, Misako Yoneda, Chieko Kai
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Oncolytic virotherapy has emerged as a promising strategy for cancer therapy. rMV-SLAMblind is a recombinant measles virus that lacks the ability to interact with SLAM and specifically infects and lyses NECTIN4 + cancer cells. We previously reported that rMV-SLAMblind oncolytic virotherapy (rMV-OVT) exerts anti-tumor immunity through the activation of immune cells in virus-treated tumors. It is thought that oncolytic virotherapy promotes anti-tumor effects via two mechanisms: direct tumor cell lysis caused by infection and the induction of immune cell-mediated tumor cell death. However, it is unknown whether the immune cells activated by rMV-OVT exert anti-tumor effects on non-treated tumors and whether rMV-SLAMblind enhances the effects of immune checkpoint blockade. This study demonstrated that rMV-OVT suppressed the growth of non-treated tumors in a bilateral tumor mouse model and enhanced anti-tumor effects in combination therapy with anti-PD1 antibody. Notably, CD4 + T cell depletion led to the trends towards favorable anti-tumor effects of rMV-OVT in some cases, which was accompanied by increasing numbers of activated CD8 + T cells. These results suggest that rMV-OVT demonstrated efficacy via immune cell-mediated tumor cell death, which was improved by combination therapy with immune checkpoint inhibitors. These findings provide valuable insights into improving the efficacy of rMV-OVT for cancer therapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Oncolytic virotherapy has shown promise for cancer treatment, but it remains unknown whether immune responses elicited by rMV-SLAMblind oncolytic therapy can exert anti-tumor effects on non-treated tumors and whether this approach enhances the efficacy of immune checkpoint blockade.
Design and population
The study used a bilateral tumor mouse model to evaluate the anti-tumor effects of rMV-SLAMblind oncolytic virotherapy (rMV-OVT) and its combination with anti-PD1 antibody.
Core methods
The authors assessed tumor growth suppression in treated and non-treated tumors following rMV-OVT administration and evaluated the impact of CD4+ T cell depletion on anti-tumor responses.
Main results
rMV-OVT suppressed the growth of non-treated tumors in the bilateral tumor mouse model and enhanced anti-tumor effects when combined with anti-PD1 antibody.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that rMV-OVT exerts anti-tumor effects through immune cell-mediated tumor cell death, which is further improved by combination therapy with immune checkpoint inhibitors.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into optimizing rMV-SLAMblind oncolytic virotherapy for cancer treatment, particularly in enhancing systemic anti-tumor immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mouse model, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of human data and potential confounding from immune cell depletion effects.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

2013−2025 年美国县、区和社区级 麻疹 传播

County, district and community-level measles transmission in the United States in 2013−2025
作者： Siyu Chen, Ana Isabel Bento
摘要中文翻译美国于 2000 年宣布消除 麻疹。2025 年至 2026 年,已有超过 3,500 起案件对该状态进行了正式审查。疫苗 的犹豫,加上 COVID-19 的破坏和错误信息,已经侵蚀了维持消除的免疫缓冲。当前的 监测 加剧了这种威胁:县级 疫苗接种 聚合在结构上掩盖了 暴发 起源的精细敏感性聚类。我们构建了覆盖美国 45 个州和华盛顿特区(2013-2025 年)的全国性多尺度 疫苗接种 数据库,涵盖 50,000 多所学校、13,000 个区和 3,000 个县,并开发了 传播 模型来量化不同空间尺度的流行病风险。2022年至2023年,学校层面的有效再生数跨越了流行病阈值,这一转变对于累计监测来说是不可见的。流感大流行后,平均易感性从约 5% 翻倍至 10%。我们还表明,县边界错位创建了跨界走廊,将疫苗接种良好的县从疫苗接种较少的人群中推至 跨物种溢出 阈值以上。受豁免政策和 疫苗接种 基础设施的影响,各州的发展轨迹明显不同。美国麻疹脆弱性的空间结构已经发生了根本性的转变:传播的潜力在学校中是超临界的,但在监测监测的范围内是看不见的。防止 麻疹 再次流行需要 麻疹 以及在学校和地区层面采取干预措施,在引发持续的 传播 之前可以拦截输入病例。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述考察了2013年至2025年美国的麻疹病毒 传播,重点关注县、区和社区级别的麻疹病毒数据如何影响暴发的风险。核心问题是 麻疹 消除状态是否因 麻疹 覆盖范围和易感性模式的变化而受到威胁。
证据基础与方法
该审查编制了2013年至2025年涵盖美国45个州和华盛顿特区的全国性多尺度疫苗接种数据库,包括超过50,000所学校、13,000个区和3,000个县。它开发了一个 传播 模型来量化不同空间尺度的流行病风险,使用县级 疫苗接种 总量和学校级有效繁殖数来评估 传播 动态。
共识与结论
麻疹病毒 传播 的潜力在学校中已经变得非常关键,但在当前 监测 系统监控的规模上仍然不可见。它还强调,大流行加剧了 疫苗 的犹豫,使平均敏感性增加了一倍,从大约 5% 增加到 10%。
争议与缺口
审查指出,县级 疫苗接种 聚集了模糊的精细敏感性聚类,因此很难在其起源处检测到 暴发。它还指出,县边界错位会造成跨界走廊,使疫苗接种较少的人群能够影响疫苗接种良好的县。
科研与实践启示
为了防止 麻疹 再次流行,审查建议在学校和学区层面采取 麻疹 和干预措施,可以在持续的 传播 发生之前拦截输入病例。它强调需要制定本地化策略来解决 麻疹 漏洞不断变化的空间结构。

County, district and community-level measles transmission in the United States in 2013−2025

Authors: Siyu Chen, Ana Isabel Bento
Original abstract (English)
The United States declared measles eliminated in 2000. In 2025-2026, more than 3,500 cases have placed that status under formal review. Vaccine hesitancy, accelerated by COVID-19 disruption and misinformation, has eroded the immunological buffers sustaining elimination. Current surveillance compounds this threat: county-level vaccination aggregates structurally obscure the fine-scale susceptibility clustering where outbreaks originate. We assembled a nationwide multiscale vaccination database spanning 45 US states and Washington, DC (2013-2025), encompassing over 50,000 schools, 13,000 districts and 3,000 counties, and developed a transmission model to quantify epidemic risk across different spatial scales. School-level effective reproduction numbers crossed the epidemic threshold in 2022-2023, a transition invisible to aggregated surveillance. Average susceptibility doubled from approximately 5% to 10% following the pandemic. We also show that county boundary misalignment creates cross-boundary corridors that push well-vaccinated counties above threshold through spillover from less-vaccinated populations. State trajectories diverge markedly, shaped by exemption policies and vaccination infrastructure. The spatial architecture of US measles vulnerability has fundamentally shifted: transmission potential is supercritical in schools but invisible at the scale monitored by surveillance. Preventing measles re-endemicity requires surveillance and interventions operating at school and district levels, where imported cases can be intercepted before igniting sustained transmission.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines measles virus transmission in the United States from 2013 to 2025, focusing on how county, district, and community-level vaccination data influence the risk of outbreaks. The central question is whether the measles elimination status is under threat due to changes in vaccination coverage and susceptibility patterns.
Evidence base and method
The review compiles a nationwide multiscale vaccination database spanning 45 US states and Washington, DC from 2013 to 2025, including over 50,000 schools, 13,000 districts, and 3,000 counties. It develops a transmission model to quantify epidemic risk across different spatial scales, using county-level vaccination aggregates and school-level effective reproduction numbers to assess transmission dynamics.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that measles virus transmission potential has become supercritical in schools but remains invisible at the scale monitored by current surveillance systems. It also highlights that vaccine hesitancy, exacerbated by the pandemic, has doubled average susceptibility from approximately 5% to 10%.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that county-level vaccination aggregates obscure fine-scale susceptibility clustering, making it difficult to detect outbreaks at their origin. It also identifies that county boundary misalignment creates cross-boundary corridors, allowing less-vaccinated populations to influence well-vaccinated counties.
Research and practice implications
To prevent measles re-endemicity, the review recommends surveillance and interventions at the school and district levels, where imported cases can be intercepted before sustained transmission occurs. It emphasizes the need for localized strategies to address the shifting spatial architecture of measles vulnerability.

麻疹 前线防御:急诊护士和急救医疗服务人员的关键作用

Measles Frontline Defense: The Pivotal Role of Emergency Nurses and Emergency Medical Services Personnel
作者： Garrett D. Hall, Diana J. Felton, Nathan R. Tan, Sarah K. Kemble
摘要中文翻译简介:麻疹 于 2000 年在美国宣布消除。然而,2025 年,该国报告的病例数达到了 20 多年来的最高水平。此外,疾病控制与预防中心 2026 年初的数据表明病例数持续增加。急诊护士和急诊医疗技术人员经常作为最初的医疗保健专业人员来评估发烧和皮疹患者。及时识别和迅速隔离对于遏制 麻疹 传播 至关重要。这篇叙述性文献综述旨在为急诊护士和急诊医疗技术人员在 麻疹 暴发 期间的识别、隔离、记录、感染控制以及与公共卫生当局的协调方面提供实用的、基于证据的指导。方法:进行了综合当前疾病控制与预防中心和世界卫生组织指南的叙述性回顾。通过使用 麻疹、急诊科、院前、急诊护士、紧急医疗服务、分诊和感染控制等关键词在 PubMed 和 Google Scholar 中进行有针对性的搜索来确定同行评审文献,重点关注 2015 年至 2026 年的出版物。优先考虑提供与紧急医疗环境相关的实用指导或流行病学数据的研究。结果:麻疹 通过空气中的 传播 传播,并在感染者离开某个区域后可在空气中悬浮长达 2 小时。早期症状包括发烧、咳嗽、鼻炎(流鼻涕和鼻塞)和结膜炎,随后出现下降的斑丘疹(皮肤上有平坦区域和小肿块的红色皮疹),通常从面部开始并向下蔓延。适当的分诊评估、感染控制措施、电子健康记录中的记录以及与感染预防和公共卫生当局的及时沟通对于中断 传播 至关重要。讨论:麻疹 仍然是医疗保健环境中最具传染性的病毒性疾病之一。急诊护士和急救医疗技术人员往往是最先识别并阻止 麻疹 传播的人。应采用明确的分类和筛查步骤,应确认工作人员的免疫力,并与感染预防和公共卫生团队密切合作,以保护患者、家庭和医护人员。

麻疹 通过空气中的 传播 传播,感染者离开某个区域后,可以在空气中悬浮长达 2 小时。

Measles Frontline Defense: The Pivotal Role of Emergency Nurses and Emergency Medical Services Personnel

Authors: Garrett D. Hall, Diana J. Felton, Nathan R. Tan, Sarah K. Kemble
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Measles was declared eliminated in the United States in 2000. However, in 2025, the country experienced the highest number of cases reported in more than 2 decades. Furthermore, early 2026 data from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention indicate a continued increase in case counts. Emergency nurses and emergency medical technicians frequently serve as the initial health care professionals who assess patients with fever and rashes. Prompt identification and swift isolation are essential to curtail measles transmission. This narrative literature review aims to provide practical, evidence-based guidance for emergency nurses and emergency medical technicians on the recognition, isolation, documentation, infection control, and coordination with public health authorities during measles outbreaks. METHODS: A narrative review synthesizing current Centers for Disease Control and Prevention and World Health Organization guidance was conducted. Peer-reviewed literature was identified through targeted searches in PubMed and Google Scholar using keywords, including measles, emergency department, prehospital, emergency nurse, emergency medical services, triage, and infection control, focusing on publications from 2015 to 2026. Studies providing practical guidance or epidemiologic data relevant to emergency medical settings were prioritized. RESULTS: Measles spreads through airborne transmission and can remain suspended in the air for up to 2 hours after an infected individual leaves an area. Early symptoms include fever, cough, coryza (runny nose and nasal congestion), and conjunctivitis, followed by a descending maculopapular rash (red rash with both flat areas and small bumps on the skin), which typically begins on the face and spreads downward. Proper triage assessment, infection-control measures, documentation within electronic health records, and timely communication with infection prevention and public health authorities are essential to interrupt the transmission. DISCUSSION: Measles remains one of the most contagious viral illnesses encountered in health care settings. Emergency nurses and emergency medical technicians are often the first to recognize and halt the spread of measles. Clear triage and screening steps should be used, staff immunity should be confirmed, and close collaboration with infection prevention and public health teams is necessary to protect patients, families, and health care workers.
View review five-element analysis

Emergency nurses and emergency medical technicians frequently serve as the initial health care professionals who assess patients with fever and rashes.
Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
Measles spreads through airborne transmission and can remain suspended in the air for up to 2 hours after an infected individual leaves an area.
Controversies and gaps
Measles remains one of the most contagious viral illnesses encountered in health care settings.
Research and practice implications
Clear triage and screening steps should be used, staff immunity should be confirmed, and close collaboration with infection prevention and public health teams is necessary to protect patients, families, and health care workers.

Lessons from Ontario’s COVID-19 Vaccination Strategies: Implications for Preventing Measles Resurgence in Canada

Authors: Stella Triboi, Fatih Sekercioglu, Fatih Şekercioğlu
Original abstract (English)
The resurgence of measles in Canada has raised concerns about poor vaccination coverage and disruptions to routine immunization programs following the COVID-19 pandemic. In 2025, Canada lost its measles elimination status due to sustained measles transmission across multiple provinces. The reported cases primarily involved populations that were unvaccinated or under-vaccinated. This paper reviews successful vaccination strategies implemented during COVID-19 and assesses their applicability to measles prevention efforts. The return of measles cases in Canada after the COVID-19 outbreak demonstrates some of the difficulties in sustaining immunization rates in the general population. Even though Canada eliminated measles from its territory in the past, some challenges remain today.

量化 麻疹 暴发 在美国的成本以及成本如何随 暴发 规模变化

Quantifying the cost of measles outbreak in the U.S. and how costs scale with outbreak size
作者： Salin Sriudomporn, Bryan Patenaude
摘要中文翻译2025 年,美国经历了自 2000 年消除 麻疹 以来最多的病例。这些 麻疹 需要采取广泛的公共卫生应对措施,以减轻、消耗大量医院资源,并对州和联邦预算造成经济和财政负担。我们使用 PubMed 以及 CDC 和州卫生部门的官方报告进行了文献综述,以确定 2000 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 10 月 7 日期间在所有 50 个州发表的研究。我们还研究了成本如何随 暴发 规模变化。总共筛选了 120 篇文章,其中 33 篇进行了全文审查,并从 19 篇文章中提取了数据,得出了 18 个不同州的 暴发 相关成本。灰色文献中还发现了八份报告。暴发 大小范围为 1 至 802 箱。每个案例的平均 暴发 成本估计为 43,203.65 美元,而每个联系人的平均成本为 443.46 美元。每个病例的平均成本从医疗提供者角度的 33,415.75 美元到包括公共卫生应对成本时的 58,591.50 美元不等。模仿公共卫生应对措施的固定成本估计为 297,746.94 美元(95% CI:4537.86 美元 - 590,956.02 美元),每增加一个病例,增量成本为 16,299.64 美元(95% CI:15,301.96 美元 - 17,297.32 美元)。相对于 暴发 规模的总成本弹性估计为 0.86(95% CI:0.65-1.07) 麻疹 暴发 在美国继续重新出现,并造成重大的财务和公共卫生负担。这些 暴发 在很大程度上是可以通过廉价且高效的 麻疹 疫苗接种 与系统级准备措施相结合来预防的。由于任何 麻疹 的固定响应成本都可能很高,因此我们的估算通过隔离与事件新案例以及事件联系人相关的增量成本,提供了有关 麻疹 成本如何随 暴发 规模扩展的改进数据,以便与预算规划和建模风险评估一起使用。

我们使用 PubMed 以及 CDC 和州卫生部门的官方报告进行了文献综述,以确定 2000 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 10 月 7 日期间在所有 50 个州发表的研究。总共筛选了 120 篇文章,其中 33 篇进行了全文审查,并从 19 篇文章中提取了数据,得出了 18 个不同州的 暴发 相关成本。我们还研究了成本如何随 暴发 规模变化。

争议与缺口
每个病例的平均成本从医疗提供者角度的 33,415.75 美元到包括公共卫生应对成本时的 58,591.50 美元不等。模仿公共卫生应对措施的固定成本估计为 297,746.94 美元(95% CI:4537.86 美元 - 590,956.02 美元),每增加一个病例,增量成本为 16,299.64 美元(95% CI:15,301.96 美元 - 17,297.32 美元)。
